Chapter 1001: 1000: Lu Yang’s Day of Suffering

"Ah?" Master Duanchen was confused, "Benefactor Lu, is your way of accumulating good deeds by bringing a group of people back with you?"

"This one practices the Joyful Zen."

"This one wants to commit suicide."

"This one knows the Hypnosis Technique..."

Lu Yang introduced each person’s background one by one, each worthy of mention. Just capturing them was no easy task, not something that could be summed up in a sentence or two.

When he got to the last one, Lu Yang gravely said, "Luckily, Old Meng and I felt that something significant would happen at the festival and were restless. We kept an eye on unusual activities in the city, and look, we found them all."

Listening, Master Duanchen broke out in a cold sweat. These people were all significant threats. If Benefactor Lu and Benefactor Meng had missed even one person, this Buddha Bathing Festival would have been doomed.

But it wasn’t his fault for the lack of preparation; these troublesome cultivators all acted covertly, and even increasing patrols wouldn’t have uncovered them.

"I wonder how you two benefactors managed to find these people, could you possibly teach me a technique or two?"

Lu Yang waved his hand modestly, "There’s no special technique, it’s just the protection of the Worldly Venerable."

Master Duanchen was moved by these words; indeed, that was the only possibility. Otherwise, even if they both had been using Spiritual Sense non-stop, it was impossible to find everyone.

"It seems both benefactors are individuals of profound fortune."

"Master, you are weary from dealing with these people, and I estimate the top eight are about to be chosen; it’s time for Old Meng and me to go to the scene," Lu Yang calculated the time and said.

Initially, after they captured the person releasing the zombie poison, they had quite a lot of time to play, but who could have expected there was a whole bunch of people waiting to be caught? After capturing them all and sending them back to Kaihuang Temple, the time was almost up.

"Indeed."

The moment Lu Yang stepped out of the temple gate, his vision darkened, and when he regained his sight, he was already near the arena.

"Old Lu, want to see how they are fighting?" Meng Jingzhou pointed to the bustling arena, quite interested.

Lu Yang’s eye twitched. No, during the festival, why had time reversed again? Could he not enjoy the festival properly?

"Don’t bother looking; their level is quite ordinary. The best among them are probably the cultivators ranked ninety-fifth and ninety-ninth on the earthly ranking," Lu Yang had already watched it once, knowing watching here would purely be a waste of time.

"What is an earthly ranking?"

Lu Yang held his forehead, having forgotten that the present Old Meng didn’t know what the earthly ranking meant: "It’s the ranking that Western Paradise Temple does for the cultivators of Buddha Country, divided into human, earthly, and heavenly rankings..."

"I see," Meng Jingzhou realized, not pondering too much, as this kind of information could be known with more research.

"But aren’t you curious about what level this earthly ranking is, Old Lu?" Meng Jingzhou was still very curious and chose to stay and watch.

On the platform, Huo Ying looked gravely at Fang Wudi, "Fang Wudi, I didn’t expect you to come too. I heard you are a Venerate innate physique, born with a Golden Dragon coiling into your body and becoming your birthmark..."

Meng Jingzhou watched excitedly for a while but soon grew disappointed: "Is this their capability? I could have beaten them both when I was at the Golden Core Stage."

"I told you, there’s nothing worth watching," Lu Yang said helplessly.

"Then how did you know that there would be earthly ranking cultivators appearing?" Meng Jingzhou was puzzled, as not even Kaihuang Temple knew who was participating in the competition; it was all registered on the spot.

"Of course I knew; I’ve lived through it all once."

"Lived through it all once, what do you mean?" Meng Jingzhou was particularly surprised.

Seeing Old Meng’s perplexed expression, Lu Yang took this opportunity to explain, as he hadn’t had the chance to explain about the Time Retrospection earlier.

"This dates back to when I first broke through to the Mid-stage of Transforming Spirit..."

Lu Yang explained everything from the beginning, recounting his experiences of the last half-year, leaving Meng Jingzhou baffled.

He had heard from the old ancestors that some Semi-Immortals could pause time, but this was the first time he’d heard of Time Retrospection.

Seeing Old Lu talk until he was parched didn’t seem like he was making it up.

"So it turns out, I didn’t expect Dao Fruit could even do something like this..." Meng Jingzhou nodded in sudden realization, then suddenly became perplexed.

"Hey, Old Lu, what were you saying earlier?"

Lu Yang: "..."

Darn, I forgot that the methods of Dao Fruit have forgetful properties, explained for half a day for nothing.

"Oh, it’s Master Lu, Master Meng." A familiar voice rang out, and Lu Yang knew without turning around that it was Tang Qiaoqiao and Cui Que’er.

"Masters, didn’t you say you were going to be judges on stage, why are you still here?"

"It’s not our turn yet." Lu Yang casually explained, not asking about Tang Chuanwu’s whereabouts like last time.

"Why is it just the two of you, where is that kid Chuanwu?" Meng Jingzhou asked on behalf of Lu Yang.

"Brother has gone with the boss to Lotus City next door to get supplies."

"Then, Miss Cui, be careful when you distribute porridge." Lu Yang advised.

Cui Que’er was surprised: "Master Lu, how did you know I was going to distribute porridge with dad?"

"Miss Cui, you forgot, you mentioned that your family goes to distribute porridge on the 5th, 15th, and 25th of every month, and today is May 15th."

"Can’t we distribute porridge one day later?" To Meng Jingzhou, today was supposed to be a day for great fun.

Lu Yang shook his head: "Of course not, doing good deeds today earns ten times the merit of ordinary days, it’s even more necessary to distribute porridge."

Cui Que’er became even more surprised: "Master Lu knows so much, I thought only us locals knew about this belief."

After splitting up from Cui Que’er and Tang Qiaoqiao, Meng Jingzhou encouraged Lu Yang: "Old Lu, shall we also go and earn some merit?"

"That’s easy." When it came to doing good deeds today, no one understood better than Lu Yang.

Lu Yang led Meng Jingzhou down the bustling streets when suddenly a man in a black cloak turned sharply, laughed maniacally, and confounding Meng Jingzhou.

"The chaos of Kaihuang City, it starts with you..."

"If the lines are the same, don’t bother saying it." Lu Yang stepped forward briskly, his large hand covered the face of the man in the black cloak, and with a clang, slammed him to the ground.

"Old Lu, he is..."

Lu Yang squatted down, took out the Soul Locking Rope, and tied up the man in the black cloak expertly, this time without even needing to perform a Soul Search.

"Don’t ask so much, I’m taking you to do good deeds."

"Ah? Oh."

Then the lascivious monk, the unobstructed cultivator, the insect cultivator...each was captured in turn, thanks to previous capture experience, this time Lu Yang was even more adept.

"Job well done, let’s deliver them all to Duan Chen..."

Before he could finish speaking, Lu Yang’s vision darkened, and he found himself near the arena again.

"Old Lu, do you want to see how they are fighting?" Meng Jingzhou pointed at the arena and said.

"Don’t bother looking, at best there will be two cultivators from the earth list."

"What’s the earth list?"

Lu Yang walked forward two steps and expertly patted the most vocal spectator on the outer edge.

"Come here, explain to him what the earth list is."

Chapter 1002: 1001: Falling into a Trap

After explaining the meaning of the Earth Ranking, Lu Yang pulled Old Meng to accumulate merit before Tang Qiaoqiao and Cui Que’er arrived, truly racing against time to squeeze out moments to enjoy.

Lu Yang also didn’t dare to neglect this bunch of lascivious monks and insect cultivators, uncertain whether this reincarnation would be the last.

If luck wasn’t on his side and it turned out to be the final round of reincarnation, failing to account for any single person could cause a major uproar in Kaihuang City.

Lu Yang had suffered from similar slip-ups before.

Once, he got caught in a reincarnation loop, having the same conversation with Tao Yaoye fifteen extra times, and later, Lu Yang felt the conversation so dull that he handled it carelessly.

As fate would have it, that turned out to be the last reincarnation, which led Tao Yaoye to believe she did something to displease Senior Brother Lu, almost bringing her to tears. Lu Yang had to backtrack and gently explain everything to Tao Yaoye, eventually clearing up the misunderstanding.

"The chaos in Kaihuang City..."

"Beauty, just give in to me..."

"My life won’t last much longer..."

"From now on your sensitivity..."

"Master, everyone is here, you can just perform a Soul Search."

"Why do I feel like you’re rushing through everything?" Meng Jingzhou couldn’t keep up with Lu Yang’s pace, as if Lu Yang already knew what was going to happen.

"Don’t mention it, I’m just exhausted," Lu Yang sighed, praying silently there wouldn’t be a sixth reincarnation.

Arriving at a time he had never experienced, Lu Yang finally relaxed, stopped by a sugar painting stall, and bought two sugar paintings, one in his image and one in Meng Jingzhou’s likeness.

He handed the one of Meng Jingzhou’s likeness to Meng Jingzhou, "Here, it’s on me."

Meng Jingzhou took the sugar painting with mixed feelings, looking it over from left to right, hesitating to take a bite.

There wasn’t anything wrong with the sugar painting; the only issue was that it was too lifelike.

Lu Yang also hesitated to take a bite, but he felt it was a shame not to buy a sugar painting resembling himself when he finally stumbled upon one.

In the Spiritual Space, whether it was because they had finally reached unknown time or because she saw Lu Yang buying a sugar painting of himself, Immortal Fairy suddenly sat up from the Lotus Platform, showing interest.

"Little Yang, come on, let this Immortal use your body."

Without allowing Lu Yang to resist, he was directly possessed by the ancient major demon.

Using Lu Yang’s body, the ancient major demon licked the sugar painting and uttered a melodious, rising and falling hum.

"So sweet."

Then she returned the body to Lu Yang.

Seeing this, Lu Yang, skeptical, also licked the sugar painting, finding it tasted just like any regular sugar painting. So why did the Fairy comment, "So sweet"?

Having licked it once, he no longer felt any psychological burden, and Lu Yang continued to eat the sugar painting.

Seeing Lu Yang eating unreservedly, Meng Jingzhou had no reason to shrink back, and he too started to eat.

The two walked through the bustling streets, munching on sugar paintings, while the chatter of passersby flowed into their ears.

"Lv Cheng, let’s get a divorce. You’re already forty and still haven’t achieved anything. If I had known this before, I would never have married you."

"Son, you’re already six years old, stop spending my money, it’s time to become independent, let’s sever our father-son relationship."

"You old thing, ever since falling ill, you have cost me so much silver, from today on, I don’t care whether you live or die, get out of this house."

Lu Yang halted in his tracks, he had been hearing many such conversations in succession, divorces, fathers abandoning their children, daughters throwing away their mothers, occurring with a frightening frequency.

"Wait a minute, something doesn’t feel right."

Lu Yang and his companion realized that they didn’t know when the laughter-filled streets had suddenly lost all laughter, leaving only chaotic footsteps and indifferent conversations.

They had been joking and laughing together, only to notice the abnormalities in their surroundings!

On the entire street, only their laughter could be heard, it was they who were out of place!

"What’s going on!"

"It’s the Oblivion Pill!"

Lu Yang suddenly realized, this was clearly the effect of the Oblivion Pill, once people lost their emotions, they would be driven by self-interest, for fathers, young sons were a burden, for children, elderly mothers were a burden, all could be discarded!

Everyone on this street had taken the Oblivion Pill!

No, that’s not right, it wasn’t just this street.

Under the scan of Spiritual Sense, privacy is hard to come by, but now Lu Yang couldn’t care about privacy anymore, his Spiritual Sense spread out, covering dozens of streets and houses, no matter whether they were ordinary people or cultivators, all had expressionless faces, walking like the living dead!

"Looking at this, I’m afraid the whole Kaihuang City has fallen into this state!" Lu Yang broke out in a cold sweat, feeling that something terrible was happening.

"Could it be the work of the Ruthless Sect? How did they manage to affect everyone!" Meng Jingzhou’s expression was serious, they were under everyone’s eyes, yet neither of them had noticed this before.

As if remembering something, Lu Yang looked down at the ground. The ground was wet with standing water, some seeping into the crevices of the bluestone streets, the surface and soil both damp, the clues forming a line.

"It’s the canal water!"

"Do you remember how Master Duanchen described the Oblivion Pill to us after he performed a Soul Search on the Three-eyed Ghost Monk?"

Reminded by Lu Yang, Meng Jingzhou also realized, "The Oblivion Pill, it dissolves upon contact with water! It’s a problem with the water!"

"Right, someone must have dumped a large quantity of Oblivion Pills into the canal water, today is the Buddha Bathing Festival, the day of the Worldly Venerable’s birth, and to celebrate his birth, water is splashed in celebration!"

"The canal water is sprinkled all over Kaihuang City, the moisture evaporating, carrying the components of the Oblivion Pill with it into the air, people inhale it without even realizing!"

"Perhaps demon beasts are even more sensitive to the components of the Oblivion Pill. Remember the Insect Cultivator we came across when we were accumulating virtue by doing good? The Insect King possessed his host, almost causing a massive insect calamity, it might all be because the Insect King was influenced by the Oblivion Pill, losing its emotional bonds!"

"Our cultivation levels are too high, even if we took an Oblivion Pill, it would only last a few breaths, and the Oblivion Pill in the air must be diluted countless times, its effect greatly reduced, so we naturally become immune to it."

"But other cultivators can’t do that!"

"Let’s find Master Duanchen, he is also at the Divinity Transformation Stage, although not as strong as us two, he should not be affected!"

The two made a quick decision, disregarding the rule against flying within the city, they took to the air and flew towards Kaihuang Temple.

They landed at the entrance of Kaihuang Temple, where the temple was still bustling with visitors, and the number of pilgrims coming to burn incense and pray to the Buddha had even increased due to the effects of the Oblivion Pill.

The pilgrims had expressionless faces, walking into Kaihuang Temple like marionettes, dropping copper coins, silver, and Spirit Stones into the donation box with extremely standardized movements.

If there had been any who had been carelessly going with the flow before, now in the state of oblivion, even the emotion to do things half-heartedly was gone.

The two pushed open the Abbot’s Room, and in a rush, they called out, "Master, it’s terrible, the whole city has been poisoned by the Forgetfulness Poison!"

Chapter 1003: 102

In the Abbot’s Room, those previously seized by Lu Yang had all been detained here, and Master Duan Chen was questioning each of them about the circumstances to make judgments on how to deal with these individuals.

When he heard the news from the pair, Lu Yang, he abruptly stood up, his eyes wide as saucers.

"What!"

Why was this Buddha Bathing Festival filled with so many incidents?

Worldly Venerable bless, fortunately, he had the foresight to invite Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou, otherwise the chaos during this festival might have been even greater.

"Hurry, tell me what is the situation now?!" Master Duan Chen couldn’t bother questioning the lascivious monk any longer, since those locked up here couldn’t escape anyway.

"Master, you would know if you extended your Spiritual Sense outside."

Master Duan Chen released his Spiritual Sense; although it did not cover as large an area as that of the other two, it was sufficient to see the abnormalities among the civilians distinctly.

All of them were expressionless and showed symptoms exactly consistent with those who had taken the Oblivion Pill, as remembered by the Three-eyed Ghost Monk.

"Based on our hypothesis, it’s highly possible that there’s an issue with the canal water. The people from the Ruthless Sect have dumped a large quantity of Oblivion Pills into the canal,"

Lu Yang announced his guess, hearing which Master Duan Chen’s complexion grew increasingly grim.

He had been negligent; not realizing an issue could arise with the canal water would indeed be a silent yet effective method to spread the Forgetfulness Poison throughout Kaihuang City.

Master Duan Chen realized the severity of the situation.

"What do the two benefactors think we should do?"

"The immediate precedence is to stabilize the civilians. On our way here, we already witnessed many ruthless actions, abandoning wives and children, and committing all manners of abandonment. If we wait any longer, I fear a massive chaos will ensue!"

"Old Meng and I discussed on the way here; the Forgetfulness Poison spread in the air is not nearly as potent as a whole Oblivion Pill.

It’s uncertain for those in the Foundation Establishment Stage, but for cultivators in the Golden Core and Nascent Soul Stages, if they hold their breath and stop inhaling more of the Forgetfulness Poison, the toxicity in their bodies should pass quickly, returning them to normal."

The limit for cultivators in this Buddha Bathing Festival was up to the Golden Core Stage hence why there were many more Golden Core cultivators in Kaihuang City now than usual, which could be a great help.

"Old Meng and I will try to stabilize the civilians and have these cultivators hold their breath."

"As for the Master, temporarily close Kaihuang Temple, and don’t let pilgrims enter anymore; lead the monks in maintaining the city’s order."

"Good, this impoverished monk will proceed to do so. But how will the two benefactors manage to stabilize the civilians while getting the cultivators to hold their breath?" Master Duan Chen wondered. With the city’s cultivators being so insensitive, the appeal from Lu Yang and his companion was greatly reduced, would they obediently comply?

"This part is simple."

As Lu Yang spoke, he took out a bodhi seed, drilled it into the soil, applied the Tree Planting Spell to grow a bodhi clone, and then pulled out a black robe with a gold skull printed on it from the Identity Jade and draped it over the bodhi clone.

...

"Old man, you’re just my mom, don’t think I will do whatever you say; I don’t need you to make decisions for my future."

"I’m doing this all for your own good."

"Do you think I would harm myself?"

"You’re ungrateful; I wish I didn’t have a son like you."

Similar conversations could be seen everywhere.

Affection and interest are two crucial factors that maintain social relationships. When only interest remains with no affection, the existing social structures nearly collapse.

Kaihuang City was on the verge of collapse.

Suddenly, a muffled groan sounded in the distance, resembling a fist slamming into the ground; the ground shook, kicking up dust.

There were also buzzing sounds of swords breaking the sound barrier, thunderous like booming thunder.

"Stop, thief!"

Three figures flew out of the dust, the sounds of their fight growing closer, more intense, and finally arriving above Kaihuang City, their presence terrifying.

People could finally make out the figures of the three shadows.

"It’s Sword Immortal Lu and Buddhist Meng."

"Who is the other person?"

"Clad in a black cloak, hard to see."

Meng Jingzhou unleashed the Arhat Fist, the fist imprint dazzling, even more blinding than the sun.

At the same time, he quietly tore apart a dozen talismans, and a grand Buddha’s chant filled the air, as if hundreds of thousands were praising and chanting scriptures for Meng Jingzhou.

Behind Lu Yang’s head emerged a halo, formed by numerous golden Sword Qi, divine like an immortal.

"The two indeed have some skills," a cold laugh came from beneath the black cloak, from Bodhi’s clone.

"Who exactly are you!" Lu Yang wouldn’t fall for this flattery and scolded the clone angrily.

"The Master of Skull Divine Sect, White Bone Lang." Bodhi’s clone released an immense oppressive force, as if heaven and earth were crumbling, covering the entire Kaihuang City, from Nascent Soul Cultivators to commoners, everyone prostrated to the ground.

The commoners did not know what Cultivation Level White Bone Lang possessed, but the Nascent Soul Cultivators did.

This definitely was a Great Power of Unification!

"Sounds like a cult."

Bodhi’s clone became furiously enraged, "Dare to insult the Divine Sect, then let this city be the sacrifice! Senluo Sword Formation!"

A Sword Array was laid down with great commotion but negligible power, nailed in the void. Above Kaihuang City appeared an Array Method resembling a pagoda, shrinking from bottom to top, much like the Senluo hell.

"Dare to slaughter the city! Heaven-Slaying Immortal Fist!" Meng Jingzhou shouted loudly.

"Extinguish Immortal Sword!" Lu Yang raised the Green Peak Sword high, its Sword Qi stretching a hundred meters, easily capable of cleaving an enormous ship on the East Sea.

The two deployed every skill they had, fighting against Lu Yang’s Bodhi clone.

All the people of Kaihuang City looked up at the sky battlefield, worried about any aftermath of White Bone Lang’s attack falling into the city and hitting them.

Meanwhile, monks from Kaihuang Temple, under the direction of Master Duanchen, divided Kaihuang City to try to reduce interactions and movements among the commoners.

After hundreds of rounds of fierce battles, Bodhi’s clone finally began to show signs of fatigue, unable to continue and revealed a fierce expression.

"Very well, very well, worthy of the legendary Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou, truly living up to your reputations!"

"Want to protect the commoners in this city, do you? Let’s see how you protect!"

"The Divine Sect will live on forever!"

Bodhi’s clone’s body quickly swelled, the change occurring so abruptly that Lu Yang and the other could not intervene in time, only to hear a loud explosion, the clone self-detonated, black smoke billowing, drifting in the air.

Lu Yang’s expression changed drastically, "It’s bad, it’s the legendary Bone Poison born from mountains of corpses and seas of blood, inhaling even a breath could cause your organs to disintegrate, not even immortals can save you!"

They tried to drive away the dense smoke, but inadvertently spread it everywhere.

Upon hearing this, the commoners hurriedly hid in their rooms while cultivators all held their breath, without exception.

Lu Yang and the other exchanged a glance, it was settled.

As they had anticipated, as long as Golden Core and Nascent Soul Cultivators stopped inhaling Forgetfulness Poison, they would quickly breakdown the poison and return to normal.

They also became aware of their own previous abnormal state and the oddity within the city.

Just when Lu Yang and the other thought they had somewhat managed to control the situation, two figures ascended into the sky, coldly watching Lu Yang and his companion.

"Worthy disciples of Daoist Buyu, indeed you possess some skills."

Although they did not recognize these two, Lu Yang easily guessed their identities, "People from Ruthless Sect, was it you who released the Forgetfulness Poison?"

Chapter 1004: 103

Seeing these two people appear, Master Duanchen’s heart skipped a beat.

One of them had appeared in the memories of the Three-eyed Ghost Monk, and was indeed his superior, an Elder of the Ruthless Sect named Bai Liting, with a Cultivation Level of the Body Integration Realm.

Since the other one was on par with Bai Liting, it meant that he also had a Cultivation Level of the Body Integration Realm.

Two Body Integration Realm Cultivators plotting against his Kaihuang City!

Could Beneficiary Lu and Beneficiary Meng handle them, and if not, what should be done?

"May the Worldly Venerable protect us," Master Duanchen silently recited sutras, praying for the safety of both men and of Kaihuang City.

"Love Poison, huh? This name isn’t exactly wrong," another Elder of the Ruthless Sect said, his name was Wu Shui.

"Why have you released the Forgetfulness Poison!" Lu Yang shouted angrily, completely baffled as to what the people of the Ruthless Sect were trying to do.

"What do we want to do? Naturally, we want to make the Oblivion Pill." Wu Shui, confident of victory, didn’t mind revealing part of the truth to the two.

They couldn’t handle the aggressively approaching Meng Family Member, so how could they handle two geniuses who had made names for themselves at a young age?

"Humans are the most intelligent of all creatures. If there’s anything that’s a natural Pill Furnace, it’s surely the human body," Wu Shui said.

A ludicrous thought arose in Lu Yang’s mind—could it be that this Oblivion Pill was refined using humans as containers?

No wonder Immortal Fairy hadn’t detected the method of refinement; it was something she had never encountered before.

"You Meng Family Members have uprooted our arrangements in Buddha Country one by one, making it impossible for us to stay there. Would it not show weakness on the part of our Ruthless Sect to leave without doing something?" Wu Shui said.

"Finding traces of our sect in Kaihuang City was just a coincidence, and you all were in the city. Wouldn’t it be a pity if we didn’t do something?" he continued.

Lu Yang’s eyes narrowed slightly, "Oh, so you mean to say, not only do you intend to meddle with the citizens of the city, but you also intend to deal with us two?"

"Of course, we want to deal with you. The two of you are the culprits who exposed our sect’s whereabouts. It is truly unbelievable that you didn’t hide back in Great Xia but brazenly continued to stay in Buddha Country, seemingly ignorant of how death is spelled."

The two Ruthless Sect Elders, having reached the Merciless State of Mind, spoke in a flat tone without any fluctuation, making their words sound quite eerie.

"I really don’t know how the character for death is written!" Lu Yang raised his Green Peak Sword, preparing to truly fight, after having verified with Immortal Fairy that these two Elders were Mid Integration Stage cultivators.

He had previously killed an Early Integration Stage Elder of the Ruthless Sect, but that was after Man Gu and Lan Ting had worn him down, making the fight a breeze.

The two Elders of the Ruthless Sect were now at their peak condition, which made them truly difficult to handle.

But it was worth a try.

"Arrogant juniors," Elder Bai Liting said coldly. In his life, he had encountered many geniuses, but never had a Mid-stage of Transforming Spirit genius threatened him like this!

"One Sword Becomes Ten Thousand!" Lu Yang roared, gold Swordmanship Domain Embryo appearing beneath his feet, countless Green Peak Swords made of Sword Qi lined up in the air, forming a grand avenue.

Lu Yang casually drew one sword, slashing at Elder Bai Liting. The sword light shone brightly for an instant and then passed.

Bai Liting, who had not cared much about Lu Yang at first, felt his hair stand on end—the speed of the sword stroke had surprised him. He instantly took out a tortoise shell from his Storage Ring to block the sword light.

The sword light only left a faint white mark on the tortoise shell.

Since it was just a probing attack, Lu Yang wasn’t surprised that Bai Liting wasn’t hurt. However, what did surprise him was the tortoise shell Bai Liting used.

"Black Tortoise Clan’s tortoise shell?" Lu Yang snorted coldly. This Elder of the Ruthless Sect truly knew no taboos, daring to use a turtle shell from the Black Tortoise Clan as a magic artifact—was he not afraid of being discovered by the Black Tortoise Clan?

Lu Yang ceased to speak. The Swordmanship Domain Embryo vibrated, and one by one, the Green Peak Swords rose from the ground, hovering mid-air, their sharp points aiming directly at Bai Liting.

"Go!"

Swoosh, swoosh, swoosh—

Countless Green Peak Swords turned into sword lights and attacked Bai Liting. Bai Liting defended himself with the Black Tortoise turtle shell and was forced to retreat continuously. These sword lights were not only frighteningly powerful but also surprisingly forceful!

"Such fierce attacks," Bai Liting murmured, not daring to be careless. He knew Three-eyed Ghost Monk and Hollow Shadow were his subordinates and had heard that Lu Yang publicly fought the former, claiming the Ghost Monk was a Body Integration Realm King of Ghosts. He thought Lu Yang was surely one to exaggerate.

Such people are usually weaker than the rumors say.

This was clearly stronger than the rumors!

Master Duanchen was overjoyed to see Beneficiary Lu had real skills.

"But what of it, the Rules grasped at the Body Integration Realm are a chasm you’ll never cross."

"Hereby, I set this Rule: ’My body’s strength increases to the level of the Black Tortoise shell.’"

As the power of the Rule poured into Bai Liting, his entire body was rejuvenated from the inside out. Now, even without using the Black Tortoise shell, the sword lights could not hurt him. The sword strikes on his body made a tinkling sound but could not inflict damage.

"Speechless’s disciple, what will you do now?" Bai Liting stood on the Swordmanship Domain Embryo, holding a bronze spear and the Black Tortoise shell, perfectly combining offense and defense.

Close combat was originally the forte of a Sword Cultivator, as a Sword Cultivator can slash out extremely powerful Sword Qi in close combat. However, as Bai Liting’s defense increased, Lu Yang’s advantage diminished. The Sword Qi hastily condensed now was unable to injure Bai Liting.

The spear moved at tricky angles, and Lu Yang, unable to attack successfully, could only defend passively. The Black Tortoise shell was also part of the offense and very heavy, acting like a blunt weapon.

"Turtle and Snake, Unify!"

The spear and the Black Tortoise shell fused together, transforming into the body of a dead Xuan Wu and smashed down on Lu Yang heavily.

Boom—

Lu Yang was directly smashed to the ground!

Just when Bai Liting thought this move would severely injure Lu Yang, he found that Lu Yang had actually drilled into the ground on purpose, and soon after, a Bodhi Tree grew from the ground, beneath which two Lu Yangs appeared.

Previously flying in the sky, he had no chance to use the Tree Planting Spell. But now that he was intentionally knocked down, he finally found the opportunity!

The Bodhi Clone was born, the primordial spirit entered, and holding the Inheriting Shadow Sword, it was a complete and separate Lu Yang!

The two Lu Yangs soared into the sky, no need for words, their cooperation was seamless.

"One Sword Becomes Ten Thousand!" The Bodhi Clone could also display the embryonic form of the Swordsmanship Domain, laying an endless number of Inheriting Shadow Swords. The domain embryo blazed with fierce flames, accompanied by the cry of the Golden Crow.

Three Flavored True Fire, Golden Crow True Fire!

The two powerful True Fires merged with the embryonic form of the Swordsmanship Domain!

Inside the Green Peak Sword’s small world, the Seven Star Sword Set unified.

Ten Thousand Swords Returning to Origin, Extinguish Immortal Sword Array!

Lu Yang pointed his sword at Bai Liting and shouted, "Here I establish the [Rule: Kneel Down]!"

Bai Liting’s pupils shrank, and with a thud, he knelt down directly.

How is this possible, Lu Yang is only at the mid-stage of Spirit Transformation, how could he possibly use the power of rules?!

Elsewhere, Elder Wu Shui took out a flying boat built from bones, standing at the bow with the Ten Thousand Soul Banner in hand, from which wailing souls gushed out, filling the flying boat.

The souls acted like sailors, each performing their duties, turning the cannons to aim at Meng Jingzhou.

"Spirits? Ridiculous!"

Meng Jingzhou shouted loudly, and a golden Dharma Aspect rose slowly behind him, as if born from the primeval sun, wild and unrestrained.

The spirits were illuminated by the radiance of Meng Jingzhou’s Dharma Aspect, screamed in agony, and white smoke emerged from their bodies.

In the presence of the supreme Pure Yang Dharma Aspect, all spirits are mere illusions!

"Fear or not, did you really think my Dharma Aspect only knows how to shine?"

Meng Jingzhou leapt high into the air, clapped his hands together, and slammed down on the White Bone Flying Boat. The hundred-foot-tall Pure Yang Dharma Aspect mimicked the action, shaking the boat with a single strike.

"Old Lu, lend me some fire!"

The supreme Pure Yang Dharma Aspect reached out, taking a portion of Lu Yang’s embryonic form of the Swordsmanship Domain. The Golden Crow True Fire merged with the Dharma Aspect, becoming more and more like the real sun.

And the fists of the Dharma Aspect held sharp Sword Qi, their power also enhanced!

"Earth-Shaking Eight Styles!"

The Third Elder had only researched the Earth-Shaking Six Forms. After Meng Jingzhou returned to the Meng Family and received guidance from Meng Junzi, Meng Junzi personally revised the techniques, adding two more forms on the original basis and modifying the original techniques to better suit Meng Jingzhou’s preferences.

This set of boxing techniques was incomparable to the past!

"I want to see how sturdy your boat really is!"

"Arrogant." Elder Wu Shui spread his arms, and seas surged up behind him, transforming into a hundred-foot giant, clashing with Meng Jingzhou!

Fire and water collided, Meng Jingzhou’s eyes turned red as if he could spew flames, the Pure Yang Power of his Single Body Spiritual Root surged!

"Being distracted by this burning desire is really bothersome!"

Meng Jingzhou took out the Oblivion Pill and swallowed them all at once.

These were obtained from the Three-eyed Ghost Monk, most of which were in Meng Jingzhou’s possession!

Elder Wu Shui was stunned for a moment, almost thinking Meng Jingzhou was one of their own from the Ruthless Sect.

He quickly came back to his senses, the power of the Pure Yang Dharma Aspect was advancing, and his own Dharma Aspect was about to collapse!

Meng Jingzhou felt an unprecedentedly good condition, a clear mind state, now he could wholeheartedly engage in the battle without worrying about his body!

"Kill."

As the four were entangled in the melee, an even more tyrannical pressure enveloped Kaihuang City, interrupting their fight.

"It seems that the young ones are quite capable these days, even challenging those in the Body Integration Realm."

An ancient figure appeared, not smiling genuinely, his gaze occasionally drifting toward Lu Yang.

"Are you Ye Zijin’s Junior Brother?"

"Vice Sect Hierarch!" Bai Liting and Wu Shui stopped attacking and flew to the side of the aged figure, bowing respectfully.

The Vice Sect Hierarch of the Ruthless Sect!

"The time is about right. It’s time to take something from Buddha Country when we leave, to give both Buddha Country and Great Xia a long-lasting lesson. This Kaihuang City should go as well."

The elderly figure spoke indifferently, and Kaihuang City trembled continuously, lifting off the ground and flying into the sky.

Then, darkness fell before Lu Yang’s eyes.

"Old Lu, do you want to see how their fight is going?" Meng Jingzhou pointed at the arena and said.

Chapter 1005: 104

"Back again."

Lu Yang took a deep breath in and out, calming his mind state.

Just moments ago, he was fighting for his life, but in the blink of an eye, time had flowed backward, restoring the peaceful days. The contrast was so great that it gave Lu Yang a feeling as if he was in a different world.

At least, it was peace on the surface.

Based on the timeline, the two elders from the Ruthless Sect should have already poisoned the canal by now, and the water currently being splashed all over the streets contained the Forgetfulness Poison, though it hadn’t fully evaporated yet.

For instance, the agitation of the Insect King, an Insect Cultivator, was due to the absorption of the Forgetfulness Poison.

It was possible that some children had already been poisoned, but because children had little influence, no one had noticed.

"Fairy, are those two elders and the Vice Sect Hierarch still in Kaihuang City?"

Fairy Eternity extended her Spiritual Sense to sense and responded, "They’re not here."

Lu Yang nodded, knowing this result was within his expectations. If those two Ruthless Sect elders had been in Kaihuang City, they wouldn’t have allowed him and Old Meng to stage a play throughout the city and only reveal themselves after the show was over.

Right now, they should be elsewhere.

A knowing smile tugged at the corner of Lu Yang’s mouth.

It took a struggle to handle two elders, and when that was done, the Vice Sect Hierarch stepped in, proud of your numbers, huh?

"Old Meng, stop watching the competition for now. There are several good deeds awaiting your action right now," Lu Yang said.

"What good deeds?" Meng Jingzhou was puzzled by Lu Yang’s sudden statement.

"Go to Yellowstone Street now, on that street, there’s someone in a black cloak, laughing strangely and holding silver needles; catch him. Then turn a corner to the entrance of the civilian restaurant, there’s a fat monk in fancy clothing... and after passing through another street, you’ll meet an old man trying to self-detonate..."

Meng Jingzhou listened to Lu Yang’s detailed information, which didn’t seem deceitful.

"What about you, what will you do?"

"I’m going to call for help," Lu Yang said, before weaving through the crowd and returning to his guest room in Kaihuang Temple, collapsing into sleep.

...

Seek Dao Sect, Task Hall, Soul Lamp Chamber.

Lu Yang’s Soul Lamp flickered on and off, prompting Gu Junye to hastily call for Dai Bufan.

Dai Bufan studied the pattern of the Soul Lamp’s flickering and deciphered the message Lu Yang was sending.

"Should we ask Vast Sea Ancestral Master to open a passage from Kaihuang City back to the Sect, so Junior Brother Lu can return for a while?"

...

Across the vast desert, a few people riding camels were especially conspicuous against the sands.

"No!" Tang Chuanwu suddenly woke up, his eyes filled with panic.

"Tang boy, what’s wrong?" The middle-aged man with a beard noticed Tang Chuanwu’s distress and signaled for the caravan to halt.

"Back again." Tang Chuanwu muttered to himself, as if awakening from a dream, drenched in sweat. How many times had he experienced this Reincarnation, the sixth or the seventh?

The shock from the end of the last cycle was tremendous, and right now, his mind was a total mess; he needed to sort out the sequence of events.

They were all from a medicine shop in Kaihuang City, currently short on supplies and en route to Lotus City to procure medicinal materials.

On their way to Lotus City, they encountered an exceptionally rare pack of Sand Wolves. These Sand Wolves were cunning and extremely dangerous, with the Wolf King being a being at the Consummation of Qi Refinement level.

They had martial arts skills on their travels, and even Tang Chuanwu’s combat strength was the highest among them all.

But even so, facing the premeditated attack of the Wolf King, they couldn’t escape their fate.

Tang Chuanwu used five reincarnations to match wits and bravery against the Wolf King, yet he still couldn’t defeat the Wolf King, and the entire team was on the verge of being slaughtered by the wolves.

During the fifth reincarnation, despite his meticulous planning, the Wolf King fell into a trap he had set, only for it to breakthrough to the terrifying Foundation Establishment Stage at the very last moment, turning all his plans into a joke.

Tang Chuanwu was nearly driven insane; the Wolf King was simply not an opponent he could deal with. He had encountered cultivators as opponents before, but that was in the city where he could stir various conflicts and maneuver through them.

But this was the desert, where there was neither village ahead nor shop behind, and he couldn’t find anyone to match the Wolf King!

On the sixth reincarnation, no longer able to bear this torment, he stopped trying to save his team and rode back to Kaihuang City alone on a camel to seek help; perhaps, he could still save one or two people this way.

But when he returned to Kaihuang City, what greeted him was a city full of heartless people; he couldn’t find any help at all, and the two masters were still dealing with two elders from the Ruthless Sect.

In desperation, he returned home to see how his sister was doing.

It was already noon by then, and Cui Que’er had finished distributing porridge and had not yet left the slums. An unknown Gold Core Period Cultivator had taken a fancy to Cui Que’er’s beauty and kidnapped her in the chaos, despite his shouts and struggles being futile.

He staggered back home only to find his sister looking at him expressionlessly, as if looking at a stranger, a look that tore at his heart.

Then, the Vice Sect Hierarch of the Ruthless Sect made a move, intending to relocate the entire Kaihuang City.

Finally, unable to bear the torment any longer, he committed suicide and arrived at the seventh reincarnation.

Tang Chuanwu was filled with despair; he knew that the Wolf King was waiting in ambush, a Gold Core Period Cultivator had kidnapped Cui Que’er in Kaihuang City, and there was a Demon Path giant planning to move the entire city.

None was an opponent he could handle!

What should he do? What was he supposed to do?

"Kid Tang?" A middle-aged man with a beard muttered to himself, wondering what was wrong with the usually clever Tang Chuanwu today.

Tang Chuanwu had thought that upon his previous return to Kaihuang City, the problem could have been easily resolved, but the last reincarnation taught him that Kaihuang City was an even more dangerous place than the desert!

Even if he could go back in time, what use was it? He couldn’t save anyone; he could only save himself!

Moments of the last reincarnation kept flooding his mind—the team mauled by wolves, Cui Que’er being kidnapped, his sister’s indifferent expression, Kaihuang City flying away...

"Ah—" Overwhelmed by chaos, Tang Chuanwu screamed to the sky, completely unable to think of a solution.

Suddenly, a sentence Master Lu had told him after annihilating the Skull Divine Sect flashed in his mind.

"We will be staying at Kaihuang Temple for a while longer; if you encounter any trouble, feel free to come find me."

...

As the spatial passage closed, Lu Yang, holding the Green Peak Sword, returned to his room in Kaihuang Temple.

He put away the Green Peak Sword, left Kaihuang Temple, and prepared to meet up with Old Meng.

A shadow rode swiftly on a camel through the streets.

Tang Chuanwu gazed eagerly at Lu Yang, who stood somewhat dazed at the temple’s entrance, and felt as if a weight had been lifted. He tumbled down from the camel with a clatter.

With the experience of six reincarnations behind him, bypassing the Wolf King, he sprinted all the way, finally finding Lu Yang—his only lifeline—before the Forgetfulness Poison fully erupted.

Lu Yang, quick to react, stepped forward to catch Tang Chuanwu, who nearly fell to the ground.

With a look of despair, Tang Chuanwu said, "Master Lu, this city is in danger!"

"The Ruthless Sect’s people will come to Kaihuang City, everyone will become heartless, and I don’t know how they do it, but it must be the work of the Ruthless Sect. The first two people who appear, you can handle, but the last one that appears, neither you nor Master Meng can handle."

"You might not believe me, Master, but everything I am saying is true, it’s all true!"

The more Tang Chuanwu spoke, the more agitated he became, until his emotions collapsed. He started speaking incoherently and couldn’t help but burst into tears. He really didn’t know what to do, whether Master Lu would believe him, or if there was a solution...

"I believe you."

Chapter 1006: 1005: I’m Here (Seeking Monthly Votes Mid-Month)

"What exactly are you saying, Master?" Tang Chuanwu thought he must be hallucinating, as the words he spoke were illogical and incoherent. How could Master Lu possibly believe him?

Lu Yang looked into Tang Chuanwu’s eyes and repeated himself seriously.

"I believe."

Tang Chuanwu’s lips trembled, unsure whether he was laughing or crying: "You believe me, someone really believes me, I wasn’t lying..."

The opportunity he discovered to possess the Time Retrospection ability stemmed from an attack targeting his sister. In the second reincarnation, he excitedly held his sister back, telling her you are in danger if you go out, you will be killed, you can’t go out.

However, what he got in return was his sister’s look of astonishment.

His sister didn’t believe him and died in the second reincarnation.

After that, he tried to save others with his Time Retrospection ability, warning them of future dangers, advising them not to take certain actions. Even when the future events confirmed his words, the result was only increased distrust.

In subsequent reincarnations, he calmed down and learned to save people using the differences in information and lies.

This time, he had an emotional breakdown and had no choice but to reveal the truth to Lu Yang. He thought Master Lu, like everyone else, wouldn’t believe him at all, yet Master Lu believed him.

Finally, someone believed him.

The clue about the Time Dao Fruit user has finally been found.

"Sit down, don’t rush, take your time to talk. What exactly happened today, and how did you know about it?"

Lu Yang quietly channeled spiritual essence into Tang Chuanwu to stabilize his emotions.

He could feel Tang Chuanwu’s pulse racing, the frequency terribly high, reaching the limit of what a mortal can withstand, indicating how agitated he had been.

Or perhaps how desperate.

Of course, Lu Yang would believe him; Tang Chuanwu appearing before him at this particular time point was evidence itself.

At this time, Tang Chuanwu should have been far away in Lotus City and not supposed to be here.

His presence here could only mean one thing—he knew what the future would bring.

The incoherent words that followed from Tang Chuanwu further confirmed Lu Yang’s suspicions.

With Lu Yang’s reassurance, Tang Chuanwu gradually calmed down.

"Master Lu, a major catastrophe is about to happen in the city. Everyone will become extremely indifferent. I don’t know what’s going on, but it must be related to the Ruthless Sect," he said.

"And the Skull Divine Sect’s Master, I don’t know how, but he’s resurrected and has become the White Skeleton Champion. The White Skeleton Champion is no match for you."

"Later on, two Elders and the Vice Sect Hierarch of the Ruthless Sect showed up. That Vice Sect Hierarch is incredibly powerful; he even wanted to move Kaihuang City. Neither you nor Master Meng could match him."

"Also, Queer... a Golden Core cultivator will abduct her."

Lu Yang quietly listened to Tang Chuanwu’s description, then extended two fingers and looked into Tang Chuanwu’s eyes.

"I only have two questions. First, how did you come to know all of this?"

"I can..." Tang Chuanwu began to speak, wanted to say Time Retrospection, but then suddenly remembered that once he said it, time would rewind again.

"Is it Time Retrospection?" Lu Yang asked calmly.

The chair clattered to the ground as Tang Chuanwu abruptly stood up, looking incredulously at Master Lu.

Tang Chuanwu dared not confirm nor deny, worried that either answer might trigger the Time Retrospection.

"Immortal Fairy, what’s going on here? If Tang Chuanwu can perform Time Retrospection, why isn’t the Time Dao Fruit with him?"

Immortal Fairy Eternity had clearly checked; there was no Time Dao Fruit on Tang Chuanwu.

Inside the Spiritual Space, Immortal Fairy Eternity sat cross-legged on the Lotus Platform, idly swinging a little foot back and forth.

"Hmm~ Since he doesn’t have the Time Dao Fruit, there’s another possibility. The user of the Time Dao Fruit might have applied its power to him."

"What do you mean?"

"You remember what Immortal I told you, don’t you? If I wish, I can use the Eternity Dao Fruit to make everyone in the world immortal."

Lu Yang recalled, "You also said that the Dao Fruit itself represents a rule. Whether you apply that rule to yourself or to everyone else is entirely up to you."

Immortal Fairy Eternity was pleased that Lu Yang remembered her words: "The Time Dao Fruit works the same way. Its user can apply the ’Time Retrospection’ rule to this Tang fellow."

"So it’s like that," Lu Yang was astonished, he had only heard the Immortal talk about it casually and hadn’t given it much thought before. Now, seeing Tang Chuanwu possess such a heaven-defying ability, he realized Immortals indeed were extraordinary beings.

"It seems like my guess was correct; you do have the ability for Time Retrospection."

Seeing Tang Chuanwu still hesitant, Lu Yang nodded, "It seems you’re not unwilling to admit it, but rather you can’t say it out loud."

"Will you die, or will time rewind... Never mind, that’s not important."

"Second question, before the Ruthless Sect appeared, you went through five reincarnations. Did anything happen to you that requires my help?"

Tang Chuanwu’s eyes widened in shock. Master Lu actually retained memories from his previous reincarnations!

The reason he hadn’t mentioned the sand wolf attack was that he felt such an incident couldn’t be changed. After seeing Master Lu, all his previous thoughts were overturned.

"Yes, our team was attacked by sand wolves on the way to Lotus City, and only I survived and escaped!"

"I see." Lu Yang finally understood why he was caught in the cycle of reincarnation today.

He crossed his arms and propped up his head, "Then let me ask you, are you willing to save them?"

"I am willing!"

Lu Yang stood up and caressed the Green Peak Sword, it seemed this reincarnation had people coming over for nothing.

"Then you initiate time retrospection, and I will go rescue your team."

As Lu Yang looked on in astonishment, Tang Chuanwu pulled out the dagger from his waist and stabbed directly into his chest without any hesitation.

...

Tang Chuanwu suddenly awoke to find himself riding a camel, the conversation with Master Lu felt like a dream.

"Little Tang, what’s wrong?" The medicine shop owner noticed something odd with Tang Chuanwu and signaled for the caravan to stop.

"Owner, the sand wolves have marked us," Tang Chuanwu calmly took out a string of Buddha Beads from his chest, snapping it with two fingers as he extracted it.

"I had the fortune of encountering Master Duanchen welcoming guests. These Buddha Beads were given to me by Master Duanchen, and they will alert me when danger is near."

"What?!" Seeing Tang Chuanwu’s serious manner, the medicine shop owner did not doubt him.

Facing a pack of sand wolves would mean almost certain death for a caravan like theirs.

"Don’t panic, let’s continue moving forward and not let the sand wolves notice anything unusual," Tang Chuanwu, experienced in dealing with sand wolves, gave instructions. "We’ll hold off the pack. Someone will come to save us."

If they showed a defensive stance now, the sand wolves would notice their failed ambush and attack directly. Continuing forward might delay their assault.

"Okay, okay." The medicine shop owner was flustered and had no choice but to follow his lead.

The camel caravan slowly moved on.

"It’s the sand wolves!" Suddenly, someone with sharp eyes shouted, and the team immediately fell into chaos.

Sand wolves, with only their nostrils showing as they looked up from being buried in the sand, suddenly burst out of the desert and pounced on the caravan.

"Prepare yourselves! Don’t panic!" The medicine shop owner drew his sword to defend.

Tang Chuanwu, riding a camel and heading straight for the Wolf King, had his physique greatly enhanced by a drop of Lu Yang’s blood, which strengthened his tendons and bones. Coupled with the secret practice of Long Arm Boxing, his combat capabilities were far superior to the medicine shop owner.

Even with Tang Chuanwu holding off the Wolf King, the rest of the people quickly found themselves in a standstill.

Not that Tang Chuanwu was faring any better. He was, after all, just a mortal. How could he be a match for the Wolf King, with its Cultivation of Qi Consummation?

"Tang boy, where is this savior you mentioned!" the medicine shop owner, running out of strength, shouted loudly. At this rate, none of them would be able to escape.

Hearing the owner’s question, Tang Chuanwu’s expression became vacant for a moment.

Why hadn’t Master Lu arrived yet? Was it because they were too far from Kaihuang City? Or was the last reincarnation just a hopeless dream? Would anybody really believe him?

Seeing his opportunity, the Wolf King roared and lunged at Tang Chuanwu.

"Tang boy, watch out!"

Snapped back to reality, Tang Chuanwu quickly raised his defenses, but it was too late. The Wolf King bit his knife, knocking him off the camel.

A man and a wolf wrestled in the sand. During the struggle, Tang Chuanwu’s strength began to wane.

Suddenly, a surge of Sword Qi pierced the Wolf King’s head, with the sword tip stopping mere inches from Tang Chuanwu.

Swish, swish, swish—

The person was yet to arrive, but his sword was already at work.

Sword Qi fell like rain over the desert, and the pack of sand wolves was impaled by each strike, killed in an instant. In just a moment, the entire wolf pack was decimated.

The sword-wielding Immortal flew in with the sun behind him. Amidst the shocked gaze of onlookers, he landed and pulled Tang Chuanwu to his feet.

"I’m here."

Chapter 1007: 106: It Just So Happens That We Have a Match for You Here

"It’s Sword Immortal Lu!" Initially, the medicine shop owner and the others were astonished to be suddenly saved by the sword qi.

But after they got a clear look at Lu Yang’s appearance, their shock turned into wild joy.

"It was Sword Immortal Lu who saved us!"

"What, still not fully awake?" Lu Yang looked at Tang Chuanwu with a dazed expression, teasing him with a smile.

"I’m awake, I’m awake." Tang Chuanwu finally convinced himself that the events from the last reincarnation weren’t an illusion. They were real, and besides himself, someone else also had memories from the reincarnation and was on his side.

Lu Yang picked up the Sand Wolf King with a pierced skull and examined it carefully. He remembered that on the way to Kaihuang City, he casually dealt with a pack of Sand Wolves and a Sand Wolf King. How could another one appear?

"I see now, wolf packs have a sense of territory. Since Old Meng and I killed the last batch of Sand Wolves, naturally another pack would migrate here."

Two groups of Sand Wolves dying near Kaihuang City should mean that, for the time being, no packs would dare to take over here.

"However, I really didn’t expect your time reversal to be triggered by death." Lu Yang was forced to endure hundreds of reincarnations, which meant Tang Chuanwu had to experience death hundreds of times, whether voluntarily or not.

"Fairy, if death can serve as a condition for time retrospection, then how was Time Immortal assassinated in the past?"

"That I don’t know. What powers a Causality Dao Fruit possesses is unknown to all. It could have been a temporary separation between Time Immortal and the Time Dao Fruit, or perhaps a reverse causality, anchoring the result of ’Time Immortal’s demise’?"

"Alright then."

Returning to reality, Lu Yang pulled out several wooden swords and asked the caravan.

"I have a bit of a fate with Chuanwu, so I’m taking him back to Kaihuang City. I heard you’re going to Lotus City to trade in medicinal materials. If you’re still going to Lotus City, you can take these wooden swords as amulets..."

"We’re going back," the medicine shop owner hurriedly said, having just stepped back from the brink of death. At this point, who would have the mind to trade? It was more important to go home and recuperate.

"Okay, then. Be safe on your way back." Lu Yang handed the wooden swords to the medicine shop owner, then pulled Tang Chuanwu onto the Sword Washing Pool and headed straight for Kaihuang City.

The two landed at the entrance of Kaihuang City, and Lu Yang put away the Sword Washing Pool: "You said before that a Gold Core Period Cultivator took a fancy to Cui Que’er and wanted to kidnap her?"

"Yes."

"Do you remember what he looked like?"

"I’ll never forget!"

"Come on, take me to recognize the person."

It wasn’t time yet. The Gold Core Cultivator who had kidnapped Cui Que’er hadn’t arrived in the slums. Tang Chuanwu searched around the slums for a while and finally found a sneaky figure, seemingly looking for someone.

"That’s him!"

"Fang Wudi?" Lu Yang raised an eyebrow, surprised to find out that the Gold Core Period Cultivator Tang Chuanwu mentioned was none other than Fang Wudi, ranked ninety-fifth on the land ranking.

If there were no surprises in this competition, he would be the champion.

Could it be that he took a liking to Cui Que’er when Qiaoqiao and Queer passed the arena?

"He probably took interest in Queer’s Profound Yin Body," said Immortal Fairy. "This Fang has some kind of innate Venerate body, which is actually a lesser body constitution similar to the Pure Yang Constitution.

Such constitutions either end like the Meng Family Kid, walking a dark path but achieving a respectable outcome, or they utilize Yin and Yang balancing methods to suppress the Yang Qi in the body, which leads to an average future."

The Profound Yin Body, born on a Yin year, Yin month, and Yin day, counted as a special physique. Lu Yang had encountered a Foundation Establishment Stage Cultivator with this constitution during the celebrations in Qingzhou. The cultivator’s practice was not slow, lagging behind Lu Yang by only one major realm.

Lu Yang had always regarded Cui Que’er as an ordinary person, not realizing she had this kind of constitution.

Since the future Fang Wudi dared to kidnap Cui Que’er for cultivation practice, it implied this wasn’t his first time doing something similar.

"Fang Wudi."

"Who is calling me?" Fang Wudi turned in surprise, only to see a large hand grasping his neck.

"Who are you?" Fang Wudi released the oppressive aura of his golden core, and inside his dantian, a plump golden core hung in suspension; it was indeed a Supreme Golden Elixir from the ranks of the First-Grade Golden Cores.

Fang Wudi, shocked, found that his prideful golden core was like a stone ox sinking into the sea, utterly unusable, and what he called the golden core’s oppressive force couldn’t even leave his body.

The last time in the cycle of reincarnation, Fang Wudi, who couldn’t be harmed in the least by any of Tang Chuanwu’s myriad tactics, was easily subdued by Lu Yang, extinguished as effortlessly as one would crush an ant.

"Lu Yang," Lu Yang indifferently reported his name, and Fang Wudi’s eyes bulged round in utter disbelief.

After all, Fang Wudi was ranked ninety-fifth on the Earth List and had a special status; handing him over to Master Duanchen might cause the master some trouble.

But it didn’t matter; Lu Yang had a more suitable candidate in mind.

Calculating the time before he set out, Lu Yang had informed Old Meng about the troubles within Kaihuang City, and by now Old Meng should have caught everyone and taken them back to Kaihuang Temple; they had agreed to meet at the temple gate.

True to expectations, Lu Yang arrived at the temple gate with Tang Chuanwu and Fang Wudi to see Meng Jingzhou just coming out.

"Back so soon? What did you go off to do in such a hurry?"

"Here, take this person." Lu Yang tossed Fang Wudi to Meng Jingzhou.

"Who is this?"

This cycle had just begun, and before the tournament where Fang Wudi was to appear had started, Lu Yang had urged Meng Jingzhou to quickly handle the trouble, hence Meng Jingzhou did not recognize Fang Wudi.

However, upon catching Fang Wudi, Meng Jingzhou could feel that he had a special constitution.

"His name is Fang Wudi, and he might be the first-place winner of this competition. He possesses an innate Venerate body, just like your Single Body Spiritual Root, both of which are of the Yang constitution. To balance Yin and Yang, he has probably coerced quite a few people."

"Master Duanchen did invite us both to act as judges to advise the top eight, so go ahead and give him your advice now."

"Oh?" Meng Jingzhou’s eyes slightly narrowed as he looked at the trembling Fang Wudi, a cold smile curling at the corner of his mouth, "Then leave him to me."

"I need to see Elder Brother Dai; I’ll see you later."

Lu Yang took Tang Chuanwu inside the guest room of Kaihuang Temple and asked him to stay there quietly.

Then Tang Chuanwu saw Lu Yang lie on the bed, suddenly losing all signs of life, and then just as suddenly, he came back to life. Soon after, a spatial gate opened, and Lu Yang stepped through it, disappearing. A while later, the spatial gate opened again, and Lu Yang returned, holding the Green Peak Sword, his spirit bright and fresh.

Just like the sixth cycle, after the Forgetfulness Poison outbreak, Lu Yang informed Master Duanchen to take charge, and he and Old Meng acted in full view of the public, as their Bodhi clones self-destructed, leaving the citizens holding their breath.

Two figures ascended to the sky, looking down at Lu Yang and his accomplice with indifference.

"The Meng Family uprooted all of our arrangements in Buddha Country, making it impossible for us to stay. Before we leave, wouldn’t it seem like the Ruthless Sect is too weak if we don’t do something?"

Lu Yang watched the two with great interest, "Oh, from what you two are saying, it’s not only about meddling with the city’s people; you also want to deal with both of us?"

"The two of you exposed our sect’s movements, making you the culprits. You don’t run back to Great Xia, instead, you brazenly continue to stay in Buddha Country; you really don’t know the meaning of death."

Lu Yang watched the two Ruthless Sect Elders with a mocking smile, "You two are at the Mid Integration Stage, but Old Meng and I are only at the Mid-stage of Transforming Spirit; how could we possibly be your match?"

"However, it’s quite a coincidence that we happen to have someone here who is qualified to be your match."

Lu Yang raised the Green Peak Sword, and a man wearing a deep blue robe stepped out from the small world of the Green Peak Sword.

"Elder Brother Dai, you must make a decision for the two of us brothers; it was the Ruthless Sect who came looking for us first."

Chapter 1008: 1007: Crushing at Every Level

Dai Bufan glanced at Lu Yang, who stood honestly behind.

People from the Ruthless Sect found you first?

So how did you know the Ruthless Sect would come, and even knew to go back to the Sect to call for help?

Dai Bufan moved his body, his bones making creaking sounds. He had been in charge of the Task Hall for years, rarely needing to lift a finger, but that did not mean he had grown weak.

He had taken over the Task Hall from the Grand Elder early on, not only because the Grand Elder was lazy; the most important reason was his strength was sufficient for great responsibilities!

Among his peers, except for Yun Zhi, he had never lost to anyone!

"It’s rare to encounter the Ruthless Sect," Dai Bufan felt somewhat nostalgic. Over the years, the members of the Ruthless Sect he had seen were souls barely clinging to life; a lively Ruthless Sect Elder like this was a first.

"Sky Change!"

Dai Bufan swiftly raised his hand towards the sky, and instantly dark clouds gathered, bringing a torrential rain that had not been seen in Kaihuang City for five hundred years, instantly cooling the city and the surrounding desert.

He was a Water Spirit Root; this was his home field.

Everywhere, the whole world seemed to become a realm of water. The citizens of Kaihuang City, who had lived so long, were seeing this version of Kaihuang City for the first time.

"Rivers in Reverse!"

The rainwater flowed through the street openings like thousands of streams, which, as if alive, turned into little flood dragons attacking the two Elders.

"Black Tortoise Shell!"

"White Bone Flying Boat!"

Elders Bai Li Ting and Wu Shui had not expected Dai Bufan to cast his spells so quickly, and could hardly believe he was also at the Mid Integration Stage.

The Black Tortoise Shell guarded in front of Elder Bai Li Ting, while Elder Wu Shui hid in the White Bone Flying Boat, using the boat’s hull to block.

The little flood dragons were agile and versatile. They hit the Black Tortoise Shell, scattered into droplets, climbed along the shell’s surface, and then reformed into little flood dragons, besieging Bai Li Ting.

Elder Wu Shui took out the Ten Thousand Soul Banner, numerous lost souls wailed, controlling the boat’s cannons to bombard the little flood dragons.

The White Bone Flying Boat drifted and bobbed on the sea like a real ship, as well as crashing into rocks, shattering to pieces.

The little flood dragons, after being dispersed, condensed again as if they had endless life. Whatever magic skill had been imbued in these little flood dragons, they could use their bodies as cages, imprisoning souls within.

Even with ten thousand souls in Elder Wu Shui’s Ten Thousand Soul Banner, it couldn’t withstand such depletion.

Elder Wu Shui’s cultivation techniques were also related to water, which was why he knew how fierce Dai Bufan under such conditions was, and that he must be contained!

"Here, I set the rule: ’Rain shall not fall in Kaihuang City’."

Instantly, Kaihuang City turned into clear skies, and the little flood dragons made of rainwater also disappeared one by one.

A battle in the Body Integration Realm relies on setting up mutual restrictions with each rule established.

But Dai Bufan, faced with the rule set by Elder Wu Shui to disperse the clouds and rain, showed no intention of countering with his own rule.

He took out a red banner emblazoned with auspicious clouds; the moment the Five-colored Auspicious Cloud Banner was unfurled, countless intricate and beautiful characters flew from the banner, branding the void, layering one Array Method upon another.

The Array Method had nine layers, named the Nine-layered Pagoda Formation. Once the Pagoda Formation was activated, the two Elders instantly bent over, unable to stand straight.

"Xuanwu Manifestation!" Elder Bai Li Ting fused his spear with the Black Tortoise Shell, summoning the corpse of Xuan Wu, intending to break through the Great Formation with the weight of Xuan Wu.

But the Xuan Wu corpse collided with the boundary of the Array Method, like hitting mud, unable to exert any strength.

Dai Bufan charged into the Array Method, swinging the Five-colored Auspicious Cloud Banner like waving a club.

"Xuantian Axe!"

"Thunder Fire Red Gold Fist!"

The two Elders each summoned an axe capable of splitting space, and a fist carrying a trace of the power of Heavenly Tribulation.

Dai Bufan remained unmoved, not even raising an eyebrow, swinging the banner to strike, shattering the Xuantian Axe with a single blow, striking straight at Elder Wu Shui’s face, sending the two Elders scrambling for their teeth. No matter their sky-high abilities or methods to recover from injuries, they were of no use.

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou stood in Kaihuang Temple, looking up at the battle. Seeing their usually quiet Elder Brother Dai transformed into a fierce warrior slaughtering all around, they shivered with fear.

"This the first time you’ve seen Elder Brother Dai fight someone."

"He strikes so viciously."

The two of them theoretically were on par with a Mid Integration Stage cultivator, but that was for an ordinary Mid Integration Stage cultivator.

If they encountered a cultivator like Dai Bufan of the Integration Realm, they’d end up no better than these two Elders.

An aged voice echoed throughout Kaihuang City.

"So young yet with such a cultivation level; you are no ordinary junior," he said.

As the aged figure appeared, the Nine-layered Pagoda Formation collapsed on its own, and the two Elders finally found relief and quickly sought protection from the old man.

"Vice Sect Hierarch."

"Lou Nan?" Dai Bufan held the Red Banner, his combat spirit unabated.

Lou Nan, Vice Sect Hierarch of the Ruthless Sect, Early Tribulation Stage.

"All the people in the city have been poisoned, the cultivators are alright since they can detoxify themselves, but the commoners are frail and only I know the cure."

"If the poison in these people isn’t cured, they will die unnatural deaths within ten days."

"If you want the people of this city to live longer, you’d better hand them all over to me."

As he spoke, Lou Nan released the aura of Passing Tribulation, pressing it all onto Dai Bufan alone, forcing him to transform his body into a water form to mitigate the burden.

The antidote was known only to Lou Nan; neither Bai Liting nor Wu Shui knew it. If Dai Bufan wanted to save everyone in the city, he had to let Lou Nan take them all.

"Why is he insisting on taking the entire city’s people?" Lu Yang wondered, it was the same during the previous reincarnations; Lou Nan had directly teleported Kaihuang City away.

Immortal Fairy guessed casually while lying on the Lotus Platform, "Perhaps he’s trying to condense the outline of a Dao Fruit. By observing the ruthless actions of the entire city for a long time, if he’s lucky enough to have an insight, he might have a chance to condense an outline of a Dao Fruit related to ruthlessness."

"Master Lu, what should we do?" Tang Chuanwu was breaking out in a cold sweat. As Lou Nan appeared, even Dai Bufan, who seemed like a war god, had to struggle to maintain his stance and was powerless to resist. Could it be that they had to initiate the next reincarnation and seek a new method of breakthrough?

Lu Yang smiled at Tang Chuanwu, "Don’t worry, I have plenty of people on my side."

With that, he shot into the sky, standing side by side with Dai Bufan, firmly raising the Green Peak Sword.

"Want to take the entire city’s people? Did you ask my sword?"

"Mid-stage Transforming Spirit," Lou Nan would have laughed if he weren’t in the Merciless State of Mind.

Could a mid-stage Transforming Spirit reverse the heavens?

"Take my sword!"

Lu Yang raised the Green Peak Sword high, the swordsmanship majestic. As the sword fell, a small world opened, and a Great Snow Mountain large enough to cover the entire Kaihuang City floated out from the small world, directly knocking Lou Nan away.

If previously Dai Bufan’s calling of the wind and summoning of the rain had merely cooled down Kaihuang City, the appearance of the Great Snow Mountain turned it cold.

Two silhouettes flew out from the Great Snow Mountain, one tall, one petite.

The tall silhouette sneered at Lou Nan, "Calling everyone ’junior’ so freely, boy, are you that old?"

"The Merciless Poison, is it that hard to cure?" the petite figure scoffed.

When Lou Nan saw the faces of the two, he immediately broke out of his Merciless State of Mind, his earlier strategizing demeanor completely vanished.

"Vast Sea Daoist Lord, Chasing Moon True Person!"

Two Semi-Immortals!

Run!

Lou Nan turned to flee, and just as he was about to take Kaihuang City with him, a door appeared in front of him, teleporting him back to his original position.

"Thinking of running? Did you get my permission?"

Chapter 1009: 1008: As We Belong to the Righteous Path, We Don’t Need to Talk About the Morality of the Martial World

Although Lu Yang didn’t know how to resolve the Forgetfulness Poison, he knew there was someone who definitely could.

For instance, the Chasing Moon True Person who possessed the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo.

The last time he reincarnated and returned to his Sect to call for help, he had asked Chasing Moon True Person if she could detoxify it, and she said that although she was not proficient in pharmacology, she could directly use the Rule Power of the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo to detoxify.

Chasing Moon True Person snapped her fingers, and the effect range of the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo spread throughout the entire city, causing everyone to shiver in unison, and they recovered.

"You, you..." Lou Nan realized that no matter what kind of Spatial Divine Ability he used, he couldn’t escape the confines of Kaihuang City.

This was not surprising, as Vast Sea Daoist Lord’s mastery of the Embryonic Spatial Dao Fruit wasn’t challenged by enemies trying to flee.

No matter how many escape methods the enemy had, how could they possibly escape from the Embryonic Spatial Dao Fruit?

"Us? What about us?" Chasing Moon True Person looked at Lou Nan with a smile that was not quite a smile.

Upon seeing the smile of Chasing Moon True Person, Lou Nan felt as if his heart was harshly seized, his panic immensely magnified as if he was still a mere mortal who had fallen into the deep sea, eroded by the dark waters, with no sense of direction.

Just one face-to-face encounter, and Lou Nan had already fallen prey to Chasing Moon True Person’s move, totally unaware of it himself.

"Great Black Sky Splitting Palm!"

Seeing that he couldn’t escape, Lou Nan was forced to stake everything in one last struggle, as two huge black palms tore open the sky, with ancient Divine Demons hissing between the clouds.

The entire city was restless, and even though they did not understand the combat methods of High Level Cultivators, they could see the severity of this move.

If this palm strike were to land, the entire Kaihuang City would be reduced to nothing!

"Mystic Yellow Finger."

Facing this terrifying attack, Vast Sea Daoist Lord, calm and composed, extended a finger swathed in the Mystic Yellow two energies, and poked towards the sky, dwarfed by the two giant black palms, this finger seemed too small to be seen.

But the moment the finger collided with the giant hand, it met an obstruction like the world’s barrier, unable to be pressed down any further.

Lou Nan gritted his teeth and sweated profusely, having exerted his full strength, yet compared to Vast Sea Daoist Lord, who seemed as calm as a breeze, this palm strike didn’t seem terrifying at all.

Putting aside Vast Sea Daoist Lord’s Semi-Immortal status, he was also a great power at the Pass Tribulation Peak, Lou Nan was merely at the Early Tribulation Stage, how could he possibly contend against him?

"Ultimate Cold Intent."

The Moonlight Power condensed into snowflakes and scattered over the human realm, bringing an extreme coldness, falling on the huge black palms. Every spot where a snowflake landed quickly turned into ice, even Lou Nan, who was casting the spell, indirectly felt this severe cold through his magic skills.

But in just a moment, the huge black palms turned to ice blocks, and with a slight push from Vast Sea Daoist Lord’s finger, a crack opened in the palm and quickly spread throughout the entire hand.

Crash—

The ice-block encased giant hand shattered and fell, a spatial vortex appeared below, and Vast Sea Daoist Lord shifted it all to the East Sea.

Leaving aside the Seven Emotions Dao Fruit Embryo, Chasing Moon True Person could call herself an old veteran at the Transmission Tribulation Stage.

Lou Nan panted heavily, not expecting this move to cause no obstacles for the two of them but also due to the collapse of the technique, even he, the spellcaster, suffered a backlash.

Vast Sea Daoist Lord stood with his hands behind his back, casually speaking, and drew an invisible line across half of the city towards Lou Nan.

Lou Nan’s hair stood on end, feeling a crisis impending, but was clueless about where the attack would come from.

"Dodge!"

In a hurry, Lou Nan dodged, only to see a horizontal line appear at his waist, his lower body not responding to commands, and still remaining in place.

"Spatial techniques!"

Lou Nan finally understood what Vast Sea Daoist Lord had done—he had used spatial slicing techniques to bisect himself at the waist!

"Combat Star Shift!"

Lou Nan roared, igniting his Essence Blood to wield the Combat Star Shift capable of transferring injuries, risking his Essence Blood to rejoin his bisected halves.

"Anger." Chasing Moon True Person issued an order to Lou Nan, whose recovery fueled an uncontrollable rage, charging at Vast Sea Daoist Lord like a crazed bull.

Lou Nan’s palm displayed the Five Elements Spells, each finger conjuring one of the spells, too powerful for other Early Tribulation Stage Cultivators to withstand directly.

"Haha, now this is interesting." Vast Sea Daoist Lord laughed ceaselessly, as Lou Nan, dodging repeatedly since the fight began, wasn’t fully concentrating on the battle but instead frantically sought a way to escape.

How could that make for a satisfying fight?

"Blood Sacrifice Sky Fist!"

Lou Nan’s originally pallid hair unexpectedly turned black strand by strand, signaling a return to youthful vigor at the peak of his power. Wisps of blood circled around him, his fists swollen red, appearing soaked with fresh blood like a monstrous criminal.

"Dragon Subduing Wood."

Just as Lou Nan’s fist was about to connect with Vast Sea Daoist Lord, a piece of wood shot out right beneath him, striking his chin and knocking out several of his teeth.

Then, more and more pieces of Dragon Subduing Wood materialized from thin air, battering Lou Nan back and forth like a ball, robbing him of any opportunity to cast his moves.

These were Dragon Subduing Woods cultivated by Vast Sea Daoist Lord, incredibly tough, typically stored in an Alternative Space and summoned as needed. Those unfamiliar with spatial fluctuations couldn’t detect how the Dragon Subduing Woods were summoned.

"I’ve had enough!" Lou Nan bellowed, erupting with a terrifying aura, blasting away the Dragon Subduing Woods.

"Let’s have a real fight if you’re capable!" After being emboldened by his emotions, Lou Nan became irritable, even forgetting the vast gap between him and Vast Sea Daoist Lord.

"Myriad Ghosts Dharma Aspect!"

Lou Nan manifested his unique Dharma Aspect, towering a thousand feet with resentment swirling around it, obscuring the sunlight.

This Dharma Aspect was composed of thousands of Fierce Ghosts, each capable of causing immense damage to villages and cities if they escaped to the human world.

"Is that all?"

Vast Sea Daoist Lord clasped his hands and twirled them as if wringing a towel. The space around Lou Nan, hidden within the Dharma Aspect, twisted like two giant hands squeezing his body, twisting it into a twister shape.
