Having lost their master’s control, the Dharma Aspect composed of tens of thousands of Fierce Ghosts collapsed thunderously, the ghosts scattering in all directions.

"Frostfall." Chasing Moon True Person whispered gently, exhaling a breath of icy chill, so cold that even spirits could be frozen.

Though the tens of thousands of Fierce Ghosts possessed great divine abilities, they were no different from ordinary spirits in front of Chasing Moon True Person. When exposed to her Frostfall Chill, they instantly turned into icy chunks, still maintaining their menacing gestures.

"Done." Vast Sea Daoist Lord concluded the battle with a spatial cage.

The spatial cage, invisible and intangible, showed nothing around Lou Nan. Yet, should Lou Nan try to escape in any direction, no matter how far he ran, he would be transported back to the starting point.

Seeing Vast Sea Daoist Lord and Chasing Moon True Person descend to Kaihuang Temple, Lu Yang hurried forward to greet them.

"The master has worked hard, the real person has worked hard."

"What hard work? Next time there’s a situation like this, remember to call me, I’m ready to serve anytime," Vast Sea Daoist Lord expressed with great joy, rarely having the chance to demonstrate his skills before the public.

Lu Yang heaved a sigh of relief. He had been worried that calling upon two Semi-Immortals to deal with a mere Early Tribulation Stage Cultivator might seem like overdoing it to the master.

As if seeing through Lu Yang’s concerns, Vast Sea Daoist Lord advised, "For us on the Righteous Path to deal with the Demon Path, overwhelming the weak with the strong and bullying them is necessary."

Chapter 1010: 1009: The Method of Refining the Oblivion Pill

"Senior Chasing Moon, should we put Great Snow Mountain back into the sword?"

Lu Yang cautiously asked, knowing that storing Great Snow Mountain within the Green Peak Sword’s small world would require the personal intervention of Chasing Moon True Person.

Having such a massive snow mountain floating above Kaihuang City was certainly impressive, but it was also quite frightening.

"Is it over?"

Chasing Moon True Person asked indifferently, somewhat reluctant in his heart, as it was rare for him to come out.

Seeing the concern in Chasing Moon True Person’s eyes, Lu Yang quickly bowed his head and said, "If I store Great Snow Mountain in my Green Peak Sword, then the area around me can be considered part of Great Snow Mountain’s domain, and all you need to do, Senior Chasing Moon, is not stray too far from me."

"That works as well." Chasing Moon True Person showed a reluctant expression, acquiescing to Lu Yang’s suggestion.

"Is it, is it already resolved?" Tang Chuanwu could hardly dare to breathe during the battle, anxious and uneasy.

This was a Transcendance Tribulation Stage supreme powerhouse, and he was subdued just like that?

"It’s resolved." Lu Yang stretched lazily, thinking that despite all the unexpected occurrences during the Buddha Bathing Festival, in the end, everything had been dealt with.

"You have all worked hard, benefactors." Master Duanchen hurriedly came out to give thanks.

Vast Sea Daoist Lord, Chasing Moon True Person, Dai Bufan... the stature of each person here was so great it was boundless; even the Western Paradise Temple would have to receive them with the highest standard of ceremonial rites.

"I’m sorry, Master Duanchen, for turning the Buddha Bathing Festival into this state," Lu Yang apologized, as after the successive battles, there was no doubt that the festival could no longer go on.

"It doesn’t matter, it’s good that everyone is safe."

Even though this year’s Buddha Bathing Festival had to be abruptly suspended, Master Duanchen could envision that the battle in Kaihuang City, featuring the capture of the Ruthless Sect’s Vice Sect Hierarch by two Semi-Immortals, alongside the participation of Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou, would definitely become a widely told tale.

In this story, Kaihuang City was undoubtedly an inextricable part of the narrative.

This way, would Kaihuang City still worry about not being famous enough, or not having enough visitors to Kaihuang Temple?

The impact of this year’s Buddha Bathing Festival exceeded Master Duanchen’s original expectations by more than tenfold.

"Master Duanchen, we will take our leave," Lu Yang bowed as he bid farewell.

"Chuanwu, would you like to come back with us to Seek Dao Sect?" Lu Yang asked, since he knew about Tang Chuanwu’s Time Retrospection ability, it was out of the question to leave him in Kaihuang City.

If other Immortals discovered him, that would be problematic.

"Can I bring my sister and Queer?" Tang Chuanwu asked nervously, as what he was most concerned about were these two, for whom he had cycled through reincarnation countless times.

"Yes," Lu Yang replied, aware of Tang Chuanwu’s worries.

Tang Chuanwu breathed a sigh of relief, as this was the first time things had gone so smoothly since he had acquired the Time Retrospection ability.

Luckily, he had encountered Master Lu.

Meng Jingzhou was surprised and secretly messaged Lu Yang, "Old Lu, what’s the situation with this kid, and why are we taking him to the Sect?"

"It involves the Dao Fruit of an Immortal."

Meng Jingzhou grew even more surprised, "How did you get involved with an Immortal again?"

"Get lost, as if I wanted this."

"Grandmaster, Seniors, what about you two?"

"Wait a moment." Vast Sea Daoist Lord raised his hand to signal, then activated his embryonic Spatial Dao Fruit. Two elders from the Ruthless Sect screamed miserably as they fell out from a spatial black hole, also confined within a spatial prison.

These two elders fled in a panic after seeing the vice hierarch’s defeat, not realizing they had already been marked by Vast Sea Daoist Lord and couldn’t escape even if they tried.

Vast Sea Daoist Lord flicked his finger, and three spatial cages were sent into the Green Peak Sword’s small world, "You all go back, I have to make a trip to the East Sea."

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou looked at Vast Sea Daoist Lord with odd expressions. Master, is your trip to the East Sea really for official matters?

"The snow mountain is in your sword, I’ll follow you," Chasing Moon True Person said, barely stopping short of openly saying he wanted to stay outside a bit longer.

"Can’t we ask the master to teleport us back?" Meng Jingzhou quietly asked. It would be so convenient.

Lu Yang gave Meng Jingzhou a glare, "Do you want us to have an exit record without an entry record?"

Lu Yang and his group separately found Tang Qiaoqiao and Cui Que’er. With Tang Qiaoqiao, it was easy to handle; as siblings, wherever Tang Chuanwu went, she would follow.

As for Goodman Cui, upon hearing that his daughter was going to Great Xia, he was extremely reluctant to let her go but also knew that this was her opportunity and could not obstruct her. He bid farewell to Cui Que’er with a heavy heart.

"Miss Que’er has a Profound Yin Body. Our sect does not have extensive research on this aspect. Keeping her at Seek Dao Sect would have been a delay."

Lu Yang turned to ask Chasing Moon True Person standing nearby, "Senior Chasing Moon, can you send Miss Que’er to the Fairy Palace?"

"I can, but she will need to undergo the Fairy Palace’s entry trial."

"Of course," Lu Yang trusted Cui Que’er’s character.

The group made their way through the desert and safely returned to Great Xia, back to Seek Dao Sect.

Since the journey from Kaihuang City to Seek Dao Sect was long, and they were accompanying three ordinary people, this return trip was even slower than the outbound journey.

Bai Ye also appeared for a few nights during the journey, learning that as long as the Great Snow Mountain was placed inside the Green Peak Sword, he could leave with Lu Yang, he was grinning from ear to ear.

Lu Yang arranged for someone to send Cui Que’er to Laurel Fairy Palace, along with an item from Chasing Moon True Person as a token.

Dai Bufan returned to the Task Hall, quite satisfied with the trip to Kaihuang City.

"Junior Brother Lu was in such a rush to contact me, I thought something might happen, but it was just an Early Tribulation Stage Cultivator... wait, when did I start looking down on Early Tribulation Stage Cultivators?"

After settling Tang Chuanwu and his sister, placing the Great Snow Mountain behind Sky Gate Mountain as usual, Lu Yang returned to Sky Gate Mountain and took out the Green Peak Sword.

With a flash of cold light from the Green Peak Sword, Eldest Senior Sister walked out of the small world.

Lu Yang was concerned that something might go wrong with the heart of Kaihuang City, like an Immortal cloaking the city and preventing him from summoning Eldest Senior Sister. So he simply invited Eldest Senior Sister into the Green Peak Sword as well.

Unfortunately, no such incident occurred along the way.

"You brought Tang Chuanwu back just like that?"

Yun Zhi raised her eyebrows, puzzled by the intentions of the Time Dao Fruit user, wondering why they would grant Tang Chuanwu this ability. She had thought Tang Chuanwu was connected to the Time Dao Fruit user and anticipated some obstacles during their return.

On the road, Lu Yang had already asked Tang Chuanwu how he came to possess the ability of Time Retrospection. Tang Chuanwu shook his head; he had no idea where this power came from.

After all, it was a power that activated upon death. Perhaps Tang Chuanwu was born with this ability, but it was only half a year ago when he accidentally died that he discovered his ability to rewind time.

"Oh, Junior Brother, did you want the recipe for the Oblivion Pill?" Yun Zhi took out three spatial cages from the Green Peak Sword’s small world, which contained the Vice Sect Hierarch and two Elders.

"On the way, I took the liberty of interrogating them. It’s easy to deal with the ingredients and their proportions. The only problem is that someone needs to ingest the raw materials, use the human body as a Pill Furnace for Refinement, and the solidified blood that flows out is the Oblivion Pill."

"As expected," Lu Yang had roughly guessed the result from the conversations between the two Elders.

This was going to be problematic. They, Seek Dao Sect - a Righteous Sect - couldn’t possibly use ordinary people to refine the medicine.

Suddenly, Lu Yang’s gaze flickered to the three Ruthless Sect members.

"Hey, we can just feed the raw materials to these three and then drain their blood. This is Body Integration Realm, and Transcendance Tribulation Stage blood we’re talking about. The Oblivion Pills refined from it could even be used by Integration Period Cultivators!"

Chapter 1011: 1110: Specialty of the Buddha Country

"So it seems, indeed it can be done." Yun Zhi nodded slowly after careful consideration.

"Right?" Seeing Eldest Senior Sister supporting his idea, Lu Yang became more and more excited. "We only knew before to use prisoners’ bodies for Refining and their souls to create Spirit Devices. But in recent years, there have been more and more prisoners in Captive Peak, and our Sect’s magic artifacts are plentiful enough; we don’t really need to refine any for the time being."

"Just right, we can make use of the remaining physical bodies of the prisoners in Captive Peak and refine their blood into Elixirs."

"Moreover, they are all at Integration Period and Transcendance Tribulation Stage, with exceptionally strong self-healing abilities. Inhale a couple of breaths of Spiritual Energy, and the blood that flowed out will return."

The main source of income for the Seek Dao Sect is Refining Pills, but even so, the Sect could not mass-produce Elixirs for Integration Period Cultivators to use; they were all refined by the Seventh Elder and a few of his disciples.

Now, thankfully, making the Oblivion Pill doesn’t even require the use of a Pill Furnace, just feeding the medicine and drawing blood—quite convenient.

Depending on the dilution degree of the blood, it can be used by anyone ranging from Body Integration Great Powers to ordinary people.

"There’s one more thing, Meng Junzi encountered the Grey Immortal while searching for the untraceable Yingtian Immortal in Buddha Country."

"Ah?" Lu Yang exclaimed in surprise. He had heard that Meng Junzi had fought with an Immortal in Buddha Country, but he thought it was with the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord or The Controller of the Great Yu Dynasty. It turned out to be with Ashy Bean.

"He’s still alive?" There was no doubt about Ashy Bean’s fighting strength. Even the alliance of the Four Ancient Immortals might not find it an easy fight, and going solo would be even more life-threatening. Didn’t the Yingtian Immortal have to abandon his body to escape death? The Qilin Immortal and Immortal Jiuchong have yet to show up probably because they too are hiding from Ashy Bean.

"Still alive. From Meng Junzi’s description, it seems that the Grey Immortal intentionally let him go.

Yun Zhi recounted Meng Junzi’s encounter with Ashy Bean, primarily for Fairy Eternity, with Lu Yang just listening in.

Fairy Eternity emerged from behind Lu Yang, wrapped her arms, and nodded, "Those who have seen her forget about her existence; I have not yet seen any Immortal with such a characteristic. It doesn’t sound like any Divine Ability or magic skill. It’s probably a sign that she is approaching ’Transcendence.’

"It seems that the path she’s taking is the right one—achieving Transcendence is about severing Causality and erasing one’s sense of existence."

"She forged an identity and deceived that Meng guy, so does that mean ’faking one’s identity’ won’t generate Causality with others?"

This was also unknown to Fairy Eternity. The Realm that Ashy Bean is in has never been achieved by any Immortal from antiquity to the present. This method of avoiding Causality is most likely something Ashy Bean figured out on her own.

"Then I will go and talk to the Seventh Elder first, to discuss the specifics of refining the Oblivion Pill."

Watching the silhouette of Eldest Senior Sister, carrying three empty space cages, walking away, Lu Yang frowned.

"Strange, I feel like I’ve forgotten something."

That shouldn’t be right. He found Tang Chuanwu, captured the Vice Sect Hierarch of the sect without an invitation, this trip to Buddha Country was very successful.

"Second-in-command, you’re back from Buddha Country?" Yun Mengmeng heard about Lu Yang’s return and immediately ran over.

Lu Yang inwardly cursed—bad luck, finally realizing what he had forgotten; he forgot to bring back specialties from Buddha Country!

But then again, it seems like there really wasn’t anything worth bringing back from Buddha Country as a specialty.

Doudou?

"Where are the specialties?" Yun Mengmeng asked with excitedly wringing hands.

Lu Yang broke into a cold sweat, trying to phrase his words delicately, "Well, Sister Mengmeng, I had intended to bring back some local specialties for you, but there was a bit of an accident..."

Fairy Eternity, ever so understanding, stepped up to provide cover for Lu Yang, "Of course, there are specialties, but it’s not something specific. It’s a special cooking method from Buddha Country—Doudou."

"Little Yang specifically requested that I learn this cooking method to impress you with my skills, Third Boss."

"Immortal, I..."

"Little Yang, there’s no need to explain. I understand your concerns. Don’t worry, you know full well my learning abilities. I assure you the Doudou I make will be authentic in flavor."

"Second-in-command, really?" Yun Mengmeng’s eyes sparkled as she looked at Lu Yang when she heard both the Chief Housekeeper and the Second-in-command care so much about her.

Facing Yun Mengmeng’s innocent gaze, Lu Yang couldn’t bring himself to deny it and could only nod his head.

At that moment, no matter how unpalatable the Buddha Country gruel might be, Lu Yang was hoping that the Fairy would keep it authentic.

May the Worldly Venerable bless... oh no, I mean may Eldest Senior Sister bless me.

Sky Gate Mountain had a kitchen used by Fairy Eternity during Ancient Times, and Eldest Senior Sister had kindly stocked it with plenty of ingredients.

Fairy Eternity told Lu Yang and Yun Mengmeng to wait outside as she marched into the kitchen, ready to strut her stuff.

Her soul was strong enough to touch physical objects.

"Second-in-command, what all did you do in Buddha Country?" Yun Mengmeng asked out of boredom as they waited for Fairy Eternity to cook.

Lu Yang said earnestly, "On the surface, my trip to Buddha Country was for pleasure, but in reality, I received intelligence about Wu Qing’s presence there. I used that as a cover to carry out a thorough and detailed investigation in Buddha Country..."

Yun Mengmeng listened attentively, not expecting the Second-in-command’s trip to Buddha Country to involve so much consideration. When she heard about the Second-in-command’s exquisite plans, she could not help but exclaim admiringly, "That’s amazing!"

"Here we go, my specially-made gruel is ready." Fairy Eternity floated out two plates of gruel with her mind.

"It smells so good!" Yun Mengmeng sniffed, and though she had tasted many delicious foods over the three years since she arrived in the outside world, none were as fragrant as the Chief Housekeeper’s gruel.

"Of course, this is made by me, even Yingtian Immortal and the others have praised my cooking skills."

"Little Yang, you eat too." Fairy Eternity handed the other plate of gruel to Lu Yang.

She was always fair in her actions, not favoring anyone. Since she had made gruel for Yun Mengmeng, she couldn’t neglect the Second-in-command.

Lu Yang shivered, Why do I have a share too?

Lu Yang was inwardly torn and didn’t dare to eat right away, stealthily glancing at Yun Mengmeng beside him.

Yun Mengmeng ate a spoonful of the gruel with a smile, making satisfied noises and continuing to eat spoonful after spoonful.

Lu Yang was utterly astonished; could it be that Fairy Eternity outperformed herself this time, and it was actually delicious?

He finally made up his mind, ate a spoonful of the gruel, and immediately felt sour, sweet, bitter, spicy, and salty flavors overwhelm him, his head becoming dizzy.

With a bang, Lu Yang’s head hit the table. He saw Yun Mengmeng, who looked as if she was drunk, stand up unsteadily, giggling and pointing at Lu Yang.

"Eh, why are there three Second-in-commands?"

Sister Mengmeng, you were poisoned, why didn’t you say so earlier? If I had known, I wouldn’t have eaten it...

After an unknown amount of time, Lu Yang woke up from his deep slumber, his head foggy and aching fiercely: "What just happened?"

Fairy Eternity kindly looked after the Dynasty’s senior official: "Little Yang, you’re too tired from your trip to Buddha Country; you fell asleep halfway through your meal."

"Oh, then I’ll continue eating..."

...

"What just happened?"

"Little Yang, you’re too tired from your trip to Buddha Country; you fell asleep halfway through your meal."

"Oh, then I’ll continue eating..."

Chapter 1012: 1011 Whose Advice to Follow?

Lu Yang had a very long dream, and although he couldn’t recall what he dreamt about, he remembered that he had been cursed by an Immortal and had fallen into an endless cycle of reincarnations.

That’s not right, he brought Tang Chuanwu back, so why was there still reincarnation?

Once he realized this, Lu Yang’s mind gradually woke up, and he suddenly lifted his head, his eyes full of confusion.

What happened just now, how had he fallen asleep, and why couldn’t he remember anything?

Why had it gotten dark?

And why was there a warm sensation in his stomach, had he made another breakthrough?

The diligent Fairy Eternity had already cleaned up the dishes and wiped the table, everything was just as it had been before Lu Yang started eating, quietly waiting for Lu Yang to wake up.

Every time Little Yang woke up, he would take a bite and then fall back asleep—this cycle repeated many times. After finishing a bowl of porridge, Little Yang finally slept soundly.

Even fighting off sleepiness to eat his own porridge proved just how delicious his cooking was.

"You have experienced too many cycles of reincarnation in Buddha Country, and while your physical body hasn’t been affected much, your spirit has taken a great toll. Look, you fell asleep just now."

"Is that so?" Lu Yang felt he wasn’t as tired as Fairy Eternity described.

"Where’s Sister Mengmeng?" Lu Yang faintly remembered her being here.

"Oh, she danced away."

Lu Yang showed a puzzled expression, vaguely grasping a clue, but he had just woken up and wasn’t very clear-headed, his mind working slowly.

"By the way, Fairy, do you know any body refining techniques?" When Lu Yang fought with the Ruthless Sect Elder, he realized that even though his physique was far superior to his peers, he was still vastly outmatched when facing an Integration Period Cultivator.

It would be best to cover this weakness.

"What’s so hard about refining the body? The simplest way is to get struck by lightning. It so happens we know people here; Little Yang, you can choose any kind of Thunder Tribulation you want."

"...Is there another way?" Lu Yang thought that cultivation should be progressive, there was no need to be so aggressive.

"Immortal Fairy has a unique body refining technique that has never been taught to outsiders."

Before obtaining the Eternity Dao Fruit, Fairy Eternity did not rely on self-detonation to fight her enemies but on her unique body refining technique to compete with the great ancient geniuses, enjoying unrivaled glory.

"What is it?"

"The Pill Furnace body refining method—throwing you into a pill furnace, repeatedly roasting you with True Fire, and then adding various medicinal herbs into the furnace, allowing you to absorb the medicinal properties of these herbs amid the flames. Doing this for forty-nine days straight, you’ll see initial results. If you think that’s not intense enough, you could burn for a hundred days."

"...Is there another method?" Lu Yang felt like asking Fairy Eternity for cultivation methods might not have been a wise decision.

Lu Yang rarely asked Fairy Eternity for advice, and she was brimming with confidence and eagerness.

"Ah, don’t be so picky, the body refining techniques of Immortal Fairy aren’t something just anyone is qualified to enjoy. You should be secretly delighted."

Fairy Eternity, without further discussion, took possession of Lu Yang and controlled his body to walk into the Fairy’s kitchen.

In the kitchen stood a three-legged pill furnace, its walls carved with nine dragons, four phoenixes, and other ancient beasts, clearly invaluable.

"Why is there a pill furnace here?!"

"What do you mean? Isn’t it very normal to cook with a pill furnace?" Fairy Eternity said matter-of-factly, feeling that Little Yang was too naive.

She controlled Lu Yang’s body to lift the furnace lid and climb inside.

Fairy Eternity snapped her fingers, and a raging fire blazed beneath the pill furnace, the unique Immortal Fairy Fire belonging to the Fairy.

The Laurel Fairy Palace used the Immortal Fairy Fire, a relic from Ancient Times, for refining pills, refining tools, and heating hot springs—a shared treasure among all the elders and disciples of the Fairy Palace. Yet, Lu Yang had the privilege of enjoying the Immortal Fire alone, a luxury only afforded to high-ranking officials of the Great Bean Dynasty!

With a mere thought from Fairy Eternity, the ingredients—or rather, medicinal herbs—near the kitchen wall began to float, orderly flying into the pill furnace.

Immortal Fairy Eternity was indeed a master at cooking; as the herbs entered the Pill Furnace and were scorched by the Immortal Fire, they instantly vaporized and then condensed into a few drops of Spiritual Liquid.

These few drops of Spiritual Liquid represented the most essential part of the herb, with all impurities already removed - a pill refining technique only top-tier Pill Grandmasters could employ.

More and more herbs flew into the Pill Furnace, turning into boiling Spiritual Liquid, merging with each other, some adhering to the furnace walls, some sinking to the bottom.

"Done, all that’s left is to cook."

Immortal Fairy Eternity borrowed and returned in kind, restoring control of the body back to Lu Yang.

Previously seized by possession, Lu Yang had not yet felt it; now, as he regained his physical body, he immediately sensed the horrifying high temperature of the Pill Furnace. Some kind of restriction had been placed on the furnace, preventing flight, leaving Lu Yang hopping back and forth with his burning feet.

And there were drops of boiling Spiritual Liquid spilling onto his body, causing a piercing pain.

"Ah—"

Lu Yang’s screams could be heard coming from the Pill Furnace; the pain caused him to crash against the furnace walls, but the Pill Furnace was as heavy as a mountain, clearly not something Lu Yang could move.

God knows how long it had been, maybe a quarter of an hour, perhaps a day; Lu Yang was scorched until he completely lost the ability to perceive time.

...

"Junior Brother, your idea is indeed feasible." After getting confirmation from the Seventh Elder, Yun Zhi sent the three members of the Ruthless Sect to Captive Peak to gradually bleed out. Returning to Sky Gate Mountain and hearing the screams coming from the Fairy Kitchen, she hurriedly went to investigate.

Upon entering the kitchen and seeing the burning Pill Furnace, Yun Zhi instantly understood what her Junior Brother was doing.

This must be the Fairy’s method.

Yun Zhi opened the furnace door and pulled Lu Yang out.

Lu Yang’s clothes had already turned to ash, and he himself was scorched black, looking like charred wood; only when he spoke could one discern where his mouth was by his teeth.

"Eldest Senior Sister..." Upon seeing his Eldest Senior Sister, Lu Yang looked as if he had seen his savior.

Then Lu Yang heard the Eldest Senior Sister chastise the Immortal Fairy Eternity, who was enjoying the show, "Fairy, how can one take shortcuts in Body Refinement? Merely placing oneself in the Pill Furnace to cook, what if it leaves behind hidden dangers for the future?"

"To refine the body, one must train under heavy loads, endure to strengthen the sinews and bones, train until one cannot get up, wring out every bit of strength from the body, and yet still continue to cultivate. Only then can the physical limits be broken, and this is the proper technique of Body Refining."

The charred Lu Yang’s complexion instantly turned pale as memories long suppressed surged in his heart.

In the year when he had just entered the Sect, the Eldest Senior Sister, to lay a solid foundation for him, endured day and night refining his body, only letting him go when he reached Foundation Establishment.

That time was so painful it made him feel worse than death, and the mere thought made him shiver.

The Eldest Senior Sister’s Body Refining technique was by no means lighter than the Fairy’s.

About matters concerning Lu Yang, Immortal Fairy Eternity would not show weakness, "This is this Immortal’s Body Refining Technique, how could there be any flaws? Look, isn’t this Immortal just fine?"

Yun Zhi spoke earnestly, "Body Refinement should follow the proper path. Looking for shortcuts could harm Junior Brother."

"My Body Refining method has no issues."

"The orthodox Body Refining technique is the Righteous Path."

"Little Yang, whose advice will you take?"

"Junior Brother, whose advice will you take?"

Immortal Fairy Eternity and Yun Zhi both looked down at Lu Yang, who was sprawled on the ground, still unable to recover his breath.

Lu Yang looked up, his eyes glistening with tears, his face a picture of utter despair.

Can I choose neither?

Chapter 1013: 1012 A Beautiful Day Begins

"Well, I think I don’t really need to pursue the Art of Body Refining too much; I’m quite content with how I am now," Lu Yang whispered, shivering like a cold-stricken quail, unable to look directly into the eyes of his Eldest Senior Sister and Fairy.

It wasn’t a matter of not wanting to offend either of them, but rather that choosing either option would likely cost him his life.

However, it was clear that neither Fairy Eternity nor Yun Zhi had any intention of letting him off the hook.

"How can that be acceptable? The Art of Body Refining is extremely important for cultivators. Look at this Immortal’s own body—oh, I do not presently have a physical form, but when I did, why do you think I was able to dominate the ancient times?

It’s because this Immortal’s cultivation had no weaknesses, whether it was Divine Abilities, magic skills, or physical tempering, I had everything," Fairy Eternity spoke from her own experience.

Fairy Eternity made her case.

"The Fairy elder speaks sensible words," Yun Zhi nodded in agreement.

Lu Yang felt a tingling in his scalp, realizing that he had no way to bypass this challenge and would need to find another method.

"The Body Refining Technique that the Fairy and Eldest Senior Sister possess must certainly be among the top in the world. What I mean is, is there a way to combine these two techniques?"

"For instance, Eldest Senior Sister’s orthodox Body Refinement practices involve toughening the sinews and bones, breaking through the physical limits, whereas Fairy, you refine the body in a pill furnace to help me absorb the essence of medicinal herbs."

"We could have me absorb the essence of medicinal herbs through taking a medicinal bath, using the essence to break through my body’s limits, and supplement that with appropriate exercise to improve absorption efficiency."

Lu Yang remembered that when his Eldest Senior Sister first taught him cultivation, it involved training by day and soaking in a medicinal bath by night.

Combining the two methods, there would be no need for toughening sinews and bones, no pill furnace for body refining—just soaking in a medicinal bath would suffice!

Fairy Eternity gave a soft exclamation of surprise, her eyes brightening: "Combining the two methods? That’s an approach I hadn’t considered; it could be worth a try."

Eldest Senior Sister appeared thoughtful, persuaded by Lu Yang’s proposal: "To take the best of both worlds, it does sound like an effective plan."

...

A beautiful day began.

Lu Yang woke up from his bed and went to the back of Sky Gate Mountain, namely Great Snow Mountain, and with a heave, he carried it and started running laps around Sky Gate Mountain, quickly becoming so exhausted that he was gasping for breath.

Chasing Moon True Person, who lived on Great Snow Mountain, accompanied Lu Yang’s running, jostling up and down with the rhythm.

After finishing his laps, Lu Yang returned Great Snow Mountain to its original location and continued with his squats while carrying it.

"One, two, three... one thousand."

Lu Yang silently counted, completing a standard one thousand weighted squats. His warm-up was done.

He then tore open a Teleportation Talisman and with a flash before his eyes, the scenery changed; he found himself before the pitch-black waters of a sea where visibility was next to none.

This was the deepest part of the East Sea, where the water pressure was terrifyingly high, quickly crushing ordinary magic artifacts to thin sheets of metal, which was why a Water-Repelling Pearl was essential when coming to the East Sea.

But Lu Yang did not bring a Water-Repelling Pearl.

Standing there, he felt as though he was bound with thousands of his own weight, encountering immense resistance with every movement and requiring all his muscular strength to act, truly a struggle.

"Little Yang, keep it up; this Immortal believes in you," cheered Fairy Eternity, moving effortlessly and unaffected in that place, waving her flag enthusiastically in support of Lu Yang.

Lu Yang, fully immersed in his cultivation, assumed his stance and executed the Lu Family Arhat Fist, each move slow as if in a replay, extremely slow-motion.

After one set of Arhat Fist, Lu Yang was so tired he was panting, arms trembling, without the strength to even stand on the seabed; his body relaxed and he began to drift upwards.

Lu Yang floated from the depths of the East Sea all the way to the surface, the changing pressures during this journey causing him immense discomfort as if all his organs were crushed together, indistinguishable from one another.

He floated on the sea surface, drifting with the waves, and as the sea eagles circled above him, he shakily took out a Teleportation Talisman from his bosom and transported himself back to Sky Gate Mountain.

Puppets had long been waiting silently for Lu Yang’s return at Sky Gate Mountain.

Upon seeing Lu Yang teleported back, a Puppet stuffed a top-quality Great Recovery Pill into Lu Yang’s mouth, allowing him to regain his strength.

To assist in Lu Yang’s Body Refining, Eldest Senior Sister had cleared a place on Sky Gate Mountain and set up a Gravity Array. Once he had recovered his strength, Lu Yang entered the Array and felt his limbs as heavy as if they were filled with lead, unable to lift them.

In such a condition, he needed to wield the Green Peak Sword to hone his Swordsmanship.

Whenever Lu Yang could no longer swing his sword, a Puppet would appear and feed him Great Recovery Pills.

After an unknown number of repetitions, eventually, Lu Yang took Great Recovery Pills without any effect, powerless, he closed his eyes and collapsed to the ground with a thud.

Lu Yang’s eyelids drooped, simply too tired to open his eyes.

Seeing this, the Puppet carried on with the procedure and carried Lu Yang to the Fairy’s kitchen.

By this time, Immortal Fairy had already finished preparations, with the Immortal Fire burning vigorously, its intense heat felt even from a distance.

The Puppet opened the furnace door, and a wave of hot air rushed out.

Hoisting Lu Yang by his legs, the Puppet stuffed him inside, and throughout the process, Lu Yang didn’t even have the energy to struggle or resist.

Various aged herbal medicines, under Immortal Fairy’s control, flew into the Pill Furnace and refined into Spiritual Liquid, dripping onto Lu Yang’s body.

"All set, the rest depends on how well Little Yang absorbs it," said Immortal Fairy gleefully, clapping her hands, her task for the day successfully completed, and now it was just a matter of waiting for Little Yang to "come out of the pot."

"In the morning, he trains his body to break through its limits, and in the afternoon, he is put into the Pill Furnace to absorb the medicinal properties. He not only absorbs the properties but also replenishes what his body lacks."

"Little Yang is smart indeed to think of combining my method with Miss Yun’s. Why didn’t I think of this before?"

On the basis of Lu Yang’s proposed method, Immortal Fairy had made improvements, allowing her and Yun Zhi’s methods to be more effectively combined, and the results were significant.

"It seems Little Yang is quite adapted to this method now. He used to cry out when entering the furnace, but now he doesn’t make a sound."

Lu Yang’s face was against the floor, lying at the bottom of the Pill Furnace, closest to the Immortal Fire.

He felt an excruciating pain, and he could even smell the scent of charred flesh.

Lu Yang had the intention to cry out in pain, but he was too exhausted to even summon the energy to scream, leaving him to the mercy of the furnace’s roasting.

"Fairy, this isn’t the combination method that I wanted..."

After a while, seeing that Lu Yang was roasted enough, the Puppet opened the furnace door and pulled him out.

Unlike the previous time when the process was interrupted halfway by Yun Zhi, this time, after an afternoon of roasting, Lu Yang’s body underwent a transformation from charred to the growth of new skin, emerging clean and white, exuding a medicinal fragrance.

The Puppet carried Lu Yang back to the Abode, allowing him to rest for a good night.

In his sleep, nightmares struck, and Heavenly Venerate Tofu reappeared, cheering on Lu Yang, hoping he would persevere.

...

A beautiful day began.

Lu Yang woke up from the bed, his eyes devoid of any brightness.

Chapter 1014: 113: The Road to Wealth for the Qiongqi Clan

Lu Yang staggered like a walking corpse, struggling on the verge of life and death.

A hundred and ten days had passed, his virtues had reached consummation, and he had achieved some success in body refinement, temporarily escaping from the bitter sea.

The moment he realized that the cultivation ended, Lu Yang looked bewildered and fell asleep instantly, sleeping continuously for three days.

Upon awakening, he saw Fairy Eternity.

"Congratulations, congratulations," Fairy Eternity clapped squawkily.

At that moment, Lu Yang’s body was enshrouded in a hazy golden light, impervious to ordinary attacks. This Protective Golden Light was a symbol of ultimate body refinement.

No candles were lit in the abode; it was pitch-black inside. With the Protective Golden Light, Lu Yang was as conspicuous as a little golden statue. During the three days of Lu Yang’s sleep, he had been shining brightly continuously.

The Protective Golden Light was part of Lu Yang, and even without being taught, he could manage it freely. The first thing he did upon waking was to quickly retract the light—it was too dazzling.

"It’s good I brought back Tang Chuanwu. If he had performed Time Retrospection again, who knows what might have happened."

Though these days didn’t feel different from having experienced Time Retrospection.

Lu Yang lay in bed, his mind blank, feeling incredibly happy not having to endure weight training or being stuffed into an alchemy furnace.

He clasped and then loosened his hand, feeling the vigorous power contained within his body, a stark contrast to the past—so much so that if he could block out the incessant noise from Fairy Eternity, he could even hear the sound of his blood flowing.

"It wasn’t torment for nothing."

Despite the enormous gains, Lu Yang was not keen on experiencing the torment of body refinement by Fairy Eternity and Eldest Senior Sister again.

It was hardly a life for anyone.

"Second-in-command, there is someone with the last name Ao at the gate of the sect looking for you," Yun Mengmeng knocked on Lu Yang’s abode door and shouted, relaying the message from the discipled guarding the sect’s entrance.

"Someone with the surname Ao?" Lu Yang stepped out of the abode, somewhat puzzled.

Wasn’t that Ao Ling? What did she want with him?

Lu Yang exited the sect and saw three unfamiliar women.

"Senior Brother Lu, don’t you recognize us?" one of the women teased.

"It’s Junior Sister...Senior Ao Ling, ah," Lu Yang suddenly realized; these three were clearly Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi in disguise.

As for the third person, judging by her stature, it must be Jin Caiwei.

The relationship between Ao Ling’s trio and Lu Yang was confidential; even within the Seek Dao Sect, only a very few knew about it. To avoid trouble, they had come to see Lu Yang in disguise.

"Please, come inside quickly." Lu Yang hastily invited the three women in.

They were three Semi-Immortals, and both ethically and logically, he couldn’t let them stay outside.

The three women followed Lu Yang into the Seek Dao Sect and headed towards Sky Gate Mountain, where they removed their disguises to reveal their stunningly beautiful true faces.

"What brings you here?" Lu Yang smiled, pouring tea and water for the three women.

"Enlightenment Tea?" Jin Caiwei displayed a look of astonishment; she hadn’t tasted such a vintage of Enlightenment Tea in a long time.

"It’s Wei who wanted to see you," Ao Ling smiled, as she and Jiang Lianyi were just passing by.

"Senior Wei," Lu Yang looked at Jin Caiwei somewhat surprised.

Jin Caiwei sat in the Emperor’s Chair, her feet not touching the ground, holding her teacup and feeling embarrassed under Lu Yang’s stare.

Jin Caiwei spoke softly with her head lowered, "I was wondering, the Oblivion Pill that has recently appeared on the market, does it have anything to do with you, Young Sect Master?"

Since purchasing candles to corner the market, the already impoverished Qiongqi Clan had become even poorer.

Jin Caiwei had accidentally learned that the Oblivion Pill, which had recently become very popular in Great Xia, originated from the Seek Dao Sect, and felt it might be related to Lu Yang, the Young Sect Master, hence she shamelessly came to seek cooperation.

In the agonizing past three months for Lu Yang, the Oblivion Pill experiment had succeeded, received approval from the Dynasty for sales within the boundaries of Great Xia, and was widely praised; describing it as earning a fortune daily would seem an understatement.

"Somewhat related, but all I did was provide a small idea," Lu Yang humbly said, having roughly guessed the purpose of this Emperor of Vast Wealth of the North Pole visiting him.

Upon hearing that it indeed had something to do with Lu Yang, Jin Caiwei’s eyes lit up, "Then, Young Sect Master, could you think of a way to let our Qiongqi Clan become the agent for selling elixirs in the Demon Territory?"

If they could become the agents in the Demon Territory, their Qiongqi Clan would certainly not be so poor anymore.

Lu Yang gently shook his head, "Senior Jin, you’ve forgotten, our relationship is supposed to be secret, unknown to the world. If the Sect were to give you the Qiongqi Clan the agency rights, it might let the observant guess our connection."

"I see..." Jin Caiwei, upon being refused by Lu Yang, was quite disheartened.

"However, Senior Jin, if you wish to make money, it’s not necessarily the only way through agency."

"What other methods are there?" Jin Caiwei suddenly looked up, disbelievingly staring at Lu Yang.

Lu Yang did not directly answer, but instead asked, "I’ve had an idea for a while, but never had the chance to confirm it with you, Senior. Could you possibly clear up my confusion?"

"What is it?"

"Since Senior’s Plane Dao Fruit Outline can compress three-dimensional objects into two dimensions, and turn two-dimensional objects into three-dimensional ones, have you ever tried, compressing what you see into two dimensions?"

"What do you mean?" Jin Caiwei did not understand.

Lu Yang, smiling, stood up and pointed towards the scenery of Seek Dao Sect, "Painters, upon seeing beautiful sceneries, will paint them as keepsakes. Could Senior mimic this act by compressing this beautiful scenery into two dimensions and imprinting it?"

Jin Caiwei seemed to understand, yet not quite, and she took out a piece of paper, activated the Plane Dao Fruit Outline, and imprinted the scenery of Seek Dao Sect onto the paper.

"Like this?"

Lu Yang picked up the paper, where the "painting" on it exactly matched the real scenery, and he defiantly said, "Exactly like this."

"Could Senior perhaps assign this ability to an object, like a telescope?"

"That’s a natural, easily done," Jin Caiwei nodded; it was barely a thought away.

Lu Yang’s smile grew broader, "Then, Senior could try assigning this ability to a telescope, and then sell it."

Jin Caiwei puzzled, "Would people buy such a thing?"

"Senior might try it out."

Seeing Jin Caiwei skeptical, Lu Yang took out a Recording Orb, "If Senior feels unsure, you could also try this."

"If Senior can turn two-dimensional objects into three-dimensional ones, could you make the scenes on the Recording Orb three-dimensional?"

Jin Caiwei took the Recording Orb somewhat uncertainly, she had never considered this before, "I’ll try."

She activated the Plane Dao Fruit Outline, and the Recording Orb projected a beam of light in which two lifelike figures were fighting.

"Is this my battle with Zhu Tian?" Jiang Lianyi saw herself in the Recording Orb.

Jiang Lianyi’s fight with Zhu Tian during the founding ceremony of the Demon Nation had been recorded by many and widely circulated; Lu Yang had also bought a copy of the Recording Orb as a souvenir.

"Senior could apply the ability of the Plane Dao Fruit Outline on the Recording Orb, making all the scenes played by the Recording Orbs three-dimensional. It should fetch a good price."

Chapter 1015: 1014: Was the Solar Lamp Your Idea, Young Sect Master?

"Can we really earn Spirit Stones this way?"

Jin Caiwei still couldn’t believe that her Dao Fruit Outline could actually make money, but seeing the Young Sect Master looking so confident, it seemed there might really be a viable plan.

"I dare say I’m quite confident in my ability to earn Spirit Stones," Lu Yang said with a reserved smile, having never worried about Spirit Stones before.

in order to convince Jin Caiwei, Lu Yang discussed successful case studies with her.

"I won’t even mention the patent fees for the Flying Car, which bring me a huge amount of Spirit Stones every year."

"For instance, our Heavenly Court Sect worked with the Jiuyou Sect to let the Jiuyou Sect host a barbecue restaurant once more, and we take sixty percent of this barbecue restaurant’s earnings."

"There’s also our collaboration with the Yaoyang Sect in developing solar energy products, the most famous of which is this solar lamp."

The initially skeptical Jin Caiwei suddenly looked like a tiger, her pupils turning vertical with two small fangs showing and the air around her rising as her hair fluttered up: "Was the solar lamp your idea, Young Sect Master?"

Lu Yang didn’t understand why Jin Caiwei reacted so strongly but still explained truthfully, "I mainly provided them with a direction, suggesting they develop solar lamps, solar water heaters, and such."

Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi, standing aside, barely held back their laughter.

It seems Senior Brother isn’t aware of how much Caiwei spent on candles.

"Second-in-command, your fruit platter has arrived," Yun Mengmeng interrupted Jin Caiwei’s emotional moment, carrying the fruit platter.

Yun Mengmeng, carrying the neatly cut fruit platter, curiously spied on who the guest of the Second-in-command was.

Were they Dragon Clan Ancestor Ao Ling and Phoenix Ancestor Jiang Lianyi?

Yes, the Second-in-command had mentioned that he established a Heavenly Court Sect organization, and Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi were both members.

"Second-in-command?" Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi, hearing Yun Mengmeng calling Lu Yang that, were a bit surprised, as not everyone could call Lu Yang Second-in-command.

"Right, let me introduce, this is our Immortal Lineage’s newly added Third Boss, Yun Mengmeng. The former Third Boss was promoted to Fourth Housekeeper."

"Immortal Lineage’s Third Boss?" Jin Caiwei had no reaction when she heard Lu Yang’s introduction, but Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi were quite astonished.

"Yes, recruited by Immortal herself," said Immortal Fairy, floating out from Lu Yang’s body.

"This girl has condensed an Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit; I took a liking to her at first glance."

"She has an Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit?"

Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi were very curious about this Yun Mengmeng, as even during Ancient Times, only Immortal Sister possessed an Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, with all other Semi-Immortals having Dao Fruit Outlines related to immortality or invincibility.

They hadn’t expected to meet a Semi-Immortal with an Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit here.

"There’s no need to call us seniors; let’s interact as equals," Ao Ling and the other woman gestured, indicating that, whether in terms of status or cultivation level, Yun Mengmeng need not address them as seniors.

Scratching her chin, Immortal Fairy thought for a moment and then instructed, "Based on the time of entry, I taught Xiao Ling and Lianyi first, and this girl came later. Just call them Senior Sister."

Yun Mengmeng, obedient to the Chief Housekeeper, immediately corrected her address: "I’ve met Ao Senior Sister, Jiang Senior Sister."

Lu Yang looked at Immortal Fairy with astonishment; if it was based on the order of entry, then why had he become Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi’s Senior Brother?

Immortal, is your ranking system reliable?

"What is this Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit about, who are these two people?" Jin Caiwei looked around at the happily mingling crowd, feeling out of place as if she was the only stranger there.

Especially the woman who emerged from the Young Sect Master’s body, she felt like she had seen her in the Ancient Times, but upon closer reflection, could not recall any memories.

When they were in the Imperial City, whenever Fairy Eternity interacted with Jin Caiwei, she used Lu Yang’s body.

Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi looked at Jin Caiwei with half-smiles, "Little one, matters of the Ancient Times are very complex, many things not even your father is aware of."

"For instance, the existence of Immortal Sister."

Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi stood next to Fairy Eternity and introduced her solemnly, "Immortal Sister possesses the most powerful Dao Fruit, the Eternity Dao Fruit, which even makes our husbands envy her pure heart."

"She, she’s an Immortal?!" Jin Caiwei stuttered, unable to believe it.

Speaking of this, both women spoke with pride, "More than just an Immortal, Immortal Sister is the head of the Five Ancient Immortals, not even our husbands can match her."

"Not just in strength, but look at Immortal Sister’s perfect beauty, she also holds the title of the First Beauty of the Ancient Times."

"Alas, Immortal Sister was cruelly schemed against, someone erased her existence from everyone’s memory, that’s why you weren’t aware of her."

Now that Fairy Eternity had appeared before Jin Caiwei, it meant Jin Caiwei could know the stories about Fairy Eternity, and Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi could take this opportunity to thoroughly tell this Little Tiger about her.

Both women took turns and finished narrating the real stories of the Ancient Times regarding Fairy Eternity’s existence, and Jin Caiwei sat in the Emperor’s Chair for a long time, unable to pull herself out of thoughts.

Turns out her memories had been tampered with, what Sister Ao Ling and Sister Jiang Lianyi narrated was the truth.

"Wait, you just said she and my father are of the same generation, she even held me?"

"Doesn’t that mean I should address you as Immortal Ah..."

"Hmm?" Fairy Eternity raised an eyebrow, her gaze landing on Jin Caiwei with a hint of danger.

"Immortal Sister." Jin Caiwei quickly corrected herself.

Fairy Eternity then withdrew her dangerous gaze.

"By the way, senior predecessors, do you know anything about Time Retrospection?" Lu Yang remembered Tang Chuanwu’s situation and wanted to ask Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi for their opinions.

Tang Chuanwu’s issue was naturally also asked to Yingtian Immortal, but Yingtian Immortal also couldn’t understand why a user of the Time Dao Fruit would grant Tang Chuanwu this ability.

"Time Retrospection? A technique of the Time Immortal?"

"Yes. I recently went to Buddha Country, where I happened to meet a mortal who possessed the ability of Time Retrospection."

"Is that so?"

"And it’s strange, that person is just a regular human, he doesn’t even have a Spirit Root, and Fairy also said he doesn’t possess the Time Dao Fruit, it seems the user of Time Dao Fruit attached the ability of Time Retrospection to this mortal."

Since everyone present was trusted, Lu Yang described Tang Chuanwu’s situation in detail.

Ao Ling seemed to have thought of something, "The user of Time Dao Fruit didn’t leave any clues on Tang Chuanwu, which means he doesn’t want to be found."

"But he is making his presence known in this way."

"Is it possible that he is sending a message to a certain Immortal, perhaps telling that Immortal that he possesses the Time Dao Fruit now?"

"And that he and that Immortal are adversaries."

Chapter 1016: 1015: Immortal Lineage Battles the Four Imperial Protectors of the Heavenly Court

"Provocation, or something else?" Ao Ling’s words gave Lu Yang new insights.

"The fact that they dare not show themselves means that the user of the Time Dao Fruit is weaker than the Immortal receiving the message, and it’s very likely that the two are mortal enemies."

"Who could the Immortal receiving the message be? The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord?"

"The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and his cohort have always been collecting the Four Immortal Fruits to open the box of the world and venture into the outside world; among all the Immortals, the forces of Great Qian are probably the most eager to get their hands on the Time Dao Fruit."

"Or could it be that the user of the Time Dao Fruit is calling out to the mastermind behind the scenes?"

At this point, Lu Yang’s expression became gravely serious. If that were the case, then the identity of the Time Dao Fruit user was nearly revealed.

However, everything was just speculation, and there was no concrete evidence.

"Speaking of which, it has been quite a while since I tested my strength against the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit. Junior Sister Mengmeng, how about we spar a little?" Ao Ling looked at Yun Mengmeng, licked her lips, and her eyes showed her eagerness to try.

Ao Ling was a battle fanatic. Otherwise, she would not have condensed a Dao Fruit Outline that grows stronger with every hit she takes.

When Fairy Eternity was still a Semi-Immortal, Ao Ling had already sparred with her and knew well the power of the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit. Now that there was a suitable opponent, of course she wanted to test her strength.

"Shall we try?" Yun Mengmeng, with her thin skin, was too embarrassed to refuse Ao Ling’s request.

"Since you two want to fight, why don’t we invite one more person?" Immortal Fairy always loved a lively scene.

"Invite one more person?" Ao Ling wondered who else they should call.

"Wait up." Immortal Fairy concentrated, summoning Sha Meless, who was chanting Buddhist scriptures to the livestock at the slaughterhouse.

Sha Meless’s body flew uncontrollably towards Sky Gate Mountain, knowing that it was Chief Housekeeper summoning him.

"Chief Housekeeper."

"Let me introduce you. This is our Immortal Lineage’s Fourth Housekeeper," said Immortal Fairy to Jin Caiwei, the only person present who didn’t know Sha Meless.

"Fourth Housekeeper, after such a long contemplation on the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, you must have gained some insights," Immortal Fairy said, clearly valuing Sha Meless’s progress.

Hearing the high regard from the Chief Housekeeper, Sha Meless smiled sheepishly. Indeed, ever since he started receiving guidance from the Chief Housekeeper, he had practiced diligently, and his mastery over the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit had greatly improved.

"It’s just some cultivation observations," he modestly replied.

"Then it’s a perfect opportunity to show everyone. Go and practice with Lianyi," said the Chief Housekeeper.

"Oh," Sha Meless subconsciously nodded obediently, then suddenly realized what the Chief Housekeeper meant.

"Ah?"

Jiang Lianyi smiled. It had been a long time since she had faced off against the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit. Indeed, it was an opportunity.

"Look how perfect this is. We have the Immortal Lineage’s Third and Fourth Housekeepers facing off against the two Heavenly Kings of our Heavenly Court Sect," said Immortal Fairy, very pleased with her arrangement.

From the expressions of some people, Lu Yang sensed that the Fourth Housekeeper was not very satisfied with the arrangement of the Immortal Fairy.

"Come on, everyone, let’s fight inside Little Yang’s sword," said Immortal Fairy, playing the role of organizer and calling everyone together.

Battles between Semi-Immortals can only take place in locales like Small Worlds; a direct confrontation on Sky Gate Mountain would reduce it to flat land in moments.

Lu Yang took out the Green Peak Sword and invited everyone into the Small World.

The Small World within the Green Peak Sword was as lush and verdant as ever; the last owner, Wu Yao, was clearly an Immortal with taste.

"A Small World within the sword, eh?" Jin Caiwei had not expected Lu Yang to not only possess an Immortal Sword but for it to also contain a Small World. This was a portable Small World, far more valuable than those static Secret Realms.

And here she was, a Semi-Immortal with nothing to her name.

"Senior Sister Ao Ling, please enlighten me!" Yun Mengmeng bowed with clasped fists, her posture impeccable, a result of her grandmother’s teachings from a young age.

"Elder Lianyi, please enlighten me!" Sha Meless understood the ancient rites quite well and there was not a single fault to be found.

Boom——

Yun Mengmeng and Sha Meless heeded Fairy Eternity’s teachings and took the initiative right from the start of the battle by self-detonating, creating a massive impact. The entire Small World felt a slight tremor.

In the midst of the self-detonation’s smoke, the cries of dragons and phoenixes pierced through the heavens, dispelling the dense fog.

The black dragon and golden phoenix soared in the sky, like the embodiment of yin and yang, together in perfect harmony. It is said that the appearance of the dragon and phoenix brings auspiciousness, and this was truly such a moment.

Facing their Semi-Immortal opponents, Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi did not dare to delay and directly transformed into their Dragon Phoenix True Forms.

"Again!"

Facing Ao Ling’s True Dragon form, Yun Mengmeng showed no fear and self-detonated once more, not giving Ao Ling any time to catch his breath.

Sha Meless grew somewhat afraid when facing Jiang Lianyi. He still existed in ghostly form, without a physical body, while Jiang Lianyi, as an Ancient Ancestor of the Phoenix Tribe, was the most powerful being of the Phoenixes. With just a slight flap of her wings, endless seas of Nirvana True Flame would emerge, which was especially terrifying.

This was the pure Yang fire, his nemesis!

However, Sha Meless had no other choice; fleeing was out of the question.

Then fight!

Sha Meless’s gaze solidified, and his thoughts transformed in a mere instant, also beginning a series of continuous self-detonations!

Four Semi-Immortals engaged in intense battle within the Small World, causing it to quake like an earthquake. Rivers flowed backward, mountains shifted places, and Lu Yang watched as his well-kept Small World was quickly turned into ruins, which distressed him greatly.

At this moment, Lu Yang understood very well why Great Xia mandated the cleaning up of the aftermath of battles between Great Powers.

"Not bad, the fight." Fairy Eternity looked up at the four battling Semi-Immortals and seemed quite satisfied.

It is only through battle that one can gain experience and enhance the understanding of the Dao Fruit Outline.

"But it still feels like something’s missing." Fairy Eternity glanced and saw Jin Caiwei standing alone, having a sudden realization.

"I know, Little Tiger hasn’t found an opponent yet. Little Yang, why don’t you train with Little Tiger? Your Art of Body Refining just had some breakthroughs."

Now, there was one more person who wasn’t very satisfied with Fairy’s arrangement.

"Me?"

Jin Caiwei was still looking up at the sky watching the battle, and didn’t immediately grasp what Fairy Eternity meant. After realizing the great news, she couldn’t help but show a smile.

No, no, can’t smile.

Realizing she was smiling, she quickly forced herself to suppress the smile, not wanting to show her feelings too obviously.

"It would indeed be somewhat difficult for you alone, but you have Immortal me, right?"

"The two of us combined can’t beat a Little Tiger?"

Seeing Lu Yang still worried, Fairy Eternity had to analyze the forces of both sides for him to reassure him to fight with peace of mind.

"Look, isn’t our Immortal Lineage averaging Semi-Immortal strength? The three of them, as the Four Imperial Protectors of Heavenly Court, also average Semi-Immortal strength."

"It’s a battle of Semi-Immortal forces against Semi-Immortal forces, quite fair."

"Then Fairy, what can you do to help me?"

"I can wave flags and cheer for you, provide you with moral support!"

"..."

Fairy, I kindly resurrected you, why would you do this to me?

According to unofficial historical records, the Grand Minister Lu Yang, under the pretense that His Majesty was too young to understand court politics, held power over the imperial court, causing chaos in the government, and monopolizing authority. His Majesty, under the guise of leisure and play, ordered the three Great Generals to eliminate the Grand Minister and his affiliates.

Chapter 1017: 1016: The Emperor Leads the Battle Personally

"Young Sect Master, rest assured, I definitely won’t hold back."

Jin Caiwei said earnestly, signaling Lu Yang to relax, and then without giving him time to react, she charged at him.

"That’s good... Wait, what did you just say?" Lu Yang was about to praise Jin Caiwei for being sensible when he realized something was off about what she had said.

Excitedly, Jin Caiwei revealed her golden vertical pupils, and two white tiger ears sprouted from her head; she could no longer hold back her laughter, showing two small tiger teeth, like a hungry tiger pouncing on its prey, and with a roar, she lunged at Lu Yang.

Jin Caiwei’s attack was too fast for Lu Yang to anticipate, and he hastily lifted the Inheriting Shadow Sword to defend.

They were now in the small world of the Green Peak Sword, and Lu Yang couldn’t use the Green Peak Sword, so he had to use the Inheriting Shadow Sword.

"Hmm, blocked it?" Jin Caiwei was surprised, although she had used very little strength in that punch, it shouldn’t have been blockable by someone in the Divinity Transformation Stage.

Lu Yang’s arms tingled from the shock, and he stepped back twice to stabilize himself.

He had thought that Jin Caiwei’s strike would send him crashing into a tree.

"Lucky I practiced body refinement ah—"

As Lu Yang was reflecting, he saw Jin Caiwei rocket straight toward him, propelling herself forward with her legs and using her head to send Lu Yang flying.

Crack—Crack—

This time, just as Lu Yang had hoped, like a kite with a broken string, he crashed through several large trees before finally coming to a halt.

Now realizing what Lu Yang’s body could withstand, Jin Caiwei knew how much force to use in battle.

Lu Yang felt as if his body had fallen apart, but after a hundred days of body refinement, not only had his physical strength reached a new level, but his recovering ability had improved beyond what it had been before.

Without needing healing pills, his nearly disassembled body could quickly recover from the injuries!

With Lu Yang’s strong recovery ability, Jin Caiwei’s attacks did not cease, leaving him no time to catch his breath.

"Earth Shrinking!"

Lu Yang, taking advantage of being sent flying again, burrowed into the ground and emerged in another direction, keeping his distance from Jin Caiwei.

"County City in Palm!"

As Lu Yang’s cultivation level had improved, the previously Village in the Palm had become much more powerful!

A hand large as a county filled Jin Caiwei’s entire field of view, piercing through clouds and bearing down on her!

"Space-related divine abilities aren’t to be used like that."

Jin Caiwei roared into the sky, a tiger’s bellow shatter the space around them, and the County City in Palm disintegrated in mid-air before even touching her.

Lu Yang’s right hand trembled as he felt the terrifying aspect of Jin Caiwei’s roar, a mastery of spatial arts that could nullify such attacks.

Facing such a formidable enemy as Jin Caiwei, Lu Yang dared not hold back, unleashing all of his best moves.

"One Sword Becomes Ten Thousand!"

"Three Flavored True Fire, Golden Bird True Flame!"

"Tree Planting Spell, Primordial Spirit Leaves Body!"

"Sword Washing Pool!"

"Arhat... oh, I can’t use that one." Lu Yang promptly pulled back, not daring to use the Lu Family Arhat Fist against Jin Caiwei, lest it might lead to a pyrrhic victory.

Magic skills and swordsmanship moves were deployed in rapid succession, but facing a semi-immortal like Jin Caiwei, all such tactics were as ineffective as trying to shake a tree.

"Go Little Yang, go Little Yang!" Immortal Fairy lived up to her words, providing support for Lu Yang as promised.

As Lu Yang fought Jin Caiwei, with Immortal Fairy cheering in his spiritual space, it was like His Majesty leading troops into battle personally—an event significant enough to be recorded prominently in history books, and one can imagine how inspiring this scene must be for the soldiers.

Despite Lu Yang’s forming of factions, controlling the court, disrupting the political scene, and flattering those above while bullying those below, he still remained loyal to the Great Bean Dynasty and did not harbor any disloyal thoughts.

Seeing His Majesty hold him in such high regard, overwhelmed with tears and deeply inspired, Lu Yang mustered his strength in one effort, then declined in the next, and was exhausted in the third, being utterly defeated by Jin Caiwei.

Just as Jin Caiwei was about to make her next move, hearing Fairy Eternity cheer for the Young Sect Master, she suddenly felt something was amiss.

Jin Caiwei recollected various interactions with the Young Sect Master.

Back in the Imperial City, Lu Yang’s personality had been mercurial. Now, it seemed certain that Immortal Sister had possessed Lu Yang, taking over and returning at will.

Immortal Sister was the Chief Housekeeper of the Immortal Lineage. The Young Sect Master was the Second-in-command. Sisters Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi needed to call the Young Sect Master ’Senior Brother’, yet called Yun Mengmeng, the Third Boss, ’Junior Sister’.

The Young Sect Master did not show the respect one would ordinarily have for Immortals or superiors towards Immortal Sister; his attitude was quite casual.

Immortal Sister referred to the Young Sect Master as "Little Yang" and didn’t care about his attitude either.

Now she personally came forward to cheer for him.

The relationship between the Young Sect Master and Immortal Sister seemed not as simple as it appeared on the surface.

With this thought, Jin Caiwei hesitated, and her blows noticeably weakened.

Should she win this battle, or should she not?

"Ten Thousand Swords Returning to Origin!"

The Seven Star Sword Set revolved around the Inheriting Shadow Sword, merging into one blade, with the Sword Qi transforming into a hundred-yard-long surge. As soon as it appeared, it caused the space to tremble with formidable power.

"Ahhh—"

Jin Caiwei cried out faintly, was struck by the sword, flew backward, clutched her chest as if to vomit blood but could not, and turned her head slightly to leave her last words.

"Young Sect Master, what a skillful maneuver, you’ve won..."

Lu Yang was utterly confused.

Wait, how did I suddenly win? My sword stroke wouldn’t even kill Old Meng!

Lu Yang turned his head towards Fairy Eternity: "Fairy, did you do this?"

Fairy Eternity looked innocent: "I didn’t do anything."

"Strange."

Lu Yang couldn’t understand how the situation had suddenly reversed?

He sheathed the Inheriting Shadow Sword, pondering the gains from this battle: "Speaking of which, this battle was indeed very helpful. Elder Jin used the way of space to counter my County City in the Palm. By pondering on this method, I can greatly enhance its power, and I’ve also gained a lot from the magic skills and swordsmanship areas."

Summarizing after every battle helps to rapidly enhance one’s strength.

Hearing Lu Yang’s clear analysis, Fairy Eternity slapped her thigh and realized: "Ah yes, that was exactly my thought."

Fairy Eternity nodded repeatedly, very pleased with Lu Yang’s performance: "It seems, Little Yang, your comprehension is quite good, to grasp this layer of meaning from me."

"..."

Lu Yang had defeated Jin Caiwei with the ease of a breeze sweeping fallen leaves. However, a battle among four Semi-Immortals was still ongoing, sufficiently proving Lu Yang’s weight as the Second-in-command, indeed living up to his name.

Although Yun Mengmeng and Sha Meless lacked combat experience and cultivation level, falling behind in their fight against Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi, as carriers of the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, they could not lose easily.

Lu Yang silently prayed; no matter who wins or loses, I hope you four wrap it up soon. If this continues, my Small World will turn into ruins, and restoring it to its original state would be difficult.

In the sky above the Small World, a space fracture appeared, and Yun Zhi emerged, witnessing the intense combat of the Four Immortals.

"How did it come to this?"

Chapter 1018: 1017: The Demise of Bean Emperor, The Fall of the Dynasty

Yun Zhi had to return to her hometown for some matters, and when she returned to Heaven’s Gate Peak Mountain, she found it deserted, with only the Green Peak Sword planted in the ground, exuding an extraordinary aura.

She entered the small world of the Green Peak Sword and saw four Semi-Immortals fighting, all of whom she recognized.

Plus the small white tiger lying on the ground, so in total, were there five Semi-Immortals involved in the battle?

This scale of battle seemed to have only occurred during Ancient Times when Semi-Immortals roamed everywhere.

"Stop fighting!"

Fairy Eternity shouted, halting the battle among the four Semi-Immortals. With a formidable enemy ahead, it was not the time for infighting.

As the leader of two major forces, her word was law, and none dared disobey. Ao Ling, Jiang Lianyi, Yun Mengmeng, and Sha Meless, four great experts, simultaneously ceased fighting and stood behind Fairy Eternity.

Jin Caiwei also stopped playing dead and quickly got up to join the group.

At that moment, the herd mentality proved very effective.

Fairy Eternity spread her arms, with the five Semi-Immortals standing on either side of her, lined up in order.

This was the force she had painstakingly cultivated to overcome Yun Zhi!

Lu Yang turned his head and glanced up at Fairy Eternity floating behind him, then crouched down and stealthily moved to the least conspicuous position in the team.

"Miss Yun, bet you didn’t expect that, right beneath your eyes, I could amass such a considerable force!" Fairy Eternity proudly boasted to Yun Zhi about her power.

Aside from Emperor Xia, the Prime Minister of the Great Bean Dynasty, all the people from the Great Bean Dynasty were present.

All four members of the Immortal Lineage were there, among them Yun Mengmeng, who also held several positions, including Chief of Staff of the Great Bean Dynasty.

Actually, the Seek Dao Sect also had a faction opposing Yun Zhi—Little Medicinal Kings from the medicine garden; however, it was too late to call them now.

"Everyone attack together and let Miss Yun witness our capabilities!" Fairy Eternity commanded, and Yun Mengmeng made the first move.

"Watch out!"

Yun Mengmeng had never been so vigorous before, even during her battle with Ao Ling.

Yun Zhi’s eyes twitched, wondering why you were stirring up trouble here.

Initially hesitant and unsure whether to attack, Jin Caiwei saw that even Yun Mengmeng, who had just condensed the Dao Fruit Outline at the early stage of Tribulation, took action, so she figured it should be no problem for her to join in.

Besides, wasn’t Immortal Sister around to back them up?

"Roar—"

Jin Caiwei transformed into a majestic, enormous Skeleton Winged White Tiger, and upon seeing this, Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi, not to be outdone, also transformed into their Dragon Phoenix True Forms.

True Dragon, Phoenix, and Qiongqi—these three legendary immortal beasts, even during Ancient Times, had never joined forces against an enemy. Historically unprecedented, these three beasts were actually cooperating now!

Sha Meless looked gloomy, honestly going up to self-detonate as a warm-up.

Why was it that today he was not fighting Jiang Lianyi, then Yun Zhi? Being a Semi-Immortal, why had he only encountered such opponents ever since his debut, without a single win?

Speaking of which, the only time he had an advantage was during a fight with the second Chief of Staff from Great Yu.

"Charge now, this is the time when the dynasty needs you to charge into battle. Once we defeat Miss Yun, being promoted and ennobling you as nobles and ministers is not beyond us—"

Fairy Eternity stepped forward to inspire the troops, promising ranks and rich rewards.

Lu Yang felt that being promoted and ennobled with higher ranks and appointments as ministers had its difficulties; posthumous recognition was more likely.

The three Great Generals, invigorated, formed the Three Powers Formation. Ao Ling, having the strongest physique, charged at the forefront while Jiang Lianyi and Jin Caiwei flanked him, cooperating flawlessly.

Fairy Eternity exemplified a true monarch amongst rulers; unlike other monarchs who merely commanded armies, she personally participated in the battle against Yun Zhi.

Fairy Eternity’s decision was firm, and not even the senior imperial adviser Lu Yang’s persuasion could sway her.

As a close witness to the rise of the Great Bean Dynasty and designated as the next Bean Emperor, Lu Yang, seeing the decline of the Great Bean Dynasty, could not help but feel a sense of lament.

Lu Yang used the Heaven-Deceiving Life-Defying Technique and the Ground Shrinking Technique to bury himself in the ground, resolving to share fate with the Great Bean Dynasty.

...

Time flew by, and as swiftly as it came, the mighty river of the east washed away all heroes.

A pale hand emerged from the grave, and a young man climbed out, his eyes clouded with confusion.

"How long have I slept? What year is it now, and why am I here?"

He saw the ravaged small world around him, and the dead bodies of once-invincible acquaintances from various eras, their memories gradually reawakening.

"The founders of the Great Bean Dynasty, Fairy Eternity, the Dragon Clan Ancestor Ao Ling, the Phoenix Ancestor Jiang Lianyi... Have these awe-inspiring figures ultimately failed to defeat that unparalleled enemy?"

Lu Yang’s lips trembled, unable to believe the harsh reality before him, "The Semi-Immortals have fallen; why am I the only one alive?"

"What are you mumbling about, Second-in-command?" Fairy Eternity rose from the ground as if nothing had happened.

Ao Ling, Jiang Lianyi, and others also came back to life one after another.

"Oh, it’s nothing." Lu Yang regained his composure, as if he hadn’t been the one talking just a moment before.

Jin Caiwei was the most shocked of all, as the recent battle had been devastating, and they, the Semi-Immortals, couldn’t withstand even two moves from this person named Yun Zhi.

Who exactly is she?

"This is our Master of the Heavenly Court Sect." Seeing that Jin Caiwei didn’t recognize the Eldest Senior Sister, Lu Yang introduced her.

"She’s also a part of our Ancient Heavenly Court?" Jin Caiwei hadn’t expected that aside from the Immortal Sister, there would be such a venerable figure among their ranks.

"I brought a few peaches; let’s share them." Yun Zhi caused everyone to perk up as she unwrapped her bundle to reveal freshly picked Immortal Peaches from back home.

Originally, she had intended these for her Junior Brother after his Body Refinement, to celebrate his achievement with a few peaches.

Little did she expect that upon her return, she would find everyone in a huge melee in the Green Peak Sword’s Small World.

"These are Immortal Peaches!" Jin Caiwei’s eyes lit up; these were incredibly precious items, once tasted through the generosity of the Qilin Immortal.

Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi hadn’t tasted Immortal Peaches for a long time, and suddenly bringing them out made them quite nostalgic.

Only Yun Mengmeng behaved most indifferently, "Xiao Zhi, these peaches are...?"

Yun Zhi used a look to warn her close friend to be cautious with her words, "I grew them from peach pits."

"Ah? Oh right, that’s true."

"You can grow Immortal Peach trees from peach pits?" Fairy Eternity wondered, unaware of this possibility.

"You can," said Yun Zhi confidently.

Post-battle, everyone gathered to share the peaches, filling the air with the sweet scent of the fruit. The scene was warm and cozy, and Fairy Eternity, using Lu Yang’s body, also enjoyed an Immortal Peach.

"Delicious—it’s just like the old times."

"Although not as good as Immortal Sister’s cooking, it’s still quite delicious."

Except for Sha Meless who, being a Soul Body, missed out.

In the Spiritual Space, after tasting the Immortal Peach, Fairy Eternity remembered the total annihilation of the Great Bean Dynasty and sighed deeply.

"What’s wrong, Fairy?"

"Could I only resort to eating humans?"

Lu Yang was greatly startled, "Why would it come to consuming humans?"

"Isn’t it the story you told me, Little Yang, about an Emperor who, after being defeated and taken captive, dug out his own heart and liver to fight back and ultimately returned in triumph?"

"...Fairy, what I told you was about enduring hardships to exact revenge."

"That’s quite an elegant expression."

Chapter 1019: 1018: Dark Shadow

In an unknown land, towering palaces pierced the clouds, resembling the elusive Fairy Palace of legends.

Within the Fairy Palace, murals depicted bizarre events, massive giants riding divine oxen to plow the earth, saints splashing ink to paint among the stars, divine beings descending to dispel demons and bring peace to the world... Thirty thousand years had passed, yet the murals remained as vivid as if freshly painted, as if the painter had just left moments ago.

If any denizens of Great Qian were here, they would easily recognize these events as the legends passed down orally, and the protagonists of these legends were none other than the Heavenly God Immortals, renowned for their great virtues under the Great Qian Dynasty.

Among the murals, the most conspicuous depicted the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord standing above the ninth heaven, supporting the sun with one hand, the moon with the other, worshipped by the Heavenly God Immortals.

And the true Mid-heaven Emperor Lord still resided in this magnificent Fairy Palace.

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, lazily sitting on his throne made of Immortal Gold, supported his head with one hand.

This was an ancient tome, written in ancient scripts that few in the world could comprehend, save for beings like the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, born at the end of ancient times and thus most able to fully understand these ancient writings.

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord closed the book and icily surveyed the ministers below who had come to report, "So you say, you have still not found out who has been using the Time Dao Fruit to perform Time Retrospection?"

The ministers trembled, famous in their own right during the Great Qian Period, all Cultivators of the faith lineage, and facing the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, the ultimate embodiment of the Great Qian Dynasty’s faith, they felt an immense suppressing force.

"My lord, the tokens are rare, making it difficult to track that person’s whereabouts." One of the Semi-Immortals boldly stepped forward, speaking bitterly, as if finding the Four Ancient Immortals were so easy, they wouldn’t have failed to locate even one in the past three hundred thousand years.

"Hmph."

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord snorted coldly, frustrated that the clues to the Time Dao Fruit were right in front of him, yet he could not find it no matter what.

The Semi-Immortal felt no sensation from Time Retrospection, but he was vividly aware that, in the past half year alone, it had been used over a hundred times.
