This was outright provocation right in his face, and yet, he was powerless!

"Searching for the Four Immortals with a token is like looking for a needle in the sea. Does the Power of Faith have such a big impact on intelligence?" A sudden voice echoed in the Fairy Palace, causing everyone to panic.

"Who’s there!"

On hearing the voice, the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord sprang to his feet, his beard fluttering like an enraged True Dragon, revealing a massive golden Dharma Aspect holding a dark vortex, rendering the entire space exceedingly unstable, ready to explode at any moment, with time almost coming to a stop!

This was their Great Qian Dynasty’s secret location, known only to Semi-Immortals assigned by him over thirty thousand years, absolutely not a place outsiders could know, and even if an outsider wanted to perform a Soul Search, any memory regarding this place would be wiped clean!

"Heh, why such anger?" The voice remained vacuous and fearless, unaffected by the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord’s vigilant gaze or the slowing of time.

A figure shrouded in darkness abruptly appeared in the Fairy Palace; nobody noticed how he got there, the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord included.

It was like a fantasy.

"Who are you! How did you find this place!"

As the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord bellowed, murals around the dome of the Fairy Palace seemed to come to life, ready to step out from the walls and battle on all sides.

A youth dressed in ancient ceremonial robes appeared, holding a bone staff adorned with various symbolic ornaments.

Another Immortal of the Great Qian Dynasty, the Controller.

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and the Controller stood side by side, both Immortals warily observing the figure cloaked in darkness.

From the end of Ancient Times, the two had struggled, establishing the Great Qian Dynasty amidst ruins, became Immortals, achieved Dao Fruits, and had watched over the passage of 300,000 years, yet never before had they faced pressure like that brought by this shadowy figure.

Formidable enemy!

An absolute formidable enemy!

The dark figure chuckled lightly, indistinguishable as a man or a woman, "Who I am isn’t important. Would you prefer I fabricate a real identity to have someone chase you down?"

"As for how I found this place," although the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and The Controller could not make out the appearance of the dark figure, they could feel he was smiling as he spoke on this subject.

"I saw you carve out space and construct the Fairy Palace. How could I not know of this place?"

The two Immortals were shocked; they were Immortals when they had established this place, yet this person had observed them in secret without their knowledge!

"Swallow the Sky and Devour the Earth!"

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord suddenly attacked, his Emperor Dharma Aspect sweeping across thousands, leveling the world, a vortex devouring everything instantly swallowed the dark figure!

The saints and Immortals in the mural came to life, stepping out from the walls, each holding various gourds, stone cauldrons, Dao swords, and other precious Immortal Treasures, attacking the dark figure simultaneously.

This was a force capable of sweeping across directions, overwhelmingly powerful for the entire world. Now, it was all used to confront the dark figure.

The dark figure allowed various attacks to fall upon him, with the mural’s mystical beasts biting into his body.

His body shook slightly, and he directly sent the saints and Immortals flying back into the mural!

The originally magnificent mural turned blood red—these saints and Immortals were bleeding!

The dark figure, like a non-existent phantom, strode forward a few steps, ignoring the devouring vortex.

He didn’t care about the sudden violent acts of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and The Controller; he sat down on the ground as if he was in his own home, his posture relaxed.

The dark figure waved his hand, "Relax, we are not enemies. I’m not here to fight with you."

"Rather than that, seeking the Four Immortals, seizing the Dao Fruit, and opening this box world, from this perspective, is also beneficial for me. Perhaps I haven’t seen the real stars in a long time."

"What is your purpose here!"

Even though the dark figure clarified his stance, the two Immortals still dared not relax their vigilance. The other party’s strength and knowledge undoubtedly surpassed theirs—who exactly was he!

The Four Ancient Immortals? No, he himself said that seeking the Four Ancient Immortals in the Great Qian was beneficial for him. Could it be... the mysterious being who fought with the Four Ancient Immortals?!

"I just saw you seeking the Time Dao Fruit; you seemed to be struggling, and I happen to know something and thought to inform you," he said.

"What is it?"

"Recently, a newcomer has joined the Seek Dao Sect in Great Xia; his name is Tang Chuanwu. The retracement of time over this past half-year all stems from him."

"You would tell us this out of kindness?" the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord asked skeptically. Such valuable intelligence, would he really share it so readily?

"As I said, our interests in seeking the Four Immortals align," he explained.

The dark figure stretched lazily and disappeared before everyone’s eyes, leaving behind the gravely serious Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and The Controller, along with the Divine Monarchs staring at each other in disbelief.

Chapter 1020: 1119 Let me try

Mid-heaven Emperor Lord gazed at the spot where the dark shadow had disappeared, his brows furrowed in silence; it was a long while before he spoke.

"Eliminating one’s presence, could it be the Causality Dao Fruit?"

As far as he knew, the second Imperial Teacher of Great Yu possessed the outline of the Causality Dao Fruit, which enabled him to do similar things that other Semi-Immortals could not detect.

If the dark shadow possessed the Causality Dao Fruit, then everything that had just happened made sense.

"Causality Dao Fruit, indeed, that’s a possibility," The Controller nodded, agreeing with Mid-heaven Emperor Lord’s view, "By erasing causality, one can enter and exit any place unnoticed."

"What do you plan to do?" The Controller asked. This matter emitted an odd vibe, but then again, it was rare to get a lead on the Time Dao Fruit; it would be somewhat unreasonable not to try.

Mid-heaven Emperor Lord was silent for a moment, pondering for a while before finally making up his mind.

"I’ll give it a try."

...

"Chuanwu, Qiaoqiao, are you adapting well to life here?"

After bringing Tang Chuanwu back to the Seek Dao Sect, Lu Yang was either engrossed in fire Body Refinement, witnessing the rise and fall of the Great Bean Dynasty, serving as the mentor of fairies to enhance their precision of expression, and hardly had time to visit Tang Chuanwu and his sister.

Lu Yang had arranged for the siblings to stay in a vacant wooden cottage at the foot of a mountain, an area surrounded by beautiful, picturesque scenery.

When Tang Chuanwu, who was practicing boxing, saw the long-absent Master Lu appear, he wiped off his sweat with a towel, his expression excited.

"Thank you, Master Lu, for bringing me and my younger sister here; we’re really adapting well!"

"The masters here are all very kind, often assisting us and guiding me in boxing."

Tang Chuanwu’s boxing skills had advanced to a level that would be considered a martial arts Grandmaster in the external world.

He hadn’t expected the masters of the Seek Dao Sect to be so approachable, neither mysterious like the monks of Kaihuang Temple, nor as aloof as the other cultivators he had encountered, who behaved as though they were above everyone else.

Even a randomly selected master from the Seek Dao Sect might be stronger than Master Duan Chen.

And among them all, Master Lu was undoubtedly the strongest.

Tang Chuanwu had listened to other masters of the Seek Dao Sect talk about Master Lu’s legendary experiences and aspired to be like him, such as his challenges during the Foundation Establishment stage where he defeated cultivators in the Divine Transformation Stage, achieving something unprecedented in history that even the geniuses of past generations could not accomplish.

Moreover, Master Lu had made outstanding contributions to the sect, served as the Acting Sect Master of Seek Dao Sect for a day, and after his term ended, stood on the platform accepting challenges from the masters but remained undefeated throughout.

Master Lu’s cooking skills were also exceptional; having cooked for Qiu Jin’an, the Sect Leader of the Five Elements Sect, Qiu Jin’an was so excited after tasting the food that he pounded on the table, asking Master Lu how the dish was prepared...

In reality, Master Lu was even more outstanding than the descriptions in storytelling.

"Master Lu has come?"

Upon hearing the noise outside the house, Tang Qiaoqiao, holding a spatula, ran out.

Although no one had explicitly told her why she had come to the Seek Dao Sect, she had guessed, from the words of her brother and Master Lu, that some unknown changes had occurred to her brother, and they had brought him to the Seek Dao Sect to protect him.

"Qiaoqiao, how is life for you here?"

"Life is very good!"

Tang Qiaoqiao vigorously nodded; this place was a sanctuary that ordinary people could only dream of, and her brother was here too, what else could be lacking?

Moreover, since her brother came here, he was no longer always so suspicious.

"If you’re used to living here, that’s good."

"You have the Three Spirit Roots, which is sufficient to meet our sect’s threshold for joining. If you can pass the entrance test, you can officially become a member of our sect."

Although Tang Qiaoqiao was a clone cultivated by the Left Protector of the Skull Divine Sect and could progress much faster in cultivation than ordinary cultivators, such an identity held no standing in the Seek Dao Sect. Anyone’s background could be more bizarre than Tang Qiaoqiao’s, and no one would care about this aspect of her identity.

"As for you, Chuanwu..."

Seeing Master Lu mention him, Tang Chuanwu’s expression fell. After arriving at the Seek Dao Sect, he had tested for Spirit Root, only to find that he had none and could not cultivate.

Seeing Tang Chuanwu like this, how could Lu Yang not know what Tang Chuanwu was worried about? He smiled and said, "Leave the cultivation matter to me, I will find you some elixirs and spiritual objects, enough to cultivate a Spirit Root."

Cultivators could employ means to implant Spirit Roots in mortals, but the cost was exorbitant, far beyond what a low level cultivator could afford.

"Really?" Tang Chuanwu suddenly looked up, his eyes bright.

"Of course, it’s true. When have I ever lied to you?"

"However, you are not allowed to use your abilities during the entry test," Lu Yang cautioned. Time had reversed twice, and no matter how the sect set the entry test, Tang Chuanwu would definitely pass, which would be cheating.

"I won’t!"

"Someone is coming to our sect, and they bear ill intentions," Immortal Fairy suddenly warned, pointing upwards, signaling Lu Yang to look at the sky.

"Who is it?"

Lu Yang was puzzled. Who would be tired of living, daring to cause trouble at the Seek Dao Sect? Even if someone did come, they should choose a time when Eldest Senior Sister was not around.

Following the direction the Fairy was pointing, Lu Yang looked up. He saw an emperor’s silhouette bathed in golden light abruptly appearing above the Seek Dao Sect, emanating boundless majesty. The entire Seek Dao Sect was plunged into a terrifying quagmire.

"Mid-heaven Emperor Lord?!"

Lu Yang recognized the visitor. Although the portrait of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord hadn’t been passed down to later generations, the Ying Tian Immortal had seen him, and Lu Yang knew what the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord looked like from the descriptions of Ying Tian Immortal.

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, founder of the Great Qian Dynasty, held a naturally majestic presence, commanding respect with every gesture. Beneath his imperial might, the common people trembled involuntarily, unable to harbor thoughts of resistance, only able to offer their deepest reverence. Lu Yang had only experienced a similar feeling from the Ying Tian Immortal.

"Why on earth would the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord come here? And so openly?"

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord held a gourd entwined with purple and golden colors, uncorked it, aimed it at the Seek Dao Sect, and declared aloud:

"Tang..."

He had barely uttered "Tang" when a white streak of light flashed atop Sky Gate Mountain, shooting straight into the sky. Accompanied by a jingling sound, it quickly struck the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord’s head, smashing it downwards before he could react.

Boom—

Then it quieted down, as if nothing had happened at all.

Tang Qiaoqiao blinked, not understanding what had just occurred.

"Oh, the pan is going to burn."

She quickly ran back to the kitchen with her spatula.

...

In an unknown place, the Great Qian Immortal Palace.

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord coughed lightly twice and opened his eyes, breaking off the connection with his clone.

Seeing this, The Controller quickly asked, "How did it go?"

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord turned to look at The Controller, his eyes as imposing as ever, a gaze only someone accustomed to power could possess. After a long silence, he slowly spoke:

"I tried, but I couldn’t win."

"Also, I’ve been thinking, I was too hasty in searching for the Time Dao Fruit earlier. There’s no need to rush the search for the Time Dao Fruit."

Chapter 1021: 1020

When Lu Yang rushed back to Sky Gate Mountain, he saw Eldest Senior Sister holding Mid-heaven Emperor Lord by the throat with one hand while her fist greeted his face.

Mid-heaven Emperor Lord’s head tilted to the side, and he became motionless.

"Is he dead?!"

Lu Yang was shocked; Mid-heaven Emperor Lord had made his entrance like an emperor who consumed the heavens and swept through the eight wastelands, so how had he fallen so quickly?

"It seems it’s just a clone."

Fairy Eternity said after taking a look at Mid-heaven Emperor Lord.

"But it can still be useful." Yun Zhi’s hands formed a gesture, and using some unknown magic skill, a red line appeared on the clone of Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, stretching out towards the unknown distance.

"I’m stepping out for a bit."

Leaving these words behind, Eldest Senior Sister vanished, leaving only an Illusory Shadow in her place.

"What is this?"

"Miss Yun is reversing causality, attempting to use this opportunity to calculate the location of this person," explained Fairy Eternity. Although the clone was activated by the main body and the connection was severed immediately, traces were still left behind.

An hour passed, and Yun Zhi flew back with three Mid-heaven Emperor Lords.

"Why so many?"

Lu Yang thought this was like wholesale.

Placing these Mid-heaven Emperor Lords down, Yun Zhi shook her head in disappointment: "He was not controlling this clone with his main body, but through his main body controlling a clone, which then controlled another, and so on, until the last clone came to our sect."

"I pursued three clones in a row, but the connection beyond that was too weak; I couldn’t reverse-engineer the subsequent clones."

"Nevertheless, it’s not entirely fruitless. Based on the Soul Search results of these four clones, I’ve learned a few things."

"What things?"

Yun Zhi informed Lu Yang and Fairy Eternity about the dark shadow that invaded the Fairy Palace and about the leaked information regarding Tang Chuanwu.

Lu Yang roughly unraveled the sequence of events behind this incident.

"So, Mid-heaven Emperor Lord realized that the dark shadow wanted to use him as a test, and since he dared not come in person, he sent a clone instead. If the clone could capture Tang Chuanwu, it would mean the dark shadow didn’t betray him, and all would be well."

"If the clone fell into our sect’s hands, then it would relay this part of its memory to us as revenge against the dark shadow?"

"It seems the dark shadow is the mastermind."

"But the question remains, how did the mastermind learn about Tang Chuanwu’s situation?"

Lu Yang furrowed his brow; he had discovered Tang Chuanwu by chance. Could it be that the mastermind also stumbled upon him accidentally?

"Did the mastermind know about Tang Chuanwu all along, secretly observing and looking for clues about the user of the Time Dao Fruit?"

"Or did he just happen to discover Tang Chuanwu too?"

After pondering for a moment, Yun Zhi speculated, "It might not be entirely coincidental."

"Perhaps it was the battle between Grey Immortal and Meng Junzi that drew the mastermind’s attention."

"Subsequently, the mastermind stayed in Buddha Country temporarily and just happened to notice something unusual about Kaihuang City."

"Don’t forget, in the sixth reincarnation, you, Little Junior Brother, and Junior Brother Meng attracted the Vice Sect Hierarch of the Ruthless Sect after fighting with their two Elders."

"In the eighth reincarnation, you, Little Junior Brother, and Junior Brother Meng chose not to fight with the Ruthless Sect Elders but had Junior Brother Dai engage in battle instead. That’s the difference."

"Junior Brother Xiao Yang, you’re unaffected by reincarnation because of the presence of Fairy Senior, but the behind-the-scenes antagonist may not be aware of this."

"Later, the behind-the-scenes antagonist traced back to Tang Chuanwu through what happened in Kaihuang City and, out of caution, didn’t make a move. Instead, he informed the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, hoping he would test the waters."

Yun Zhi glanced at the four clones still lying on the ground, continuing, "Out of caution, the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord really only made a tentative move."

As the designated hours arrived, the four clones gradually dissipated, leaving nothing behind, not even suitable for Refining."

"Or it could be that he could use the Causality Dao Fruit to ascertain Tang Chuanwu’s situation, like knowing that the more people who are aware of Tang Chuanwu’s Time Retrospection, the stronger the causality becomes, allowing the behind-the-scenes antagonist to sense Tang Chuanwu through the Dao Fruit."

As the Fire Dynasty fell, Left Historian Fairy likewise disappeared. The Insight Dao Fruit has no combat power, but it’s very useful for finding people."

The three of them thought for a long time but couldn’t be sure how exactly the behind-the-scenes antagonist had found Tang Chuanwu.

"Don’t let Tang Chuanwu leave the Sect for now."

This incident means that not only the Great Qian Dynasty but also the behind-the-scenes antagonist are aware of Tang Chuanwu’s existence, which is more dangerous than Li Haoran’s situation. Leaving the Seek Dao Sect, he could be captured the next moment."

...

"Wuhu, New Year is coming."

Inside the Spiritual Space, Fairy Eternity tore down the Imperial Palace and built a small wooden hut. She put up couplets, wrote the character for ’fortune,’ hung up the big red lanterns, and, dressed in bright red clothes adorned with festive decorations, everything exuded celebration.

For cultivators with extended lifespans, it is customary to celebrate once every ten years, and Seek Dao Sect was no exception.

Not just in Lu Yang’s Spiritual Space, but the entire Seek Dao Sect was filled with joyous vibes, with big red decorations visible everywhere. Everyone was dressed in new clothes, and Senior Brothers and Sisters who were out on missions all returned. No one would take on missions this month, instead, whenever they met, they would grin and greet each other with a festive "Happy New Year."

Fairy Eternity spread out writing brush, ink, paper, and inkstone on the ground. She lay on the ground, kicking up her small feet, licked the writing brush to moisten its tip, dipped it in ink, and started writing the couplets with serious attention.

"Job well done!"

Having written the couplets, Fairy Eternity got herself covered in ink spots, with several streaks of ink accidentally marking her face.

Those who achieve great things are not bound by trifles. Fairy Eternity, being such a person, naturally did not care for these details.

She floated in the air to put up the couplets while Lu Yang stood at the doorway watching.

"Little Yang, take a look, are these couplets even?"

Lu Yang looked up, reading the couplet seriously.

First line: After this meal, there’s still the next

Second line: After today comes tomorrow

Title: Another year passes

"Great, Your Majesty has written a well-matched, naturally flowing couplet that can be passed down for generations."

"As the saying goes, food is paramount for the people. This first line’s focus on food reflects Your Majesty’s philosophy of valuing the people over the monarch. As for the second line, it seems extraordinarily plain and ordinary, yet it actually contains the oft-overlooked principle of time. Your Majesty’s mention will surely broaden the people’s understanding!"

"This title is a stroke of genius, adding yet another layer to the couplet, indescribably wondrous. Indeed, it is indescribably wondrous."

"Looking at the five years since the establishment of the Great Bean Dynasty, not one sovereign has had Your Majesty’s talent."

"Your Majesty possesses an abounding literary grace, serving as a role model for scholars the world over, truly admirable."

"Hehe, it’s not that good," said Fairy Eternity, scratching her head bashfully. She merely wanted to know if Little Yang thought the couplet was hung straight.

But Little Yang spoke his heartfelt words, and she couldn’t say it wasn’t her intention.

Chapter 1022: 1021: New Year’s Money

After dealing with Fairy Eternity, Lu Yang stepped out and ran into Yun Mengmeng.

"Second-in-command, happy New Year."

Having said that, Yun Mengmeng stretched out her hand and batted her eyelashes at Lu Yang, leaving him completely baffled.

"Second-in-command, it’s time for New Year’s money," Yun Mengmeng whispered, a bit shyly. It was rather awkward to bring up such a topic.

"Handing out red envelopes? Hold on, Sister Mengmeng, whether we consider cultivation level or age, shouldn’t it be you who gives me New Year’s money?"

"But you’re the Second-in-command, and I’m the Third Boss," Yun Mengmeng declared with righteous force.

"I..."

Yun Mengmeng’s words left Lu Yang speechless and with no other choice, he obediently handed over the New Year’s money.

It wasn’t much money; it was the thought that counted.

"Fourth Junior Brother, happy New Year."

Gan Tian Senior Sister also came over and gave Lu Yang New Year’s money.

"Wait a second, Little Sweet, try this," Yun Mengmeng handed Gan Tian a paper packet.

"What’s this?"

Gan Tian unwrapped the paper packet, which contained snacks like sunflower seeds, peanuts, and winter melon candy.

"Humph, I made sure to find out ahead of time that these are the foods one should eat for New Year’s." Yun Mengmeng, in preparation for these snacks, paid attention to whose sunflower seeds and peanuts were tasty, and learned to make them all by herself.

"Second-in-command, you have some too," Yun Mengmeng also gave Lu Yang a bag of snacks.

"Thank you."

Lu Yang ate a sunflower seed and felt the rich spiritual energy rush straight to his crown.

"Where did you get these sunflower seeds?" Lu Yang was shocked. Could such a tiny seed contain so much spiritual energy?

"Oh, I cleared out a spot in the medicinal herb garden to plant things like sunflowers and peanuts. I usually cultivate them with top-grade spirit stones and irrigate them with Taiyi True Water, and the Little Medicinal Kings help take care of them," said Yun Mengmeng casually.

She wasn’t the kind of cultivator who was lazy and ignorant about grains; on the contrary, she cared about food quality and believed that only the food she grew herself could be of the highest quality.

In fact, that was indeed the case; no one else’s sunflower seeds could surpass the quality of what Yun Mengmeng grew.

Using top-grade spirit stones to cultivate sunflowers—even Lu Yang, who was wealthy, trembled at the thought.

Is this how Eldest Senior Sister expected you to spend your pocket money?

"Speaking of tasty things, I have some too, grown by me. Consider it New Year’s money," said Immortal Fairy, noticing the impact of Yun Mengmeng’s sunflowers on Little Yang and not willing to let the Third Boss outshine her.

She magicked up a golden apple from thin air and presented it to Lu Yang: "Second-in-command, happy New Year, this is your New Year’s gift."

Immortal blessings from the Fairy, gifts from His Majesty, the Chief Housekeeper taking care of subordinates—this all made Lu Yang feel incredibly honored.

"Thank you, Fairy." Lu Yang accepted the apple and smelled a refreshing fragrance. Although it was grown by a fairy, since it was fruit, it should be fine, right?

"Since when did you start growing apples, Fairy?" Lu Yang was curious. It was one thing for Yun Mengmeng to grow sunflowers in the medicinal herb garden, which he just couldn’t see, but how could Immortal Fairy, who lived in a Spiritual Space, grow apples?

"What apple? This is the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit. Once you eat it, you’ll have the outline of an Immortal Dao Fruit," said Immortal Fairy with anticipation.

Lu Yang shuddered with fright, nearly dropping the delicate Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit.

Was obtaining the outline of an Immortal Dao Fruit truly this easy?

If word of this got out to the outside world, those at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage would go crazy for it.

"Fairy Eternity chuckled proudly, "Do you think just anyone can obtain the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit by eating it? Only you, Second-in-command, can."

"I’m not eating it."

Lu Yang had far-reaching ambitions; being a mere Semi-Immortal wasn’t his goal—he wanted to become an Immortal, and specifically, one that wasn’t constantly bullied by Fairy Eternity.

If he ate the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, wouldn’t he be at Fairy Eternity’s mercy for the rest of his life?

But once Fairy Eternity gave something away, there was no taking it back, and no matter what Lu Yang said, she wouldn’t take back the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit.

Lu Yang sighed and reluctantly accepted it for the time being.

"What about Eldest Senior Sister?"

"Xiao Zhi went back home. She said she’ll be back on the first day of the new year," Yun Mengmeng said while cracking sunflower seeds.

"Why didn’t you go back?"

"Oh, I’m planning to go back home on the first day too."

"Alright then."

"The emotions of the New Year may just inspire me to compose a new piece. I’ll take my leave now," Senior Sister Gan Tian didn’t forget about her cultivation even during the New Year’s celebration.

It was the first time for both Lu Yang and Yun Mengmeng to celebrate the new year at the Seek Dao Sect. They went down the mountain together and ran into Meng Jingzhou, who was out visiting people to wish them a happy new year.

Seeing Lu Yang, Meng Jingzhou’s eyes lit up generously, "Old Lu, happy new year, here’s your New Year’s Money."

"Get out of here!" Lu Yang laughed and feigned a kick in his direction, but Meng Jingzhou smoothly dodged it, having anticipated the move.

After joking around for a bit, Lu Yang asked, "Why didn’t you go home?"

Meng Jingzhou put away his New Year’s Money and rolled his eyes. "Isn’t it because after my family’s ancestor got beaten up in Buddha Country, he became superstitious and told me not to return, fearing it’d bring bad luck."

Lu Yang shook his head in disappointment, "Meng Junzi should be sensible at his age; how can he believe that stuff? He got beaten in Buddha Country simply because of bad luck, and somehow it seems like it’s our fault."

"Right," Meng Jingzhou agreed wholeheartedly; nothing really happened to them in Buddha Country, save for the arrival of the Vice Sect Hierarch from the Ruthless Sect at the Early Tribulation Stage, but that wasn’t a big deal.

Lu Yang also felt it wasn’t a serious affair but wanted to double-check, so he asked Yun Mengmeng, still cracking sunflower seeds, "Sister Mengmeng, would you say it’s a big deal if you encounter a cultivator at the Early Tribulation Stage in Buddha Country?"

Yun Mengmeng swallowed a salty seed and looked a bit puzzled, "That’s no big deal at all."

Seeing that Yun Mengmeng agreed with him, Lu Yang nodded. It seemed his understanding was on point, and he could only reassure Old Meng, "Elders can get a bit superstitious as they age; just understand that."

"Eh," Meng Jingzhou sighed, as a junior he could only comply with the elders’ wishes.

Man Gu had returned to his barbarian tribe to celebrate the new year and was not at the Seek Dao Sect.

Su Yiren and Qin Yanyan both came to the Seek Dao Sect to celebrate the new year, and Li Haoran’s family was happily making dumplings together.

To be precise, the entire family was researching how to create a magic artifact that could automatically make dumplings.

Seeing that Lu Yang and Yun Mengmeng came to visit, everyone greeted them warmly, but Lu Yang didn’t dare to stay for long, fearing that Fairy Eternity might get the idea to make dumplings as well.

Fortunately, Fairy Eternity didn’t have the terrifying thought of making dumplings on such a grand festive occasion; instead, she reminisced about an ancient tradition.

"Speaking of which, isn’t there a custom of hiding copper coins in dumplings during the new year? Whoever finds one is said to have good luck."

"I secretly placed a copper coin in one of the dumplings I made, saying I’d take care of whoever found it for the entire year."

"But it’s strange, the dumplings have all been eaten, and none of the four Yingtian Immortals found that copper coin."

Lu Yang thought to himself that no one would dare admit to finding it; even he would’ve swallowed the coin with gritted teeth.

"Do you think it’s possible that because my dumplings had Star Core filling, they mistook the copper coin for a Star Core and ate it?"

Chapter 1023: 1022: Traditions

Lu Yang had intended to take Yun Mengmeng to visit Tao Yaoye, but before they could ascend the mountain, they saw disciples from Dust Free Peak gathering at the base of the mountain, chattering lively among themselves, with Tao Yaoye among them.

"Junior Sister Tao, what are all of you doing at the base of the mountain?" Lu Yang asked, puzzled.

"Senior Brother Lu, happy New Year! And to you too, Senior Sister Yun, happy New Year! You see, the New Year is approaching, and we are doing a big clean-up."

"A big clean-up with you all standing at the base of the mountain?"

"Of course, it’s because Master is here," Tao Yaoye explained with a grin.

Suddenly, a powerful aura burst forth from the mountaintop, continuously expanding outward and stirring up countless dust particles.

"Look, Master is using her Pure Immortal Body to do the cleaning. She usually suppresses the Pure Domain, but now with the New Year clean-up, once the Pure Domain is unleashed, all the dust gets cleaned away."

"Now, the mountain is very clean."

This was the first time Lu Yang witnessed a Pure Immortal Body being used this way, and he found it extravagantly luxurious.

"Convenient, right?"

"Indeed convenient."

"The senior sisters say, every year our Dust Free Peak has the cleanest sanitation."

Lu Yang thought that made sense, as no other mountain peak had the Sixth Elder cleaning as thoroughly.

"Speaking of which, it seems like no one cleans at Sky Gate Mountain," Lu Yang suddenly recalled, realizing that cleanliness was indeed important during the New Year.

"There are people doing work." Fairy Eternity said, relieving Lu Yang of any burden to worry, telling him to just enjoy himself, "When we left Sky Gate Mountain, the Fourth Housekeeper led our sect’s Master and others like the skewer chef to do the cleaning. I don’t know if it was voluntary or if Miss Yun instructed them."

The Master of Eternity Sect, along with Master Liu, Master Gao, and others, were ghouls refined by the Eldest Senior Sister and were absolutely obedient to her.

"That’s great," Lu Yang said with a smile, relieved to have one less thing to worry about, now he could truly enjoy the New Year.

Pitter-patter, pitter-patter...

How could the New Year be without the sound of firecrackers? As New Year’s Eve approached, the festive atmosphere in the Seek Dao Sect intensified.

Some senior sisters, certain that the firecrackers couldn’t reach them, held two strings of firecrackers and circled around, not letting go even after both strings had finished.

It had been a long while since Lu Yang had set off firecrackers himself, and seeing this, he felt the urge to give it a try.

Whoosh—

A rocket whizzed past over Lu Yang’s head, bursting not far away.

Then came a series of "whoosh" sounds as countless rockets flew past, some from the left, some from the right, creating a continuous series of explosions.

Only then did Lu Yang realize that, unknowingly, many senior brothers and junior brothers had gathered at both sides, pulling out cannons from their storage rings, shooting at each other, the deafening explosions reverberated around them.

The rockets were of average power, only capable of killing a cultivator at the Qi Cultivation Stage, and in the Seek Dao Sect they could be casually used, not even damaging their clothes.

Some senior brothers, finding the cannons too slow, simply swung their arms and threw the rockets to the other side, who retaliated in the same way.

There were also senior sisters sitting on stools laden with countless rockets below them. A spark fell and ignited the pile, blasting them skywards.

Pitter-patter.

The stools exploded in the sky, resembling gorgeous fireworks.

"... How is this different from the firecrackers I remember?" Lu Yang said, his mouth twitching. This was no longer just setting off firecrackers; the Seek Dao Sect had almost turned into an arsenal.

"I actually think it’s quite alright," Yun Mengmeng laughed, her eyes reflecting the light of the fire, "It’s much less powerful than when I self-detonate."

"It would be a big problem if it were as powerful as your self-detonation, right?" Lu Yang muttered under his breath.

"Not at all," Fairy Eternity appeared and preached, "Back in the days during New Year’s, I often self-detonated a few times to liven up the atmosphere, and it eventually became a tradition."

"People worship me and want to imitate my grandeur, also trying to cause a stir during New Year’s. But they can’t create an uproar as big as my self-detonation, so they settle for setting off fireworks instead."

"So self-detonating during New Year’s is the most orthodox practice?!"

"Obviously, don’t you see the influence I have?" Fairy Eternity boasted, hands on hips.

"Considering how uneventful New Year’s seems here, why don’t I just self-detonate once to let you experience the tradition?"

"No need, no need," Lu Yang jumped in fright. If the Fairy self-detonated once, the whole Seek Dao Sect would be blasted into the sky.

Boom—

The sky resounded with thunderous booms, filled with dragon roars and lion’s roars.

Lu Yang looked up and saw that, to his surprise, his senior brothers and sisters were using the Art of Transformation to morph into dragons and lions, fiercely battling in the sky with fireworks serving as a backdrop. The scene was grand and beautiful, fit for a major final battle between the protagonist and antagonist at the end of a Dream Mirage.

"Is this how dragon and lion dances are performed during New Year’s?"

Lu Yang increasingly felt that this was completely different from the New Year’s he knew.

"Hey, is that also a lion?"

Lu Yang noticed among the group of dragons and lions, there was one lion that looked distinct: around twenty to thirty meters long, with a fearsome appearance, round rolling eyes, and red fur that resembled burning clouds, topped with a single horn, and exceptionally lively in its dragon and lion dance performances.

More accurately described, it was sticking out its tongue and shaking its head in a cheerful manner.

The demon beast resembling a lion noticed Lu Yang’s gaze, flew down towards him, and stared at Lu Yang, speaking human language.

"Can you see me?"

"Who are you?!" Lu Yang was instantly startled. Was he not supposed to see it?

"Well, isn’t this a Nian Beast?" Fairy Eternity recognized the demon beast’s origin instantly.

"Nian Beast? The one cultivated by Time Immortal with Time Dao Fruit?" Lu Yang remembered. Fairy Eternity had briefly mentioned Nian Beasts before, and Eldest Senior Sister had also talked about wanting to catch one but never succeeded.

"Right, it’s a demon beast created by Time Immortal, quite magical."

"This is a demon beast that lives within time, appearing only on New Year’s Eve each year, then leaping through time to the next New Year’s Eve and so on. That’s why the term ’passing the year’ came about."

"They are fond of red and enjoy lively atmospheres. Every year during civilian New Year celebrations, they join the festivities, hiding among them. This time, probably because our sect’s New Year celebration was louder than usual, it attracted one."

"Since their existence is related to Dao Fruit, only immortals can see them."

"So you’re a Nian Beast?" Having understood the origin of the demon beast in front of him, Lu Yang was no longer nervous.

"You’re an immortal, no, you’re only at the Divinity Transformation Stage!" The Nian Beast was shocked. Only immortals could see them, and this mortal was only at the Divinity Transformation Stage. How could he possibly see it and also know about their existence as Nian Beasts?

What is the Second-in-command doing?

Yun Mengmeng sensed something and turned to look at Lu Yang. She saw him talking as if he were speaking to something invisible, performing quite convincingly.

"Is the Second-in-command so childish? I stopped doing that when I was sixteen."

Chapter 1024: 1023: Nian Beast

"This mortal really is a bit odd," the Nian Beast said playfully. Being naturally curious and lively Demon Beasts, they wouldn’t otherwise mix in with the crowds and play every year.

With a roar, the Nian Beast lunged at Lu Yang, who swung his arm in a full circle and smashed it down. Since his physical body had improved, he found close combat quite amusing.

"I’m not afraid of you!"

The two wrestled together, with the Nian Beast overpowering Lu Yang and roaring in his face, dousing him with saliva.

"Three Flavored True Fire, ptui!"

Lu Yang had initially learned the Three Flavored True Fire to release it through his mouth and catch his enemy off guard, but he had never expected to use it on a Nian Beast.

The Nian Beast was singed by the Three Flavored True Fire, its mane shriveled and curled into tiny black dots. Seeing this, Lu Yang flipped over and gained the upper hand.

The fierce battle between man and beast was unpredictable, and the complex politics involved were truly astonishing.

That is, if you could see the Nian Beast.

"Second-in-command, why did you suddenly start rolling?" Yun Mengmeng asked in confusion. She heard that New Year’s Money also serves as Money to Suppress Evil Spirits, so could it be that Second-in-command didn’t get enough and got cursed?

"I’m fighting a Nian Beast; you can’t see it," Lu Yang replied, before being pinned down by the Nian Beast again.

"You’re supposed to be the legendary Nian Beast; can’t you use some other attack methods!" Lu Yang yelled angrily, feeling as primitive as a caveman.

"Lose the battle, not the war. You use a different method, and then I’ll use another!"

"Ptui!"

"Ptui!"

Neither wanted to admit being outmatched in close combat, resolutely refusing to use any other methods, simply competing in physical strength, power, and melee skills.

Seeing Lu Yang like this, Yun Mengmeng actually believed he wasn’t just fantasizing; he really was fighting an invisible enemy.

She tried to touch the Nian Beast by feeling, but her hand passed right through it, touching nothing.

Yun Mengmeng didn’t know that the Nian Beast, cultivated from the Time Dao Fruit, was inherently related to time, having completed a life transition to an entirely different level of existence.

"Second-in-command, do you need my help?"

"No need, I refuse to believe I can’t beat it!" Lu Yang’s veins bulged as he was determined to overpower the Nian Beast.

Immortal Fairy was hopping up and down in the Spiritual Space, providing Lu Yang with spiritual support, as fighting the Nian Beast had become a traditional New Year’s custom.

As Lu Yang and the Nian Beast were locked in a stalemate, suddenly, a line of fiery clouds appeared on the horizon, rapidly approaching the direction of the Seek Dao Sect.

As the fiery clouds came closer, Lu Yang realized that these were not clouds at all, but a flock of Nian Beasts!

The leader of the Nian Beasts was as large as a small hill, exuding a terrifying pressure as the Clan Leader of the Nian Beasts.

Facing the sight of the younger Nian Beast here, the Clan Leader’s expression drastically changed: "Chujie, what are you doing? Didn’t I tell you never to come to the Seek Dao Sect, our clan’s forbidden zone, because of the terrifying major demon!"

"Beware the major demon might suddenly appear and capture you!"

Seeing the Clan Leader leading the clan members, the younger Nian Beast no longer dared to frolic with Lu Yang, pushing him away reluctantly, "Clan Leader, I’m not a child anymore. Don’t think I don’t know, all this major demon stuff is a hoax—I’ve been here so long and haven’t seen any major demon!"

"Foolish!" the Nian Beast Clan Leader roared in anger. "I nearly didn’t escape when I encountered the major demon. Do you think you’d still be jumping around if you had seen her?"

The Nian Beast Clan Leader then bit the neck of the younger Nian Beast and flung it onto his back.

For some reason, though the Clan Leader didn’t specify, Lu Yang instinctively felt that the "major demon" he mentioned was the Eldest Senior Sister.

It was infuriating that he dared to slander the Eldest Senior Sister right in front of him.

Eldest Senior Sister was such a good person, teaching her own cultivation methods, and even rushing to help when facing danger. How could such a person be a major demon?

"Hm, you can see us?"

Only then did the Clan Leader of the Nian Beast notice Lu Yang’s presence. It lay on the ground, its huge brown pupils reflecting Lu Yang’s tiny figure.

"Interesting boy, could it be that you have been blessed by an Immortal?" The Clan Leader, having seen much, realized that indeed there were such mortals who could sense their presence—each one undoubtedly lucky, cherished by the Immortals.

"Is that a wooden plaque from our lord that you are wearing around your neck?" The Clan Leader felt a familiar power emanating from Lu Yang’s chest.

"Are you referring to this? It was given to me by someone from the Five Elements Sect." Lu Yang took out a wooden plaque, which was a part from the Firmament Tree.

"Our lord’s Immortal Corpse..." The Clan Leader looked at the wooden plaque, lost in thought.

It had learned from the previous Clan Leader where the Immortal Corpse of their lord was buried.

"Wait a moment, let me call someone over." Seeing that the Clan Leader had never seen the Time Immortal but felt something for a wooden plaque, Lu Yang directly brought over a confused Li Haoran.

"Look at him, do you feel anything familiar?"

The Clan Leader frowned, unsure why Lu Yang had brought this person over.

"What feeling could I possibly have? This person can’t even see us."

Lu Yang frowned. The Clan Leader felt absolutely nothing towards Li Haoran?

"Ah well, since you can see us and possess our lord’s wooden plaque, it seems it’s also fate. It’s rare for creatures to detect our presence, so let us take you for a ride!"

The Clan Leader stopped thinking about the Time Immortal. It wasn’t in their joyous Tnature as beings of the Nian species. Due to being on a different plane of existence, they couldn’t communicate with other beings. Meeting someone like Lu Yang, a fortunate soul, naturally meant they should treat him well.

"This..."

"Come now, this is a chance many people would envy."

Without allowing Lu Yang any objections, the Clan Leader bit Lu Yang’s collar and, in the same manner as it handled a little Nian Beast, tossed him onto its back.

The Nian Beast species, having basically never encountered a creature that could talk to them, were all very excited, crowding around and chattering endlessly.

"Brother, which Immortal did you encounter?"

"In what way did the Immortal take notice of you?"

"Did you see us last year?"

"How did you end up fighting with New Year’s Day, and who won?"

"Enough, enough, shut up, I’m going to start flying now!" the Clan Leader shouted, its form turning ethereal.

Lu Yang entered a mysterious state, facing an endless stream of water blowing against him, making it impossible for him to open his eyes.

He struggled to open one eye and realized he was in the midst of the stream of time, where the experiences of his past appeared like slides on either side—from joining the Seek Dao Sect, slaying the Tiger Demon during his Foundation Establishment Stage, infiltrating the Eternity Sect, reviving the Immortal Fairy, founding the Heavenly Court... up until tonight’s New Year’s Eve.

Just when Lu Yang thought it was going to end, more images continued to appear on both sides!

The images were extremely blurry, but Lu Yang could only make out one.

In the image, the world of the box slowly unfolded, revealing a boundless, shimmering starry sky.

Boom!

The Clan Leader carried Lu Yang as they burst out of the time stream, the surrounding images collapsing.

Chapter 1025: 1024

After breaking free from the torrent of time, Lu Yang found himself riding on the back of the Nian Beast Clan Leader, surrounded by fiery red clouds that matched the color of the Nian Beasts perfectly.

The Nian Beasts, wrapped in the fiery red clouds, shuttled and played around the world, invisible to all.

"Fairy, did you see the vision just now?" Lu Yang’s heart skipped a beat. In some time in the future, a box-world would open, meaning that the Dao Fruits of the Five Ancient Immortals had all been gathered, and the hidden enemy was vanquished— or could it be that the powers of Great Qian had gathered the Dao Fruits of the Five Ancient Immortals?

"Ah, what? What did you just say?" Fairy Eternity sounded as if she was still sleepy, her consciousness drifting.

"I was asking if you saw the vision just now?" Lu Yang didn’t think too much about it; the Fairy hadn’t been herself only since today.

"The vision just now was of me... wait, what did you see?" Fairy Eternity suddenly remembered what the Time Immortal had told her about observing the future; each person’s vision of the future might not be the same.

"It’s about the box-world opening up. Why, did we see something different?"

"The same, of course the same."

"Time Immortal once told me about this phenomenon, saying that if you’re caught in the torrent of time, you can see the past and the future. What has passed is certain, but the future is uncertain. The future we see is just one of many possibilities."

"So that’s how it is."

"So, did you see the future?" the Nian Beast Clan Leader carrying Lu Yang laughed.

"I’ve already told you, being able to see us Nian Beasts is a chance many can’t even beg for."

"So, what did you see, kid? Did you see yourself becoming a Great Cultivator, or did you envision a harem of wives?" The Nian Beast Clan Leader asked with a laugh, not out of curiosity, but merely finding the topic interesting. Lu Yang pondered for a moment; despite its fearsome appearance, the Clan Leader was a good beast. There was no need to hide this from the Nian Beast Clan Leader.

"I saw the box-world being opened."

No sooner had Lu Yang finished speaking than he felt the Nian Beast Clan Leader he was riding on abruptly stop, and he was flung forward by inertia, tumbling a couple of times before coming to rest in front of the Clan Leader.

"What did you say?!" The Nian Beast Clan Leader looked at Lu Yang in astonishment. Based on experience, the future seen by people who enter the torrent of time is always closely linked to themselves.

The other Nian Beasts also showed faces of disbelief at this news.

Although the future Lu Yang saw was only a possibility, even as a mere possibility, it was terrifying.

Could it be that the opening of the box-world was intertwined with this youngster’s fate?!

What did this youngster do in the future?

"The world was opened, is there a problem?" Lu Yang was utterly puzzled, not understanding why the Nian Beasts were reacting so strongly.

"Fairy, do you know the reason?"

"Not a clue." Fairy Eternity didn’t think there was anything to it. Little Yang had revived her first, then found the Yingtian Immortal, and who knows whom he might find next. Wasn’t it quite normal for Little Yang to share a causal connection with the opening of the box-world?

"Nothing, nothing at all." The Nian Beast Clan Leader, realizing his own overreaction, hastily reassured that everything was fine.

The Nian Beasts cherished their freedom above all else and dreaded nothing more than coming into contact with someone with significant causality like Lu Yang, lest they get entangled in that causality themselves.

"By the way, I forgot to ask earlier, are you from the Seek Dao Sect?"

"Yes."

"Then do you know Yun Zhi?"

"I do, she’s my Eldest Senior Sister."

"Hahaha, so that’s how it is, my apologies. It seems we’ve had a fateful encounter, we shall meet again!"

The Nian Beast Clan Leader no longer had the desire to carry Lu Yang around for fun. A person of great causality, and also the Junior Brother of a major demon—it was better to stay away from him!

The Nian Beasts, riding on the fiery clouds, swiftly fled, leaving Lu Yang alone where he stood.

"Wait, at least send me back first!" Lu Yang chased the fire-cloud riding Nian Beast as the Clan Leader flew with him for half a day. Where had he taken him?

However, even Yun Zhi couldn’t catch up with the time-traveling Nian Beast, how could Lu Yang possibly catch up?

Lu Yang pursued but shortly lost sight of the Nian Beast’s figure and had no choice but to give up.

"Speaking of which, where is this place?"

Lu Yang noticed a bustling city not far away, filled with fireworks bursting in the air in vibrant colors: swastika shapes, lotus shapes, and Buddha Beads shapes — all incredibly beautiful. From where Lu Yang stood, he could clearly hear the sounds of fireworks from the city.

The city was surrounded by boundless desert.

Lu Yang landed and approached the city, looking up to see two large characters engraved above the city gate.

Western Heaven.

...

"This is bad, the Second-in-command has been captured by the Nian Beast!" Yun Mengmeng saw the Second-in-command disappear before her eyes. Recalling that he had just boasted about fighting the Nian Beast, panic set in.

"Oh no, oh no, oh no... That’s right, seek help from Xiao Zhi."

With this thought, Yun Mengmeng flew out of Seek Dao Sect like the wind, leaving Li Haoran standing there, still clueless.

He hadn’t yet figured out why Lu Yang had brought him here.

"Senior Brother Lu has been captured by the Nian Beast, is that a New Year’s tradition or something?"

Worried about Lu Yangxin, Yun Mengmeng hurried away from Seek Dao Sect, heading straight for her old home, the Peach Blossom Secret Realm.

She flew over a lake and with a splash, she dived into the water, swimming straight to the bottom.

The Secret Realm sensed Yun Mengmeng’s arrival and a whirlpool appeared on the lake bottom, sucking her in.

Back home, Yun Mengmeng was greeted by the familiar scent of peach blossoms. The Peach Blossom Secret Realm was warm and welcoming, with peach trees blooming everywhere, fragrance hanging heavy in the air.

The rivers branched out like tree limbs, and the surface of the water emitted a dense fog of Spiritual Energy, maintaining the concentration. The entire Secret Realm’s concentration of Spiritual Energy was comparable to that of the Five Great Immortal Sects.

"Mengmeng." People of the Secret Realm smiled and waved at Yun Mengmeng, who were also celebrating New Year’s, frolicking and welcoming the new year.

"Senior Sister Nianger, where’s Xiao Zhi?" Yun Mengmeng asked urgently.

"Xiao Zhi? She should be with Granny. What’s the hurry, why do you need her?"

"It’s urgent, I’ll explain later."

When Yun Mengmeng found Yun Zhi, she was leisurely chatting with Granny, recounting tales of the outside world.

"Mengmeng is back? Come, sit by Granny," Granny beckoned with a smile.

Despite being called Granny, she looked remarkably young, around twenty-something, and was very composed in her actions.

"Hmm? Mengmeng, why are you here?" Yun Zhi asked as she set down her teacup, slightly puzzled.

"Happy New Year, Granny, I’ve got an emergency and can’t stay," Yun Mengmeng said.

"It’s serious, Xiao Zhi. The Second-in-command has been taken away by the Nian Beast!"

"Really? What happened?"

"Well, the Second-in-command and I were watching fireworks, and all of a sudden, he rolled on the ground with a ’clang’, then swiftly pinned something down, saying it was the Nian Beast. Afterwards, I don’t know how, but the Second-in-command jumped up and brought Li Haoran over, like he introduced him to someone. And then, ’whoosh’, as if something snatched him up, he just vanished."

Yun Zhi set down her teacup, ignoring the onomatopoeic words in Yun Mengmeng’s description, and thoughtfully said, "I see, so that’s what happened. No worries, it should be fine."

Chapter 1026: 1025: Forever in a Snap, Worldly Matters Turn to Smoke

"Why am I in Western Heaven during Chinese New Year?"

Lu Yang had a strange expression. Although he practiced cultivation, he wasn’t superstitious. However, the implications of his current situation were not very auspicious.

Initially, Lu Yang was startled by the words "Western Heaven," but then he understood. Here lay Western Heaven City, managed by the Western Paradise Temple, which held a status equivalent to that of Great Xia Imperial City.

"Does the Nian Beast run so fast? It brought me to Western Heaven City in an instant?" Lu Yang felt that he hadn’t been riding on the back of the Nian Beast Clan Leader for long.

Since it was rare for him to visit Western Heaven City during Chinese New Year, and considering his treatment in Kaihuang City, Lu Yang thought it wouldn’t hurt to stroll around Western Paradise Temple.

"Hello, I’d like to enter the city."

Even on New Year’s Eve, the gates of Western Heaven City were wide open, with monks guarding the gates.

Lu Yang handed his identity proof to the gatekeeping monks.

"Lu Yang?"

The monk was startled by the name on the certificate and then, upon seeing Lu Yang’s familiar face, became even more surprised.

Could this be the legendary Lu Yang?

Lu Yang entered the city without any hindrances.

"Leaving Buddha Country is just a hassle; I’ll have to sneak across the border."

Lu Yang sighed. There was no other way; he didn’t have a departure record, only an entry record. How could he explain that?

Even if he told the Great Xia officials that he was forcibly taken out of the country by the Nian Beast, probably no one would believe him except for Jiang Pingan.

"Western Heaven City is quite lively."

Lu Yang walked leisurely, taking in the sights. The New Year atmosphere here was much stronger than at the Seek Dao Sect; at least the fireworks here wouldn’t kill anyone.

Children could be seen crouching on the ground lighting fireworks everywhere. Lu Yang, who hadn’t played with them for a long time, felt the itch in his hands and squatted down to join the children in lighting fireworks.

Sometimes Lu Yang would find unlit fireworks amongst the debris, picking them up one by one, clutching them in his palm, walking along while lighting them with the Three Flavored True Fire and tossing them aside, much amused.

The dull sound of the bell tolled, a myriad of fireworks ascending into the sky and exploding simultaneously, scattering golden sparks as cheers and Buddhist chants arose steadily throughout the city.

The New Year had arrived.

Lu Yang followed the sound of the bell toward Western Paradise Temple, from which the ringing emanated.

The Spiritual Space was equally bustling with activity—lively music, the booming of drums, the crackle of firecrackers—while Immortal Fairy Eternity cheerfully said Happy New Year amidst the hubbub.

"Also, Second-in-command, you’ve been exposed, it’s time to prepare to run," Immortal Fairy Eternity kindly added.

"Ah? Fairy, what did you say? I can’t hear you clearly!" Lu Yang shouted; the Spiritual Space was incredibly noisy.

He vaguely heard Immortal Fairy Eternity saying something intermittently about "every house’s head, to run."

In reality, suddenly a Buddha Light enveloped an area, and then it shrank, covering only Lu Yang.

A monk clad in a kasaya chanted Amitabha and then addressed the Buddha Light-covered Lu Yang, "Lu Yang Benefactor graces us with his presence. Western Paradise Temple regrets not having welcomed you from afar; would you care to join this humble monk as our guest at the temple?"

He had received a message from the guard monks that Lu Yang, the genius of the Seek Dao Sect, was visiting Western Heaven City, and as an Elder of Western Paradise Temple, it was only natural for him to personally welcome Lu Yang.

Suddenly another monk flew in with an unfriendly expression: "Elder Kong Chan, you arrived quite fast."

"Junior Brother Kong Xing flatters me."

Elder Kong Xing sneered: "Hmph, don’t think I don’t know you’re trying to use Lu Yang to find out Ye Zijin’s whereabouts."

Elder Kong Chan replied unhurriedly, "Do you share the same thought, Junior Brother?"

"Elders Kong Chan, Kong Xing, what brings you here?" Another Elder of Western Paradise Temple flew in. While addressing them, his gaze kept drifting towards Lu Yang.

More and more Elders flew over from Western Paradise Temple, causing Lu Yang’s eyes to twitch.

"So, what if I said I don’t know where my Second Senior Brother is, would you believe me?"

"Hehe, since Lu Yang Benefactor has visited our Western Paradise Temple, we shall believe him."

Hearing this, Lu Yang took to his heels and ran. This group of Elders had clearly set their sights on the relationship between him and his Second Senior Brother, determined to acquire something from him.

Handling one Elder was manageable, but who could withstand a whole group of Elders? If he didn’t run now, it would be too late!

"Chase!"

As soon as the many Elders saw Lu Yang trying to flee, they couldn’t let that happen. Displaying their Divine Abilities, Buddha Light soared to the sky, Arhats chanted scriptures, Guanyin saved souls, Worldly Venerables enlightened... Miracles abounded, instantly illuminating Western Heaven City as bright as day, and even the King of Ghosts would be purified here.

The clueless citizens, witnessing this spectacle, all came out to worship, creating a commotion that was even livelier than the New Year celebrations with bell-ringing and fireworks.

Lu Yang used Earth Shrinking to burrow into the ground, fleeing in all directions, occasionally having to change his course to evade the encircling pursuit.

"Isn’t this New Year’s Eve a bit too eventful?"

He had simply wanted to live in peace and enjoy the New Year, but who would have thought he’d encounter the Nian Beast, get swept into the currents of time, and then bewilderingly arrive at Western Paradise Temple to be warmly entertained by a group of Elders?

In terms of speed among his peers, Lu Yang was unquestionably unmatched, even the Kunpeng Clan might not meet his pace.

But this was the Western Paradise Temple, where the skies were filled with Combined Great Abilities; even with the power of three heads and six arms, he couldn’t escape.

"This won’t do, I need to think of a plan."

Lu Yang had a flash of inspiration, flipped over the high wall, created a Bodhi Wood avatar to attract attention, while his true form hid in the Green Blade Sword Small World, dropping to the ground, camouflaging as an ordinary sword.

The design of the Green Peak Sword wasn’t particularly lavish, and no one would recognize the grade of the sword.

As Lu Yang expected, the Spiritual Sense of the Western Paradise Temple Elders swept over the area several times, yet no one took notice of the ordinary longsword lying on the ground, stained with soil.

"Hmm, why is there a sword here?" The Young Monk, after finishing in the restroom, accidentally noticed an ordinary longsword lying in the bushes.

"There’s no name on the sword, did a layperson drop it here?"

"But were there any laypeople at our Temple of Miscellaneous Affairs today?"

The Young Monk was puzzled; he didn’t know much about swords but knew that the owner would be anxious about losing their possession.

"I should hand it over to the Manager."

Upon hearing this, Lu Yang realized that in his haste to hide, he had not paid attention to his surroundings and had vaulted over the wall to hide within the sword—turns out he was at the Western Paradise Temple.

However, he couldn’t let the little monk hand him over to the Manager.

"Hehe, you have a kind heart for a little monk."

"Who’s there!" The Young Monk jumped with fright, apprehensive.

"Stop looking around; it is I who am speaking."

The Young Monk’s pupils shrank immediately, almost throwing the sword away in fear.

The sword was speaking!

"Who, who are you? I must tell you, this is Western Paradise Temple!"

"Hehe, how does that matter to me—can Western Paradise Temple do anything to me?"

"Who, who exactly are you?"

"I am Tofu Heavenly Venerable, have you, little monk, heard of my name?"

The Young Monk showed a puzzled expression.

Noticing the Young Monk’s expression, the consciousness within the sword felt somewhat desolate.

"Countless ages in the blink of an eye, the mortal world’s past turns to smoke. Did I, who once reigned supreme over the heavens, joyously soaring through the skies, befriending heaven and earth, shining alongside the sun and the moon, witness the eons, to now awaken and find that the people of later generations no longer know of me?"

Chapter 1027: 1026: The Mysterious Tofu Heavenly Venerate

"Tofu Heavenly Venerable?"

Upon hearing the entity within the sword recount its past, the young monk had quite a shock; this was indeed an extraordinary existence.

He racked his brains for a long time, but still could not think of any great power from ancient times who bore the name "Tofu."

"Ah, let it be, let it be, it seems even I cannot escape the fate of being nameless through the ages."

With a long sigh, Tofu Heavenly Venerable expressed either his lament for the heartlessness of time or his reminiscence of that carefree era.

"It’s just unfortunate that my involvement with causality is too profound. Should it surface today, I fear it might trigger a cataclysmic immortal battle, and this little Western Paradise Temple would be reduced to ruins."

"Then, what should we do?" The young monk had not expected that the sword he picked up at random would involve so many issues. He trembled, deeply afraid of triggering some significant causality.

"I currently dwell within this immortal sword to neutralize causality. As long as you, young monk, do not disclose anything about me, no trouble will occur."

"That’s good, that’s good. My lips are sealed; I definitely won’t speak of the senior’s matters," the young monk hurriedly said.

"That would be best."

After this, no more sounds came from the sword; whether Tofu Heavenly Venerable was too lazy to speak or had fallen asleep was unknown.

Knowing the origins of Tofu Heavenly Venerable, the young monk dared not neglect his duty nor entertain the thought of discarding the immortal sword. He carefully held the sword and tiptoed back to his meditation chamber.

The chamber was a large communal sleeping area for six or seven individuals. The young monk quietly found a clean, long piece of cloth, wrapped up the Green Peak Sword, placed it next to his pillow in his designated spot, and then went to sleep.

...

"Xin Ding, why did you take so long to go to the restroom last night?" Upon waking up in the morning, while the young monk was getting dressed, his companion inquired.

He had slept vaguely last night, could not remember how long the young monk had been gone to the restroom, but felt it was quite a while.

"Amitabha, monks don’t lie."

"So?"

"So I cannot tell." The young monk earnestly responded, knowing he couldn’t disclose the matters regarding Tofu Heavenly Venerable, as that could lead to the destruction of the Western Paradise Temple; he now bore the responsibility of saving it.

"Ah?"

Having woken up, put on his clothes, and eaten a light breakfast, the young monk attended morning classes to chant scriptures. Along with the other servant monks, he fetched water, chopped wood, and cleaned the grounds. After all that, he sat down to meditate.

"Young monk, are you engaged in visualization?" An ancient and muffled voice came from the immortal sword; Tofu Heavenly Venerable noticed that the young monk had a foundation in cultivation, roughly at the fifth level of Qi Refinement.

Before the young monk, there lay a yellowed Arhat Demon Suppression Map, which he was using for his visualization to stimulate his spiritual sense.

In the Cultivation World, the visualization method is commonly used to grow spiritual sense. Meng Jingzhou’s method of constantly suppressing his instincts to enhance spiritual sense was one used by very few.

The young monk was startled, looking around and realizing that whether it was Senior Brothers and Junior Brothers meditating, or the Deacon Master, none could hear the Venerate’s voice.

"Stop looking; this is a spiritual sense transmission, others can’t hear it."

Only then did the young monk reply, "Yes, Heavenly Venerate."

"This is the most basic visualization map. To obtain a better one, you need to contribute to the temple and exchange Merit Points to get it."

The young monk shyly smiled, "But this Arhat Demon Suppression Map is already very good, said to be handed down from Ancient Times."

Naturally, what the young monk held was not an original, but merely a copy of an ancient visualization map.

"These things are also worthy of being called visualization maps." Tofu Heavenly Venerable shook his head, clearly unimpressed by the young monk’s method.

"Since you’ve met me, it means you’re destined to be associated with me. I will impart to you a genuine ancient visualization map, lest in the future people say I’ve lost face."

Tofu Heavenly Venerable used his Spiritual Sense as a seal, imprinting an image within the young monk’s mind.

"What is this?!"

The young monk closed his eyes, and the imprint of Tofu Heavenly Venerable’s Spiritual Sense appeared in his mind.

It was a golden divine Dragon soaring between heaven and earth, a naturally supreme being, far more imposing than any depiction of a True Dragon the young monk had ever seen in books. With a furrow of its eyebrows, the divine Dragon summoned a sky full of Immortal Thunder, as if heaven and earth were altered by its will.

"This is one of the five founders of Buddhism, whom I had the fortune to meet several times. This visualization can be called the Yinglong Visualization."

One of the five founders of Buddhism!

The young monk heard these five significant words and was greatly shocked inside his heart. Weren’t the founders of Buddhism the Worldly Venerable? How did it become five people in the words of the Heavenly Venerable?!

The young monk contemplated Venerable Ying Long and found that the effect on the Spiritual Sense was far superior to the Arhat Demon Suppression Map!

Generally speaking, the more powerful the figure in the visualization and the greater their impact on the world, the better the effect of the visualization if it is related to the viewer’s Cultivation Technique.

Such good effects indicated that the True Dragon in the visualization was connected to Buddhist teaching, and its status within Buddhism was absolutely exalted!

Indeed one of the founders of Buddhism!

The young monk increasingly felt that Tofu Heavenly Venerable was profoundly enigmatic, as this casually given item had overturned his understanding.

"May I ask, Heavenly Venerable, what exactly is the matter with the five founders of Buddhism?"

"Your Cultivation Level is too low; it is not yet time for you to know the secrets of Ancient Times."

"Yes."

From the perspective of visualization methods, actually, Lu Yang was also engaging in visualization, except his object was the Immortal Fairy rather than any pictorial image.

It was akin to Lu Yang being in constant contemplation; naturally, his Spiritual Sense far surpassed his peers.

"Speaking of which, I still don’t know what this little monk is called."

"This humble monk is Xin Ding, a servant monk in the Servant Monks Institute."

"Subduing the mind and stabilizing the world, a fine dharma name."

With the mysterious Tofu Heavenly Venerable presenting an ancient visualization, the young monk Xin Ding felt his Spiritual Sense Cultivation advancing at an unprecedented rate, experiencing an exhilarating feeling like never before.

Several days passed, and Xin Ding’s Spiritual Sense grew increasingly powerful, transforming his essence, Qi, and spirit, although the other servant monks who interacted with him daily failed to notice this subtle change.

One day, as Xin Ding finished his contemplation and walked out of his Zen room, suddenly a stone flew at him, aiming directly at his forehead.

Xin Ding, sensing something, tilted his head and dodged the blow.

"Xin Ding Junior Brother, your skills seem quite impressive these days," several servant monks said tauntingly, with the leader still clutching several stones in his hand.

"So it is Xin Cheng Senior Brother." Xin Ding responded nonchalantly, as if unaware that Xin Cheng had intentionally thrown the stone at him, causing Xin Cheng to feel as if his punch had landed on cotton, unable to exert any force.

"It’s Xin Cheng, what is he doing here?"

"Don’t you know? The selection at the Martial Monk Institute is soon, and he is all over the place snatching Merit Points to exchange for items to prepare for the selection!"

"I also heard that he is acquainted with a Senior Brother from the Martial Monk Institute, who gifted him a Fragmented Map of ’Elephants Crossing the River’."

"What, it’s actually the Fragmented Map of ’Elephants Crossing the River’? That’s a visualization that we servant monks couldn’t afford in half a lifetime!"

"Yes, it seems Junior Brother Xin Ding might be in trouble."

Chapter 1028: 1027: Lu Yang: This is Exactly How It Feels to Have a Golden Finger!

"Xin Ding, Junior Brother, I see that your movements are nimble, surely you’ve made some breakthroughs recently," Xin Cheng said with a cold smirk. "Why don’t we have a match? It’s the customary rule here in the Servant Monk Institute; the loser must give the winner ten Merit Points."

If Xin Ding refused, he would keep harassing Xin Ding, making it impossible for him to accomplish anything!

"Very well, Senior Brother has invited me, how could I possibly refuse?"

Xin Cheng was stunned, not expecting Xin Ding to agree so readily.

But that was also good, it saved time, and with the Martial Monk Institute selection coming soon, it was best to save time.

Seeing that the two were about to start fighting, some astute students quickly ran out to find the Deacon Master to intervene.

"Please, Xin Ding Junior Brother," Xin Cheng said insincerely, but Xin Ding was not one to exchange courtesies, he struck as soon as he said the word.

Both disciples from the Servant Monk Institute practiced Bodhi Fist, which was the most basic form of boxing, and Xin Ding and Xin Cheng were no exceptions.

Xin Ding attacked first; his Spiritual Sense had greatly improved, allowing for better control over his body, making his execution of the Bodhi Fist techniques more precise.

Xin Cheng was shocked. The punch was both swift and fierce. When had Xin Ding’s boxing become so powerful?

"Look, isn’t Junior Brother Xin Ding dominating Senior Brother Xin Cheng?"

"That shouldn’t be, doesn’t Senior Brother Xin Cheng possess the Fragmented Map of ’Fragrant Elephant Crossing the River’?"

"Could it be that Senior Brother Xin Cheng hasn’t seriously contemplated the Enlightenment Vision Diagram since he obtained it?"

Hearing the surrounding discussions, Xin Cheng felt so frustrated he wanted to spit blood. He had been contemplating since he obtained the vision diagram; logically, no one at the fifth level of Qi Refinement in the Servant Monk Institute should have been able to defeat him, so how could the inconspicuous Xin Ding come out on top?

Xin Cheng became disoriented, and his moves, both fists and kicks, were sloppy, even worse than before he owned the ’Fragrant Elephant Crossing the River’ Fragmented Map.

Seizing the opportunity, Xin Ding widened his stance and landed an Exploding Punch that sent Xin Cheng flying.

The crowd was in uproar as Xin Cheng, who had high hopes of entering the Martial Monk Institute, lost the match!

"Stop it all, what are you doing, Xin Cheng!" a voice from the outskirts of the crowd called out—it was the Deacon Master’s voice. Everyone quickly stepped aside to make way.

"Xin Cheng, you are intentional—uh, why are you lying on the ground?" The Deacon Master had intended to scold Xin Cheng, but saw him sprawled out on the ground instead.

"Did you do this?" the Deacon Master turned and asked Xin Ding.

"Yes, Master Uncle," Xin Ding replied.

The Deacon Master looked meaningfully at Xin Ding, "Not bad, I didn’t expect you to be such a promising seed. If you encounter any difficulties in the future, feel free to come to me. Xin Cheng, you agreed to the terms, now give Xin Ding ten Merit Points."

The servant monks looked on enviously at Xin Ding; having the Deacon Master’s support meant they could approach him with any trouble, signaling that Xin Ding had become a confidant of the Master. Who would dare to trouble Xin Ding now?

Moreover, not only was cultivation challenging, but the Deacon Master might also give guidance to Xin Ding on his cultivation practices.

It was said that the Deacon Master was originally a Golden Core expert who had failed to advance and fell back to the Foundation Establishment Stage. Disheartened, he took on the role of manager at the Servant Monk Institute.

Although his realm had declined, his experience remained.

Xin Cheng got up from the ground, reluctantly ready to give the Merit Points, but Xin Ding showed no indication of accepting them, making Xin Cheng quite embarrassed.

"What do you mean?!"

Xin Ding pressed his hands together and sincerely said, "Senior Brother, as monks, we renounce both anger and gambling. Our earlier exchange was merely a spar, with no stakes involved."

Having said that, Xin Ding turned and walked away, leaving only his silhouette for the crowd to gaze upon.

Exactly, this was the feeling, with the Golden Finger, such treatment was only right!

It’s been so long since he resurrected the Fairy, and as a "Golden Finger," the Fairy never enjoyed the treatment of defeating powerful enemies and leaving with style in front of everyone.

Fairy Eternity was quite unconvinced and felt that Little Yang was heartless in his words, "Who said there wasn’t? Didn’t I use your body and the identity of the Acting Sect Master to let you fight above your level and leave with style?"

Lu Yang held his forehead, that’s not the plot I wanted.

...

"Little monk, do you really want to enter that so-called Martial Monk Institute?"

"Yes, I do."

"Although your Spiritual Sense has improved, the boxing techniques you’ve learned are rather common, and your cultivation level doesn’t hold any advantage."

Xin Ding’s gaze was firm, "I must try it anyway."

"That’s a good attitude to have," Tofu Heavenly Venerate replied, with a hint of relief in his tone.

"Don’t rush the cultivation. Take it slowly to avoid instability in your foundation which might leave hidden dangers. As for the boxing techniques, we can think of a solution."

"Heavenly Venerate, are you going to teach me boxing techniques?" Xin Ding asked excitedly, knowing that the Heavenly Venerate’s techniques were certainly unparalleled in the world.

Tofu Heavenly Venerate chuckled lightly, "How could that be possible? My boxing techniques have dominated all the heavens and silenced gods and ghosts. If you learned my techniques, it would be too easy for others to spot the clues."

"Here’s what we’ll do, you go to the Scripture Pavilion, and I’ll see if I can find some good boxing techniques for you."

"Heavenly Venerate, we servant monks can only access the first floor of the Scripture Pavilion, and the best boxing technique on the first floor is the Bodhi Fist."

"Just go," laughed Tofu Heavenly Venerate.

Xin Ding went to the Scripture Pavilion with a mix of doubt and trust.

The first floor of the Scripture Pavilion at Western Paradise Temple was no less inferior to the Seek Dao Sect, with a collection of books that could be described as "vast."

As Xin Ding wandered around the Scripture Pavilion unsure of what to do, he heard Tofu Heavenly Venerate’s voice in his ear, "Little monk, go to row twelve, sixth bookshelf, third row on the side; there’s a book you need there."

Xin Ding went there and surprisingly found a book on martial arts with the prominent title "Arhat Fist" on the cover!

"Is this the legendary Arhat Fist that, when cultivated to the highest realm, can achieve the Arhat Fruit Position?" Xin Ding’s voice trembled as he spoke, realizing the Merit Points needed to obtain the Arhat Fist were more than all the Merit Points combined in the Servant Monk Institute!

"Heavenly Venerate, how did you know the Arhat Fist was here?"

Lu Yangxin thought to himself, of course I know, I just put it here last night.

If you wanted to learn something else, I could have you find it in the Scripture Pavilion too.

Seeing that Tofu Heavenly Venerate did not respond, Xin Ding assumed this minor issue was not worth the Heavenly Venerate’s reply.

"It must have been hidden here by some great ancient power, awaiting someone destined," Xin Ding mused, having heard such tales in storytelling, without giving much thought to the origin of the Arhat Fist.

Hearing Xin Ding’s words to himself, Lu Yang suddenly remembered a question, "Right, Fairy, are there really cultivation techniques left by the true ancients on the first floor of the Scripture Pavilion?"

"Oh yes, there are plenty. Here lies the Vigorous Vajra Palm, next to it the Prajnaparamita Zen Palm, over there is the Minor Formless Heart Sutra, under the floor tiles the Dragon Subduing and Tiger Suppressing Scripture, and at the very end, the Buddhist Introductory which reveals the Worldly Venerate Enlightenment Vision Diagram when touched by blood," explained the Fairy.

"Do you want a Fragmented Map? This place also hides plenty of those, along with various types of tokens, Shariputras, storage rings, and so on."

Lu Yang: "..."

Why is the first floor of your Western Paradise Temple’s Scripture Pavilion so bustling, while our Seek Dao Sect’s first floor only has the Dragon Phoenix Transformation?

Chapter 1029: 1028: Each with Their Own Unique Skills
