"How long have these cultivation techniques and visualization charts been hidden?"

"Looks like they’ve been stashed away for thousands, maybe even tens of thousands of years."

Lu Yang held his forehead, wondering how deep the monks of the Western Paradise Temple had buried these things. So much time and still undiscovered.

You all hid them so deeply, did you want people to find them or not?

...

"Arhat Fist is swift and ever-changing, emphasizing the release of power through the breath, chanting as loud as thunder, using the voice to bolster courage and the breath to amplify strength..."

The guidance of Tofu Heavenly Venerable came through the sword.

In the field of Arhat Fist, few in the world could match Lu Yang’s expertise. With his guidance, Xin Ding made rapid progress.

Practicing his punches in the courtyard with the boon of his powerful Spiritual Sense, Xin Ding executed each move with precision. In just half a month, he was close to mastering the basics.

"Indeed much stronger than Bodhi Fist." The more Xin Ding practiced, the more he felt the gap between Bodhi Fist and Arhat Fist, worthy to be the supreme fist technique that could certify one’s position as an Arhat.

As Xin Ding immersed himself in practice, a warm current rose from his Dantian, flowing through his limbs and filling him with strength.

"Is the elixir finally taking effect?"

Xin Ding was overjoyed. For his cultivation, under the guidance of Tofu Heavenly Venerable, he had exchanged his own Merit Points for a Lesser Grass Return Elixir. Although it was an elixir that aided breakthroughs, its nature was very mild, and the body would absorb it slowly after consumption.

With his intensive half-month of practice, his body accelerated the absorption of the elixir’s properties, and he finally broke through from the fifth level of Qi Refinement to the sixth level!

His pores opened wide to absorb the Spiritual Energy of heaven and earth to replenish the spiritual essence depleted by his breakthrough.

"If I encounter Senior Brother Xin Cheng now, I could probably knock him down with one punch, I guess." Compared to half a month ago, his strength had improved by leaps and bounds, having obtained the visualization chart of Buddhism’s founder, learned Arhat Fist, and broken through to the sixth level of Qi Refinement.

In the four Servant Monks’ Institutes of the east, south, west, and north, there were few Servant Monks with the Cultivation Level of Qi Refinement Stage Six, meaning Xin Ding truly had the qualifications to compete for a spot in the Martial Monk Institute.

"Qi Refinement Stage Six?"

"Greetings to Deacon Master Zhi Yuan," said Xin Ding as he finished practicing, brought his hands together in a salute, and greeted the Deacon Master whose Dharma name was Zhi Yuan.

"Steady and unarrogant, not bad," Deacon Master Zhi Yuan commended, sighing to himself. If he had had Xin Ding’s temperament, he would not have rushed his own breakthrough and fallen from his realm.

He had been banished to the Servant Monks’ Institute for many years, becoming disheartened and somewhat negligent in his management duties.

But half a month ago, Xin Ding’s victory over Xin Cheng captured his attention. Observing him, he realized that Xin Ding was a rare and promising talent; his temperament was too valuable to be wasted in the Servant Monks’ Institute.

"Keep this string of Buddha Beads safe, they might come in handy during the selection for the Martial Monk Institute," said Deacon Master Zhi Yuan as he removed a string of beads from his wrist.

"Deacon Master, this..." Xin Ding wanted to refuse; those were the Deacon Master’s Buddha Beads, surely not something he could afford.

Even when tallying up everything he had... oh no, that wasn’t right—he had the Yinglong Visualization, the Arhat Fist Manual, and an Immortal Sword.

"Take them. I’m not giving them to you for nothing; I have a favor to ask of you."

"This time, four Servant Monks will be selected to join the Martial Monk Institute. With your current abilities, entering the institute should be no problem."

"However, among these four Servant Monks, there will be rankings, and the one who places first in the selection will get to choose a hundred-year-old herb from the pharmacy. There’s a Spirit Core Grass there that dates back a hundred years; I need you to choose it."

Over these few days of observation, Zhi Yuan’s once dead heart began to stir again, no longer willing to be in decline and intent on returning to the Golden Core Stage. To return to the Golden Core Stage, the Spirit Core Grass was indispensable.

Due to certain reasons, he couldn’t exchange for the Spirit Core Grass himself and could only use the selection opportunity at the Martial Monk Institute to get his hands on it.

"Heavenly Venerate, this..."

"Take it. When you think about it, you’re still getting the better deal; these Buddha Beads have been blessed with Buddhist teachings and are worth slightly more than the hundred-year-old Spirit Core Grass."

"Amitābha," Xin Ding accepted.

...

The Servant Monk Institute was divided into four courts: east, south, west, and north. Xin Ding belonged to the East Servant Monk Court, which was universally acknowledged as the weakest of the four.

All the servant monks drew lots to enter the ring and fight, vying for the four spots available in the Martial Monk Institute and battling it out to see who would come in first.

Xin Ding stood out among the servant monks, as his Qi Refinement Level 6 was enough to sweep away a large number of his peers with ease.

Xin Cheng also advanced to Qi Refinement Level 6, but unfortunately, faced with the powerful Spiritual Sense of Xin Ding, he was defeated once again.

Xin Ding fought his way through, breaking into the top eight. Having just advanced to Qi Refinement Level 6, he was considered the weakest among them, and given his origins from the East Servant Monk Court, no one had much faith in him.

Xin Ding’s opponent was a servant monk from the West Servant Monk Court who had practiced at Qi Refinement Level 6 for a year and had a good chance of entering the Martial Monk Institute.

The victor of their confrontation would earn a place in the Martial Monk Institute!

"Senior Brother Zhi Yuan, I didn’t expect you would come to such a place. In the past years, you have never cared about the selection for the Martial Monk Institute,"

another monk, clad in the same attire as Zhi Yuan, approached. It was Zhi Xing, the Manager of the West Servant Monk Court.

"What, could it be that you think this kid named Xin Ding can beat someone from our West Servant Monk Court?"

"We’ll know after the trial." Faced with Zhi Xing’s provocation, Zhi Yuan remained unshaken.

Zhi Xing let out a cold laugh, said no more, and reveled in the thought of Zhi Yuan, once a lauded genius who made it onto the Earth List, being misled by beauty, failing a rash advancement, and being relegated to the same treatment in the Servant Monk Court.

Zhi Xing was about to watch the fight, eager to see how Xin Ding would lose and what Zhi Yuan’s reaction would be.

But when he looked toward the ring, the battle was already nearing its end, and Xin Ding sent his opponent flying out of the ring with a punch!

The East Servant Monk Court erupted in cheers!

Finally, someone from their court would enter the Martial Monk Institute!

How could it be over so quickly? He had only exchanged a few words with Zhi Yuan!

"You gave him the Buddha Beads!?" Zhi Xing was beside himself with fury, unable to believe that Zhi Yuan would make such a rash bet.

He saw Xin Ding wrapping the Buddha Beads around his hand, employing the Bodhi Fist with Buddha Light emanating from each strike, making the opponent from the West Servant Monk Court utterly unable to compete.

According to the rules, one could only use their possessions during the competition, and borrowing was forbidden, meaning that those precious Buddha Beads that even he coveted now belonged to Xin Ding!

"Material possessions, how could one not give them away?"

"You!"

Following Xin Ding’s match, the other three fights concluded in turn, with each victor securing their place in the Martial Monk Institute.

"It looks like it’s you four then. How fitting that someone from each of the four Servant Monk Courts has made it." A monk from the Martial Monk Institute came to manage the proceedings, and after noticing the Buddha Beads in Xin Ding’s hand, he cast a deliberate glance toward Zhi Yuan, standing not too far away.

Was that fearsome figure who once ranked in the top ten of the Earth List finally waking up?

"According to the rules, you four will fight on the ring, and the last one standing will be the first of the Servant Monk Institute."

Being first in the Servant Monk Institute meant not only selecting herbs from the pharmacy but also, more importantly, receiving a significantly larger share of the institute’s resources once inside the Martial Monk Institute.

Lu Yang stretched lazily; he hadn’t yet had to use his Arhat Fist up to this point. Facing the remaining three servant monks should be no trouble at all.

"Who do you think will win?"

"Of course, it will be our Senior Brother Xin’an from the West Servant Monk Court. Originally, Senior Brother Xin’an was unremarkable, but his Cultivation Level surged unexpectedly a year ago, and he has now reached Qi Refinement Level 7!"

"But can Cultivation Level represent everything? In the end, it might be our Senior Brother Xin Chen from the South Servant Monk Court. They say his cultivation talent used to be extremely poor, always at the bottom of our court.

Then one day, it was as if he had opened a Spiritual Acupoint, changing his formerly timid behavior overnight, and he defeated the related parties of our court, becoming the Eldest Senior Brother in one fell swoop."

"It must be our Senior Brother Xin Ping from the North Servant Monk Court. Everyone says he has awakened his latent Formless Physique, which makes him invincible within his class and capable of challenging those beyond his level. Senior Brother Xin Ping had even defeated cultivators in the late stages of Qi Refinement Level 6 while he was in the late stages of Qi Refinement Level 5.

To get the first-place reward, even though the Martial Monk Institute tried to recruit him, he didn’t go directly but chose to participate in this competition!"

Lu Yang: "..."

Why did these three opponents sound so formidable?

Chapter 1030: 1029: The Backing of Tofu Heavenly Venerate

Lu Yang decided to use his Golden Finger, "Fairy, please help me check, what’s the situation with those three people over there?"

Lu Yang dared not use his Spiritual Sense to scan those three Servant Monks, fearing that they might have backers.

"Let this Immortal check... Oh, I see."

"What’s the situation?"

"One person has a cultivator hidden in his jade pendant, another has a cultivator hidden within his compass, and another has a cultivator hidden within his body. Is this a custom of the Western Paradise Temple, or are we just following local traditions?"

"...I think Western Paradise Temple probably doesn’t know about such customs."

Lu Yang thought to himself that Western Paradise Temple should really take care of its Servant Monastery; each one of its Servant Monks seemed to be extraordinary.

The three Servant Monks from the south, west, and north all firmly believed they were the strongest and, coincidentally, chose to fight one-on-one.

Xin Ding had the lowest Cultivation Level, and none of the three took him seriously. Even with Buddha Beads, how much power could they exhibit, and how could they possibly be compared to their hidden aces?

"Carefree Steps!"

"Bodhi Sealing Fist!"

"Formless Divine Skill!"

Each of the three had had their own fortune, and the martial arts they used were not merely the Bodhi Fist. Each had its own formidable strength which made it difficult to determine the superior one in the fight.

The Bodhi Sealing Fist and the Formless Divine Skill were fierce and powerful, while the Carefree Steps was a technique for evading most attacks.

Xin Ding’s opponent was Xin Chen from the Southern Servant Monastery. Faced with the Bodhi Sealing Fist, Xin Ding dared not hold back anymore and wrapped his right hand with Buddha Beads, directly using the Arhat Fist.

Boom—

Fists collided and both stepped back several paces before stabilizing their bodies, with Xin Chen taking three more steps back than Xin Ding!

The crowd was astounded; Xin Chen, who held the advantage in Cultivation Level and was expected to win, had actually fallen behind in the fight!

"This is the Arhat Fist!" The presiding monk of the Martial Monk Institute stood up in shock, his face incredulous. He had been fortunate to see the head of the Martial Monk Institute and the Elders of the Western Paradise Temple perform it. This was the famous Arhat Fist of Buddhism, a martial art even ordinary monks like him weren’t qualified to learn!

"Zhi Yuan Senior Brother, did you teach him?" If anyone in this place might have taught Xin Ding the Arhat Fist, it would only be the once genius Zhi Yuan.

But as far as he knew, Zhi Yuan wasn’t qualified to teach the Arhat Fist.

If the higher-ups found out about this, the lightest punishment would be expulsion from Western Paradise Temple, and the worst would be complete destruction of his Cultivation Level!

Zhi Yuan shook his head calmly; he had already inquired about where Xin Ding had learned the Arhat Fist.

"He found the complete Arhat Fist in the Scripture Pavilion. Even I only have the first chapter of the Arhat Fist, whereas what he found in the Scripture Pavilion was the complete text."

Just for this reason alone, it wouldn’t have been Zhi Yuan who taught him.

"The Scripture Pavilion, huh? That kid is really lucky." The monk from the Martial Monk Institute envied Xin Ding. The Arhat Fist was such that even those at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage could use it; countless people outside coveted the Arhat Fist of Buddhism.

Lu Yang knew the Arhat Fist, and so did everyone he knew, so he didn’t think Arhat Fist was that rare; that was because he stood too high, for ordinary Divine Transformation Stage Cultivators, the Arhat Fist was a martial art tantalizingly out of reach.

"I heard that the Scripture Pavilion also holds the Vigorous Vajra Palm, the Dragon Subduing and Tiger Suppressing Scripture, Shariputra, but sadly, no one has found them yet." The monk from the Martial Monk Institute said regretfully.

Xin Ding and Xin Chen were engaged in fierce combat, with Xin Chen unable to overcome Xin Ding even with the use of magic artifacts, growing increasingly anxious as cold sweat formed on his forehead.

He had envisaged seizing victory in one fell swoop; how could he not defeat someone with a lower Cultivation Level than him?

"Trash, let me take over." A domineering voice echoed in Xin Chen’s mind, and in an instant, he lost control of his body.

Xin Chen’s fighting style changed abruptly. What was once an awkward utilization of the Bodhi Sealing Fist suddenly became fierce and powerful, with each punch heavy enough to burst a skull.

The hidden soul within Xin Chen rejoiced at the sight of the Arhat Fist. He had infiltrated Western Paradise Temple through the guise of a Servant Monk precisely to seize the chance to acquire the Arhat Fist; he thought he would have to lie low for a long time, but the Arhat Fist was now within his reach!

As soon as Lu Yang saw this, he knew what had happened to Xin Chen.

"So you’re the only one who can swap out, huh?"

"Later generations have no respect for rules, little monk. Let this Venerable fight in your stead!"

As the saying goes, practice makes perfect; having extensive experience in being possessed, Tofu Heavenly Venerable was extremely familiar with the ways of possession and immediately took over Xin Ding.

"Arhat Fist!"

Lu Yang, stepping in as the fighter, demonstrated the Arhat Fist, which stood apart from the ordinary. Chants of Buddha resonated within him, surging like a tidal wave.

He was not afraid of revealing his identity through the Arhat Fist; all in the Western Paradise Temple were monks who did not have to be concerned about losing their hair.

Lu Yang landed a punch on "Xin Chen"’s abdomen, causing "Xin Chen"’s eyeballs to nearly pop out.

Why was there also someone hidden in the other person’s body?

And their combat ability was extremely terrifying!

After Lu Yang took over the fight, the situation turned into a one-sided affair, with Xin Chen standing no chance at all!

Hidden souls within Xin’an and Xin Ping, witnessing this, both deduced what had transpired on Lu Yang’s side.

If that were the case, there was no need to remain hidden any longer. They might as well switch fighters directly!

Two powerful souls took over the bodies of Xin’an and Xin Ping and charged towards Lu Yang and Xin Chen.

The original face-off between pairs morphed into a true melee. Although all four were powerful entities, they currently inhabited Qi Cultivation Stage bodies, which severely limited their techniques regardless of their vast capabilities.

Furthermore, onlookers from the Martial Monk Institute were present, so they had to restrain themselves to avoid detection.

"This won’t do," Lu Yang clenched his teeth and muttered. He didn’t fear the three of them, and the odds were in his favor if the fight continued, but the problem was that Xin Ding was only at the sixth level of Qi Cultivation and couldn’t withstand substituting for others for long.

"Everyone, come in!"

Lu Yang no longer occupied Xin Ding’s body but returned to the Green Blade Sword Small World. He opened the Small World and sucked in the other three powerful souls with him.

All three souls believed they were the strongest and were unafraid to confront whatever techniques Lu Yang could muster, allowing themselves to be pulled into the Small World - an ideal way to rid themselves of this nuisance altogether!

Upon entering the Small World, Lu Yang finally saw the true forms of the three individuals.

An elder, a middle-aged man, and a young man.

"Unexpected, indeed. I thought I was the only one who infiltrated the Western Heaven Temple in such a manner, but it turns out there were three others like me," said the soul hidden within the jade pendant of Xin Chen, who was an elder.

"I’m known as Mo Xiaochou, at the Mid-stage of Transforming Spirit, and you should have heard of my name."

Mo Xiaochou realized the truth: the reason Xin Ding knew Arhat Fist was not because he had found it in the Scripture Pavilion but because it was taught to him by the person behind him.

He looked at Lu Yang, "I wonder if you’d be willing to teach me a set of Arhat Fist, the price can be nego... You are Lu Yang!"

Before, Mo Xiaochou hadn’t paid much attention to Lu Yang’s appearance, but now he was taken aback by the realization.

Was this the renowned Talent Lu, known for punishing evil and promoting good?

Why was he here?!

"It seems Mo Dao-friend came secretly to the Western Paradise Temple to learn the Arhat Fist," Lu Yang said with a smile that wasn’t quite a smile, as he drew the Inheriting Shadow Sword as though he was helping the temple catch a thief.

Mo Xiaochou inwardly cursed his luck. As fellow cultivators at the Mid-stage of Transforming Spirit, he stood no chance against Lu Yang.

"Hey, why don’t we three join forces to take him down?" Mo Xiaochou shouted, seeing no other solution.

The young man hidden within Xin Ping’s body ignored Mo Xiaochou’s proposition.

"Talent Lu? He’s nothing but a youth eager for quick success and unearned reputation," the middle-aged man hiding inside Xin Chen’s compass said coldly, "Since he’s a genius cultivated by the Seek Dao Sect, he must possess many fine treasures. It’d be best if you leave both your life and your treasures here."

As he uttered the word, an early-stage Body Integration aura erupted, pressing Mo Xiaochou down to his knees with the might of colossal weight.

"A mere early-stage Body Integration soul dares to be so arrogant?" Lu Yang scoffed. All three were only in their soul state, with their cultivation halved, whereas he was fully embodied with his soul intact, not fearing the middle-aged man in the slightest.

Not to mention, his soul strength was comparable to Mid Integration Stage.

The middle-aged man laughed mockingly at Lu Yang’s attitude, "Arrogant? Today, you will learn the gap between Body Integration and Divine Transformation!"

"I shall establish the Rule:..."

"Extinguishing Immortal Sword Array!" In an instant, Lu Yang released the Inheriting Shadow Sword along with the Seven Star Sword Set, forming the Extinguishing Immortal Sword Array with the eight Immortal Treasure-level swords above the middle-aged man’s head.

Before the middle-aged man could finish his speech, he was suddenly weighed down as if by ten thousand mountains, and he fell to his knees with a thud.

Lu Yang, like a Kun Peng, summoned the Inheriting Shadow Sword, imbuing it with the Three Flavored True Fire and the Golden Bird True Flame. The Golden Bird True Flame, being extremely pure and yang, was the bane of Souls!

Let alone the strength of Lu Yang’s Soul Body was so formidable that he could dominate the middle-aged man in single combat.

Lu Yang transformed into a Sword Immortal, his sword light piercingly cold. Every sword he wielded was imbued with Sword Qi, terrifying the middle-aged man to his core, for he had no time to establish any Rule.

Shk—

The Inheriting Shadow Sword pierced the middle-aged man’s skull, pinning him to a large rock.

"Fighting above your level, huh? An interesting junior," the always silent young man finally spoke, clapping lightly, his eyes showing appreciation for Lu Yang, "Mysterious Sword Arrays, the legendary Inheriting Shadow Sword, and this Small World - even I do not possess such richness in resources."

Avarice gleamed in the young man’s eyes. The value of Lu Yang’s assets was even greater than what he had hoped to acquire from the Western Heaven Temple. "Treasures belong to those predestined to own them; all of this should rightfully belong to me," he declared.

No longer restraining his aura, the young man unleashed his Early Tribulation Stage essence, an invisible pressure radiating from him as the epicenter, swiftly spreading in all directions!

Mo Xiaochou felt his soul being crushed, on the verge of scattering and facing imminent destruction!

"An Early Tribulation Stage? Indeed, I cannot beat that," Lu Yang perceived the cultivation level of the young man, and with a resigned smile, he leisurely put away the Inheriting Shadow Sword and the Seven Star Sword Set.

"Fairy, it’s up to you now."

Chapter 1031: 1030

A joyful hum emanated from within Lu Yang’s body, and a girl clad in a goose-yellow dress, looking like the girl next door, emerged from his body with a giggling smile.

The girl, cute as the girl next door, floated in the air, flying around Lu Yang with pride.

"Hum hum, so you do need this Immortal’s help, don’t you?"

"Yes, yes, I am troubled to ask for Fairy’s assistance."

"It’s not really a trouble, as a venerable official of this dynasty, to loyally assist this Immortal is to ensure your own peace, as should be."

"Your humble servant thanks you for your heavenly grace!"

The sovereign and minister chatted amiably, speaking in terms the others couldn’t understand, the atmosphere harmonious, a picture of a wise ruler and worthy subjects, completely ignoring the presence of the young man.

Faced with this situation, the young man couldn’t muster any anger, not even a will to fight; all he felt was the endless chill sprouting in his heart.

Ever since the girl appeared, the pressure he deliberately exerted had vanished without a trace, inexplicably, a change that completely surpassed his understanding.

Who is she! Who is she!

The young man’s Lingtai raced, pondering the current predicament.

Late Tribulation Stage, wait, Lu Yang just called her "Fairy," thinking of this, the young man could not help but shiver, could she possibly be a Semi-Immortal?!

And why does this junior named Lu Yang have another Soul Body within him?!

The girl noticed the young man’s gaze, her brow slightly furrowed, pouting her lips in a somewhat displeased manner.

"What right do you have to take Little Yang’s things, Little Yang is my person after all."

The young man braced for confrontation, not daring to take the girl lightly, he said politely with cupped fists, "This old man is Qing Yan, I was unaware that the junior was under your protection, no offense was intended."

"You’re the esteemed Qing Yan?" The middle-aged man nailed to the great rock by the Inheriting Shadow Sword showed an astonished expression upon hearing the young man’s title.

"Wasn’t it said that you perished at the hands of Master Mingyu?"

Master Mingyu, Abbot of Western Paradise Temple.

"The esteemed Qing Yan?" Lu Yang had heard this honorific, remembered he’d reversed Buddhist scriptures, transformed from Buddha to demon, becoming a great demon of Buddha Country, his notorious name enough to stop children’s cries, yet he hadn’t heard of him causing trouble in Great Xia.

"Since you’ve offended, you may as well stay behind." Despite the esteemed Qing Yan’s polite attitude towards Immortal Fairy, the moment she thought of him trying to take Lu Yang’s things, Immortal Fairy held no good impression of him.

These words made the esteemed Qing Yan shiver, suddenly feeling a sense of impending doom.

Immortal Fairy spoke in an even tone, but Qing Yan understood it as the arrogance of a superior to an inferior, an insignificant one whom a superior would not waste emotions on when dealing at hand.

"Do you really think I can be handled so easily?!"

The esteemed Qing Yan roared in anger; he, in the stature of Early Tribulation Stage, could not be trifled with by just anyone.

Those who had cultivated to the Tribulation Stage were all formidable beings, each with a few trump cards, not to be used unless absolutely necessary.

The esteemed Qing Yan’s eyes narrowed, chanting an unknown Buddhist scripture.

"In this corrupt world of degeneration, may ice seal the universe!"

Suddenly, goose-feather-like snow began to fall within the Green Blade Sword Small World, the temperature plummeted, and even Lu Yang, impervious to cold or heat, felt the chill, needing the Three Flavored True Fire and the Golden Bird True Flame to resist.

The temperature in the Small World dropped to a terrifying level, Lu Yang even felt as if the water in his body was about to freeze.

"The Age of Dharma’s Decline, the era of last resort!"

The Venerable Qing Yan’s scriptures changed once more, becoming fervent and progressive, his eyes brimming with frenzy and bloodthirst.

The spiritual essence in the Small World was completely frozen, unable to be refined; this was the End of Dharma Era mentioned in the Buddhist scriptures, a struggle at the world’s end. During the End of Dharma Era, spiritual essence could not be harnessed, cultivators could no longer cultivate, and they were thoroughly reduced to mortals.

"Five Ghosts Moving Mountains, Six Generals Supporting Heaven!"

Venerable Qing Yan summoned eleven giant figures, taller even than when Meng Jingzhou displayed his full Dharma Aspect.

The middle-aged man’s face showed despair. Was this the legendary ultimate move of Venerable Qing Yan, that supposedly left everyone who saw it with a slim chance of survival?

The mountains moved by the Five Ghosts and the heavens supported by the Six Generals weren’t truly existent. They were formed of humanity’s evil thoughts - noisy, angry, sorrowful...

Bathed in humanity’s malevolence, even one who survived by luck would turn utterly insane.

"Even Ming Yu wouldn’t dare to face this move head-on. Let’s see what tricks you, a Semi-Immortal, truly have up your sleeve!" Venerable Qing Yan dared not hold back, employing all his trump cards; he couldn’t believe that the girl next door would emerge unscathed!

Fairy Eternity pouted, somewhat impatiently: "What a complete mess, Arhat Fruit Fist."

Fairy Eternity launched an Immortal Fist Technique, and with the Arhat Fruit Fist—carrying a purifying intent—with just one punch, the entire Small World was instantly rejuvenated, as if spring had returned to the earth, warm and blooming.

What End of Dharma Era, with its miles of frozen evil and turbidity—all vanished without a trace, replaced by a new world teeming with life.

"Bodhi Leaf Fist."

Fairy Eternity’s fist technique changed, and the Bodhi Leaf Fist, aiding restful sleep and equally potent in purification, washed away the maliciously condensed mountains and skies with an immense tide of purifying intent, erasing the negative emotions completely.

"What kind of fist technique is this?!"

Venerable Qing Yan was astounded, Arhat Fruit Fist, Bodhi Leaf Fist, they sounded nonsensical, how could they possibly possess such power?!

"Qing Yan Dissolves into the Clear Sky!"

Seeing he was no match, Venerable Qing Yan made a snap decision and resorted to his Escape Skill. This top-notch skill had once allowed him to slip away from Master Ming Yu, capable of creating a illusory clone to deceive his opponent, while his true form transformed into a wisp of Qing Yan, disappearing without a trace, eluding anyone’s search!

"What’s that, you call this an escape skill? Time Immortal’s and Yingtian Immortal’s escape skills are much better than yours."

Fairy Eternity clasped her hands together, interlocking her fingers. Venerable Qing Yan, who thought he had vanished like smoke in thin air, was pinched into form, forced back to his original appearance.

"What kind of technique is this, who exactly are you?!"

Desperation filled the eyes of Venerable Qing Yan. This was an opponent on an entirely different level compared to Master Ming Yu. He could understand the moves of Ming Yu, but every technique Fairy Eternity used was beyond his comprehension!

"You are not worthy of knowing who this Immortal is, fix."

Fairy Eternity uttered the word "fix," and Venerable Qing Yan was struck with alarm; could this be the legendary Fixation Technique?

But he tried to struggle and found he was not fixed in place.

Fairy Eternity’s expression darkened: "If you dare move a bit, you’re as good as dead."

Venerable Qing Yan immediately was so scared that he did not dare to move an inch.

"And that goes for you two as well." Fairy Eternity used the Fixation Technique on Mo Xiaochou and the middle-aged man too, with outstanding results.

"All done."

Lu Yang hurried forward to welcome Fairy Eternity’s triumphant return: "With His Majesty in action, how could such petty figures possibly stand a chance of victory?"

"His Majesty’s martial prowess is unparalleled throughout history, indeed a naturally brilliant, supremely strategic, world-shaking figure, surpassing all predecessors, and illuminating for posterity!" Lu Yang clapped enthusiastically, genuinely full of praise.

"Right, right, this Immortal is awesome," Fairy Eternity stood with hands on her hips, brimming with pride, as if she had vanquished a formidable foe.

Chapter 1032: 1031: Qi Refinement Level 6 Defeating Qi Refinement Level 7

"Let’s hear it, what’s your situation, and why are you hiding in the Servant Monk Institute?"

With his hands behind his back, Lu Yang paced before the three soul bodies. Even though Lu Yang was only at the mid-stage of Transforming Spirit and not of high seniority, the three soul bodies were still trembling in fear, not daring to hide anything from him.

"Speak the truth."

Immortal Fairy, standing behind Lu Yang, said fiercely.

"Yes, yes, yes," the three soul bodies hurriedly responded, not daring to utter a single word of dissent.

"I am here to learn the Arhat Fist," Mo Xiaochou confessed first.

"Tell the truth."

"I am here to steal the technique of the Arhat Fist."

"The discipline towards the Servant Monks in Western Heaven Temple is lax, providing the simplest way to infiltrate. I planned on possessing one of the Servant Monks and gradually climb the ranks. Eventually, there’d be a chance to come into contact with the Arhat Fist."

"My name is Duan Qingshui." The middle-aged man at the early Integration Stage said. His purpose for infiltrating Western Heaven Temple was the same as Mo Xiaochou’s—to steal techniques. Except, he aimed to steal one of the three great scriptures of Western Heaven Temple, the "Great Collection Sutra."

"Where are your physical bodies?"

"Hidden away outside."

"Why separate?" Lu Yang wondered. Separating the spirit from the body was dangerous, as it halved one’s combat power.

"We don’t have magic artifacts capable of carrying our physical bodies without being detected," Duan Qingshui explained helplessly. They didn’t want it this way; they had no choice. Such magic artifacts were extremely rare; they couldn’t buy them even if they wanted to.

Qing Yan Venerate had fought a fierce battle with Master Mingyu, one that left only his soul remaining. After employing the Escape Skill "Qing Smoke Transforming the Blue Sky," his soul was still gravely injured. Left with no choice, he had to possess the body of Xin Ping from the northern Servant Monk Institute.

In order to help Xin Ping advance to a higher institute, allowing Qing Yan Venerate to recover his strength and look for a chance to make a scene in Western Heaven Temple as revenge against Master Mingyu, he cultivated Xin Ping into possessing the Formless Physique using a secret method, ensuring it would go undetected by anyone.

"You’ve saved Western Heaven Temple a big trouble," Lu Yang could imagine that if Qing Yan Venerate really managed to stay hidden, he could indeed stir up significant trouble in Western Heaven Temple.

However, he acted righteously without seeking reward; there was no need to report such a minor matter to Western Heaven Temple.

"Leave these three in the Small World for now, and when we return to the Sect, lock them up in Captive Peak."

All three had seen Immortal Fairy, so there was no choice but to silence them or confine them to Captive Peak.

Lu Yang hadn’t expected that in just over half a month since his arrival at Western Heaven Temple, he’d helped Captive Peak capture three more individuals. The only issue was that Mo Xiaochou and Duan Qingshui were of too low a Cultivation Level, especially Mo Xiaochou, who would be bottom-ranked at Captive Peak.

"Never mind, a mosquito is still meat no matter how small," Lu Yang believed that doing good deeds didn’t discriminate by size.

Having dealt with these three, Lu Yang finally had time to continue watching Xin Ding’s match.

"What, Xin Ding at the sixth level of Qi Refinement actually defeated Xin’an at the seventh level!"

"This is a challenge beyond his level!"

The battle in the Green Blade Sword Small World had concluded, and the Martial Monk Institute selection competition was also nearing its end. Xin Ding, who no one had faith in, defeated the most likely candidate, Xin’an.

Defeating someone from the seventh level of Qi Refinement while at the sixth level was no small feat. It signified a mid-stage practitioner overcoming a late-stage one. How could the Servant Monks not cheer ecstatically at this scene?

Especially the Servant Monks from the eastern Servant Monk Institute, who were the most excited. How many years had it been since they last claimed victory? Now, finally, they could hold their heads high in front of the other three institutes.

Lu Yang rubbed his temples in pain. The disparity between the fights inside and outside the Small World was substantial, and sudden exposure was somewhat overwhelming.

"Zhi Yuan Senior Brother, you’ve taught a good disciple," a monk from the Martial Monk Institute remarked meaningfully to Zhi Yuan. He definitely didn’t believe that Zhi Yuan hadn’t taught Xin Ding.

It seems that these years of seclusion by Zhi Yuan were a facade. In reality, he had been secretly grooming a successor all along, and Xin Ding was the prime example.

Given Xin Ding’s display of talent today, there was a chance he could enter the Heaven-Earth-Man rankings and become a famed expert listed there.

Zhi Yuan couldn’t be bothered to reply; after all, even if he denied teaching Xin Ding, the other person wouldn’t believe it.

"However, once he joins the Martial Monk Institute, he might face targeting—after all, Senior Brother, you made quite a few enemies there back in the day," the monk from the Martial Monk Institute warned, having a relatively good relationship with Zhi Yuan. But as for others, that was less certain.

Zhi Yuan glared, "How dare they! Do they really think I’m dead?"

"In any case, I will do my best to take care of him, but I can’t guarantee anything beyond that," said the monk from the Martial Monk Institute.

Zhi Yuan calmed down, pressed his palms together in gratitude, "Then I’ll have to trouble Junior Brother."

...

"I announce, Xin Ding is the top candidate in this selection," the monk from the Martial Monk Institute declared loudly, prompting cheers from the Eastern Servant Monk Institute.

"Xin Ding, as the top candidate you can choose any hundred-year-old herb from the medicine warehouse. Have you thought about what you want?"

"This Master Uncle..."

"You may call me Master Uncle Zhi Kong."

"Master Uncle Zhi Kong, I’ve made my decision."

"Then follow me."

Zhi Kong said, leading Xin Ding towards the medicine warehouse.

Zhi Kong knocked on the warehouse door ring, "Master Uncle Wu Nian, the selection at the Servant Monk Institute has ended, I’ve brought someone to pick herbs."

"Come in, just remember not to trample the seedlings at the doorway."

The great doors of the medicine warehouse opened automatically, revealing a field of herbs, with a little old man going about sprinkling water from his watering can.

The little old man flew in front of Xin Ding with his hands clasped behind his back. Facing the little man, Xin Ding felt utterly naked, with no secrets to hide.

He was definitely a great master!

"He’s just at the early stage of Spirit Transformation," came the lazy voice of Tofu Heavenly Venerable beside Xin Ding. A random person from the Green Blade Sword Small World would be stronger than this little old man.

Knowing the little old man’s cultivation level, Xin Ding still thought he was very impressive. Being at the initial stage of Spirit Transformation was already an unattainable level for him.

He feared he might never reach the stage of Spirit Transformation in his lifetime.

"What herb do you want?" asked the little old man.

"Hundred-year Spirit Core Grass."

"So it is Spirit Core Grass," the little old man surmised upon seeing the Buddha beads Xin Ding wore, guessing that he would want the Spirit Core Grass.

Spirit Core Grass was rare, and not many knew about it.

The little old man sighed, "So Zhi Yuan still hasn’t forgiven me, his master, wanting to obtain Spirit Core Grass through you."

"Zhi Yuan was the most gifted and virtuous student I had ever taken, who would have thought that he, so devoted to Buddhism, would meet the Red Lotus Demoness on a mission, become entangled with her several times, and develop worldly affections, breaking the monastic precepts? If I hadn’t separated them, who would take the monastic precepts seriously?"

"Unexpectedly, even though I separated them physically, I did not separate their hearts. He rashly attempted to reach the Nascent Soul Stage, fell ill from longing, and his efforts fell short, causing him to drop back to the Foundation Establishment Stage."

"I intended to directly give him the Spirit Core Grass, but I never imagined he would hold a grudge against me, unwilling to accept anything from me, and he also didn’t have the qualifications to obtain Spirit Core Grass..."

The little old man rambled on for quite a while, and Xin Ding managed to roughly understand Master Uncle Zhi Yuan’s story.

"I’m puzzled about something and would like to ask Master Uncle to clear my confusion."

"Ask," said the little old man with a cheerful smile, seemingly treating Xin Ding as his grand-disciple.

"If Buddhism is a sanctuary far from worldly affairs, and marrying a person from the secular world means developing worldly affections, then why not let the Red Lotus Demoness also join our Western Paradise Temple? In that way, Master Uncle Zhi Yuan marrying her wouldn’t be a problem, right?"

The little old man began to doubt his own judgment.

Did Zhi Yuan truly teach this disciple?

Chapter 1033: 1032: Relying on a Backer

Zhi Kong looked at Xin Ding with astonishment, a good question indeed. How had he never thought of this before?

"Certainly not," the little old man replied. He had cultivated for several hundred years, guarded the garden, and his Zen skills were exceptional. He spoke slowly and deliberately, smiling as he answered Xin Ding’s question.

"Marriage is a matter of the secular world. Our Buddhism is a pure land of cultivation. If the Red Lotus Demoness converts to Buddhism and marries Zhi Yuan, wouldn’t that be introducing worldly affairs into the pure land, breaking its rules?"

Zhi Kong suddenly realized the truth of the matter, worthy of being Uncle Wu Nian, resolving his confusion in a few words.

However, Xin Ding was still puzzled, his clear eyes fixed on the little old man: "But I see that gambling and fighting are rampant in our menial work division. Uncle Zhi Xing from the Western menial work division provoked Uncle Zhi Yuan, the four menial divisions competed against each other, and the Martial Monk Institute selected the top four to compete for first place.

Aren’t these all worldly matters too?"

"I..."

The little old man was at a loss for words, unable to reply to Xin Ding immediately.

Primarily because everything Xin Ding said was true, he found himself unable to retort.

Xin Ding came to a sudden realization, finally understanding the matter: "I get it, there’s only one Uncle Zhi Yuan, easy to manage. The rest involves too many people, and if our Buddhism cannot manage, we just let it be."

"I..."

The little old man was rendered speechless again. How could this seemingly simple-minded youngster speak nothing but the truth?

This youngster could not have been taught by Zhi Yuan. If Zhi Yuan had such a mind, he wouldn’t have been trapped by emotions and failed his breakthrough!

Lu Yang laughed so hard in the Green Blade Sword Small World that his stomach hurt. Xin Ding’s single-minded nature indeed made him an excellent seedling for Buddhism.

The little old man took a deep breath and spoke earnestly, "Xin Ding, you must understand that everything undergoes a process of change. Our Western Paradise Temple indeed has many impurities, but you should also see that we are cleansing these matters. It is just that such things take time."

"How much time does it need?"

"A very long time."

Xin Ding thought about it and felt like his great-uncle had said something but also as if he hadn’t said anything at all.

The little old man dared not continue the conversation with Xin Ding, lest the young monk asked more questions he couldn’t answer. He gave Zhi Kong the Spirit Core Grass and hurriedly had him take Xin Ding away.

"Uncle Zhi Yuan, here is the Spirit Core Grass," Xin Ding said, handing the Spirit Core Grass to Uncle Zhi Yuan as agreed.

Holding the Spirit Core Grass, Uncle Zhi Yuan showed a nostalgic expression: "I suppose you have met the master, haven’t you?"

"Uncle Wu Nian? Yes, I did."

"Did he mention anything about me?"

"He did."

Uncle Zhi Yuan asked eagerly, "What did he say?" Although the master had separated him from the Red Lotus, ultimately it was his own fault. He did not want to face the master because he couldn’t get over this hurdle in his heart.

Xin Ding thought for a while. He had a long conversation with the great-uncle, and it would be troublesome to repeat it. So, he decided to summarize the content of their discussion.

"He said that our Western Paradise Temple has too many impurities to manage. Uncle, your desire to marry the Red Lotus Demoness is too conspicuous, and the Western Paradise Temple cannot overlook it."

Zhi Yuan suddenly understood and seemed very excited, "So, does the master mean I can just marry Red Lotus in secret?"

The master was indeed on his side!

"Is that what great-uncle meant?" Xin Ding recalled the great-uncle’s reaction, feeling that it might not have been the case, but given his own dull wit, it was normal for him not to understand the deeper meaning of the great-uncle’s words.

After that, Zhi Kong took Xin Ding and the other four to the Martial Monk Institute. Xin’an, Xin Chen, and Xin Ping looked at Xin Ding with awe.

It wasn’t just because Xin Ding won the championship with his absolute strength, but also because the powerful Soul Body that was instructing their cultivation within their own bodies had vanished after that battle!

The strength of those three Soul Bodies was something they were all too aware of, yet they disappeared without a trace, what secrets could possibly be hidden on Xin Ding’s body!

Just thinking about this point, they couldn’t help but shiver.

"From today on, you are Martial Monks of the Martial Monk Institute," Zhi Kong led the four to receive their Martial Monk Robes and took them around the Martial Monk Institute before saying, and provided the three with new identity proofs.

"Starting from next month, each of you will get ten lower-grade Spirit Stones and ten Merit Points as a monthly allowance, if you wish to obtain more resources, you will have to rely on your own efforts."

The other three were very excited, the treatment at the Martial Monk Institute was indeed heaven and earth compared to the Servant Institute.

"I have other matters to attend to, I’ll be leaving first," Zhi Yuan, having explained almost everything, left the four behind as he had his own affairs to deal with.

"Are you four the new arrivals?" Right after Zhi Yuan left, some people immediately approached, roughly seven or eight of them, with the leader being at the Consummation of Qi Refinement Realm!

The leader glanced at Xin Ding, intentionally or otherwise; he was Xin Cheng’s backer in the Martial Monk Institute, originally thinking Xin Cheng, with the Fragmented Map of the ’Elephant Crossing the River’ he gave, could enter the Martial Monk Institute, yet he didn’t expect Xin Cheng to be eliminated by Xin Ding.

"Greetings, Senior Brother."

"Newcomers must learn the rules, each new Martial Monk must spar with us, to let you know that there are always people better than you!"

Xin Ding’s group became nervous.

"Heavenly Venerate, what should we do?" Xin Ding saw the unkind intentions of the visitors; he was definitely no match for the Martial Monk at the Consummation of Qi Refinement.

Tofu Heavenly Venerable gave a slight smile, such a trivial matter didn’t require his intervention, and indicated a clear path for Xin Ding, "Find a backer."

"I have a backer?"

...

"Great Uncle Master, they are bullying me," Xin Ding ran to the pharmacy to complain to the little old man.

The little old man didn’t expect that just after he had finished talking about the nefarious deeds at Western Paradise Temple, and within half a day, Xin Ding would encounter such deeds.

He couldn’t ignore this.

Having a Great Uncle Master at the Divinity Transformation Stage as a backer, who in the Martial Monk Institute would dare bring up the matter of new Martial Monks sparring with the older ones?

...

"Do you know, every new Martial Monk’s monthly allowance must have twenty percent handed over to us..."

"Great Uncle Master, they are extorting me."

"You are Zhi Yuan’s disciple; do you know what Zhi Yuan was like when he was in the Martial Monk Institute..."

"Great Uncle Master, since they can’t beat Uncle Master Zhi Yuan, they come to bully me."

Soon, everyone in the Martial Monk Institute knew Xin Ding had connections.

Except Xin Ding himself.

Zhi Yuan, using the power of Spirit Core Grass, returned to the pinnacle of the Golden Core, roamed around the Martial Monk Institute, and was astounded to find no one was bullying Xin Ding.

Seeing the inexplicably good attitude of everyone in the Martial Monk Institute towards him, Xin Ding didn’t think much of it, and just assumed it was the reward for steadfastly guarding the secret of Tofu Heavenly Venerable and saving Western Paradise Temple, the good deed had brought good karma.

Once they knew of Xin Ding’s identity, the Martial Monk Institute would not treat him as they did the other newcomers; resources were allocated in his favor.

"Xin Ding, others who have just joined the Martial Monk Institute can choose any Cultivation Technique from the second floor of the Scripture Pavilion, but you, being Wu Nian’s disciple, naturally have a different situation; you can choose any three Cultivation Techniques."

"Great Uncle Master, they are allowing me to cut corners."

Chapter 1034: 1033: Learning Lion Roar (Requesting Monthly Tickets at the Beginning of the Month)

Xin Ding was searching for books on the second floor of the Scripture Pavilion; according to the rules, he could only take one book with him.

The books here were dazzlingly varied, and he was confused about what to choose.

"Fairy, are there hidden opportunities on the second floor?" Tofu Heavenly Venerate asked Heavenly Venerate Dou.

Heavenly Venerate Dou opened his Immortal Sense and scanned around, then shook his head, "Like the first floor, those opportunities are gone, but there are other opportunities."

"What opportunities?"

"See that monk sweeping the floor? He is actually at the Late Tribulation Stage."

"To think such a master was hidden here." Lu Yang was immensely surprised, thankful that he hadn’t rashly used his Spiritual Sense to probe, as he would certainly have been detected by the other.

"Heavenly Venerate, which book should I choose?"

"Fairy, which book should he choose?"

"That one called Qing Yan, come here, are there any good cultivation techniques on this second floor?"

Qing Yan, an honest and straightforward figure, came over. Having lived for over a thousand years in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage and possessing multiple identities, he had visited the Scripture Pavilion in the Western Paradise Temple several times. He was the most familiar with the Western Paradise Temple within the Green Peak Sword Small World.

"How about the ’Six-character Mantra Scripture’? If you memorize this scripture, it has the effect of blessing and exorcising evil."

"No, it’s too difficult to learn."

"How about the ’Great Compassionate Mantra’? It can purify the soul."

"No need, this little monk’s soul is pure enough."

"How about the ’Great Brightness Spell’? It’s effective against soul attacks."

"Sounds pretty average."

"What about ’Lion Roar’? It’s a sonic attack that imitates the roar of a lion and can deter enemies. Although the second floor only has the first part, it should be enough for this little monk."

"That will do. Little Yang, let him choose ’Lion Roar’."

"Little monk, you choose ’Lion Roar’."

"Okay." Xin Ding trusted Tofu Heavenly Venerate greatly and took the first part of Lion Roar.

...

The treatment of martial monks and servant monks was not comparable; each martial monk owned their private monastery, and Xin Ding, unable to wait, started flipping through ’Lion Roar’ upon his return to the monastery.

After glancing through it twice, Xin Ding felt dizzy and couldn’t quite understand the technique.

"Heavenly Venerate, do you know Lion Roar? Could you teach me?"

From the Green Peak Sword, the eternal raspy voice of Tofu Heavenly Venerate replied, "Hehe, this seat has traversed through the heavens, mastered countless techniques; what is there I do not know? But if I were to teach you directly, it would only harm you, you should first read it a few times, get a superficial understanding of this technique, then I will teach you."

"Yes!"

Tofu Heavenly Venerate then turned and sought help from the Golden Finger, "Fairy, do you know how to perform Lion Roar?"

"What’s there not to know? The so-called Lion Roar is nothing but imitating the roar of lion-like Demon Beasts, bringing out the true essence of the roar to stun the opponent, just an ordinary technique."

"Can you teach me?" Lu Yang, clutching at straws, looked expectantly at Immortal Fairy Eternity.

"Lion-like Demon Beasts have never produced any notable characters, except for the Suan Ni of the Qiongqi Clan who are a bit capable; still, they haven’t even produced a Semi-Immortal."

Lu Yang had a request, and naturally, Immortal Fairy Eternity had to agree; however, since Lu Yang was a loyal officer of the Great Bean Dynasty, teaching him just an ordinary technique like Lion Roar wouldn’t do.

If he was to learn, he should learn the best!

"Look at Little Ling Lianyi and Xiao Baihu, their shouts are much stronger than Lion Roar."

"Dragon chants, phoenix calls, and tiger roars, huh?" Lu Yang nodded. It was indeed true, especially for the Qiongqi Clan. Despite being named ’poor,’ they had produced two Semi-Immortals, more than both the Dragon and Phoenix Tribes, truly powerful.

Whereas the Suan Ni among the lion-like Demon Beasts, was merely a branch of the Dragon Clan and truly not worth emulating.

"So, should I learn their calls?" Lu Yang asked, as dragon chants, phoenix calls, and tiger roars also sounded quite impressive.

Fairy Eternity shook her head, "Of course not, didn’t I just say that? The principle of the Lion Roar is to express the true essence of a lion’s roar. The stronger the lion you imitate, the more powerful the roar."

"So?" Lu Yang had a faint feeling of foreboding.

Fairy Eternity smiled without showing her teeth, revealing a clean smile, "Little Ling and the others are indeed strong, but none are as strong as I am. Little Yang, you just need to learn my voice!"

"Let me think, since they call it ’Lion Roar,’ then I’ll name this magic skill ’Fairy Shout.’

Fairy Eternity had never taught anyone before, so she could only think of a technique name on the spot.

Lu Yang: "..."

This technique’s name sounds like something I really don’t want to learn.

However, Lu Yang also admitted that Fairy’s analysis made sense. The more the subject of imitation resembled the original, the more powerful the roar would be.

He gritted his teeth, to become stronger, he decided to learn it!

As soon as Fairy Eternity heard that Lu Yang wanted to learn, she immediately beamed with joy and shooed away the unrelated people, "Go, go, go, all three of you, stay far away."

Qing Yan Venerable and the other two were chased to the edges of the Small World.

"Fairy, how should I learn?" Since he decided to learn, Lu Yang focused intensely on learning.

"Follow me, ha—" Fairy Eternity tried to open her mouth wide, like a kitten, pressing down her voice as she said "ha."

"Ha—" Lu Yang mimicked studiously.

"No, no, you need to imitate my voice, ha—"

"Like this? Ha—"

"Still not right." Fairy Eternity corrected him, pointing to her own mouth to demonstrate, "Pay attention to my mouth shape."

Lu Yang stared hard at Fairy Eternity’s mouth, trying to grasp every movement. The fairy’s mouth was very attractive, with red lips and white teeth, and her little tongue looked pink and tender.

"Stop staring at me all the time." Fairy Eternity pushed Lu Yang’s face away, her cheeks blushing, feeling somewhat odd.

Lu Yang wore a perplexed expression, wasn’t it you who asked me to watch?

Oh well, at His Majesty’s command, whatever he says, goes.

In just half a day, Lu Yang had learned ’Fairy Shout’. After all, spending his days with Fairy Eternity, learning her shouting voice wasn’t difficult.

"Ha—" Lu Yang emitted the voice of the Fairy, astonishingly powerful, the sound waves materialized, directly demolishing a mountaintop!

Then Lu Yang used his hands to form a horn shape, and deployed ’Fairy Shout’ again, the power increased a notch, even shaking the space!

Lu Yang was delighted, truly much more powerful than Buddhism’s ’Lion Roar’!

The only problem was that the voice wasn’t very intimidating.

"I didn’t lie to you, did I? This move is very powerful, it can immobilize the enemy and even has a suppressive effect on Demon Beasts. When the Demon Beasts hear my voice, I guarantee they all get scared and lie down!" Fairy Eternity said proudly, reminiscing how in the Ancient Times, any Demon Beast that heard her voice would immediately flee.

"Heavenly Venerate, I have reviewed it several times now, I think I understand the essence of the Lion Roar, can you teach it now?" Xin Ding closed the book with a sigh of relief, rubbing his temples, having finally understood the book after half a day’s effort.

Lu Yang muttered to himself worriedly, absorbed in learning ’Fairy Shout’, he had forgotten that Xin Ding was outside waiting for him.

Soon, from within the Green Peak Sword, the calm voice of Tofu Heavenly Venerable came, "Good comprehension, I’ll teach you the Transformation Technique first."

Chapter 1035: 1034: Chi Xiao Ancient Realm

"Transformation Technique?"

"Indeed, if one wants to learn Lion Roar, one must first imitate a lion and comprehend the true essence of a lion."

"So that’s how it is." Xin Ding suddenly understood, finding Tofu Heavenly Venerable’s words made a lot of sense.

Lu Yang released a Spiritual Sense imprint, which conjured a lively and vivid Suan Ni.

When Lu Yang had been in the Demon Territory, he had interacted with the Suan Ni species and remembered their ways of moving.

The conjured Suan Ni was awe-inspiring, leaping between mountains, roaring at heaven and earth as if it could swallow the sky with its wide-open mouth!

Xin Ding’s expression was in a trance, having never seen such a ferocious Demon Beast before!

"Observe this Suan Ni carefully!" came Tofu Heavenly Venerable’s voice beside his ear.

Tofu Heavenly Venerable’s voice carried a kind of unbelievable magic, which snapped Xin Ding back to reality, focusing solely on observing the Suan Ni.

Seizing the moment when Xin Ding was distracted, Lu Yang quickly used his Spiritual Sense to scan and study "Lion Roar."

The fairy was right; this was indeed a very simple magic skill, with principles not much different from "Fairy Shout." The only difference was the learning difficulty, which was much easier compared to "Fairy Shout."

Following the method written in "Lion Roar," Lu Yang formed hand seals and let out the most orthodox lion’s roar: "Roar—"

In passing, he lay down on the ground and transformed into a lion.

"..."

Lu Yang cradled his forehead with his lion’s paw, knowing something would go wrong.

Lu Yang reverted to human form and pondered, Xin Ding’s cultivation level was too low, learning Lion Roar directly was too difficult; was it really necessary to stick to the initial idea?

"Heavenly Venerable, I have finished observing."

"Good, then I shall teach you Lion Fist."

"Lion Fist?" Xin Ding had never heard of such a boxing technique.

"This boxing technique is of my own creation. When cultivated to perfection, one can transform into a real lion."

"Perfection?"

"You are still inexperienced in learning, and perfection is far from your reach. Do not aim too high; focus on mastering the basics before thinking of perfection."

"Yes."

Then, a figure as immaculate and white as tofu appeared before Xin Ding. The tofu-like figure posed, and in its punches and kicks were echoes of Suan Ni movements.

Lu Yang conjured a figure demonstrating Lion Form Boxing.

"Is this Lion Fist? It’s so powerful. If the boxing technique is perfected and I transform into a true lion, what a sight that will be!"

As the tofu figure’s movements became faster, it suddenly transformed into a Suan Ni, leaving Xin Ding lost in awe!

This was the perfection of Lion Fist!

Xin Ding earnestly began to learn, with Lu Yang, the founder, as his guide, and quickly got the hang of Lion Fist.

The fact proved Lu Yang was right. Imitating the lion’s movements before learning Lion Roar was much simpler.

It wasn’t long before Xin Ding could execute Lion Roar, and though it was nowhere near as effective as Lu Yang’s, it was sufficient for someone at the Qi Cultivation Stage.

...

"Xin Ding Senior Brother, have you heard? The Chi Xiao Ancient Realm is going to open, and we all can enter!" Xin Chen from the Southern Servant Institute, upon receiving the news, immediately came to tell Xin Ding.

"What is the Chi Xiao Ancient Realm?"

"The Chi Xiao Ancient Realm is a secret realm passed down from Ancient Times that only cultivators below the Foundation Establishment Stage are eligible to enter. For the sake of safety, the various powers usually require cultivators to be at least at the Seventh Layer of Qi Refinement and below Consummation of Qi Refinement. We can all enter the Ancient Realm!"

"Are there any benefits to entering the Ancient Realm?"

"Of course, there are benefits. Although the institute will issue a Foundation Establishment Pill when we reach Foundation Establishment, they only provide one. If you want to get more, you have to buy them with Merit Points."

"The Chi Xiao Ancient Realm is full of ancient Foundation Establishment Grass, which is the main ingredient for concocting ancient Foundation Establishment Pills. With the main ingredients, do you still need to worry about the quantity of Foundation Establishment Pills?"

"Besides the ancient Foundation Establishment Grass, at the deepest part of the Ancient Realm, there are various inheritances. If you can obtain any one of them, you will have no worries for your future cultivation!"

Xin Chen grew more excited as he spoke. If he were still in the Servants’ Institute, he definitely would not have had the chance to go to the Chi Xiao Ancient Realm. Now that he had come to the Martial Monk Institute, the benefits were apparent.

"It does sound like a good place." Xin Ding nodded. According to what Xin Chen had said, all the Qi Cultivation Stage Martial Monks from the institute would go, so he didn’t really have another option.

After Xin Chen left, Xin Ding asked, "Heavenly Venerate, do you know about the Chi Xiao Ancient Realm?"

"This Venerable has not heard of this secret realm. Since it originated from Ancient Times, it seems that its original name was lost over the long years, and it was given a new name by later generations."

"With this Venerable here, there is no harm in going."

These were the words Lu Yang conveyed from Immortal Fairy.

"Alright."

...

The day to enter the Chi Xiao Ancient Realm soon arrived.

The Chi Xiao Ancient Realm was a famous secret realm in Buddha Country. Apart from the Western Paradise Temple, many other major powers also sent their Qi Cultivation Stage disciples. Among them, Xin Ding’s seventh layer Qi Cultivation Realm was inconspicuous and went unnoticed.

"The people from Dujiang Temple have arrived!"

"It’s the Nianci Temple contingent!"

"Look, the Heavenly Vault Holy Land has come too!" someone shouted as a group of ethereal, fairy-like women in white robes flew in on a boat, attracting everyone’s attention upon their arrival.

"Isn’t that Yu Youlan from the Heavenly Vault Holy Land? I’ve heard she has an Immortal body and cultivates at an astonishing speed, unrivaled among her peers, deeply favored by the Holy Land’s patriarch. She’s bound to be on the three lists of heaven, earth, and men in the future!"

Yu Youlan’s face was veiled, revealing a pair of charming peach blossom eyes. Although she was young, it was evident that she would grow up to be a beauty and had many admirers.

Even the Martial Monks who had come with Xin Ding could not help showing a slightly infatuated look upon seeing Yu Youlan, but Xin Ding alone recited scriptures silently, unaffected by her beauty.

Yu Youlan showed no expression; she was all too familiar with her own allure. She inadvertently noticed Xin Ding, who was lowering his head reciting scriptures, and a trace of interest appeared on the corner of her mouth.

"Hmm, a little monk that’s quite interesting."

"Heavenly Vault Holy Land? Immortal recalls that there was a Holy Land with this name during Ancient Times," Immortal Fairy said, remembering that the Heavenly Vault Holy Land was quite a force back in the Ancient Times, with two Pass Tribulation Cultivators who were eligible to attend lectures given by the Five Immortals.

"Qing Yan, come over and tell me about it?"

"Reporting to the Immortal, it is said that the Heavenly Vault Holy Land’s patriarch came into possession of half an ancient inheritance, which allowed them to rise from obscurity and establish the Holy Land. Moreover, this ancient inheritance originated from a place known as the Heavenly Vault Holy Land."

"No wonder it sounded familiar; it turns out there is an inheritance from that Heavenly Vault Holy Land."

"It’s rumored that there’s another part of the Heavenly Vault Holy Land’s inheritance within the Chi Xiao Ancient Realm. Every time the Ancient Realm opens, the Holy Land sends people to try to obtain the inheritance, but they’ve never succeeded."

With Elder Qing Yan, who knew everything in Buddha Country, many things were convenient to understand.

As the Ancient Realm opened, Xin Ding entered with the Green Peak Sword.

The Green Peak Sword Small World could shield causality, and naturally, it could also shield the Ancient Realm’s probes, easily deceiving the Chi Xiao Ancient Realm.

"So this is the place," realized Immortal Fairy upon entering the Ancient Realm.

Chapter 1036: 1035: The Interesting Little Monk

"Fairy, where is this place?"

"Hasn’t Immortal already told you? In the Heavenly Vault Holy Land, only two at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage are qualified to listen to Yingtian Immortal’s lectures. Besides sharing his cultivation experience, Yingtian Immortal mainly talks about how to maintain composure and style," the Fairy explained.

"After the lecture, one must complete the assignment. People from a few other Holy Lands in the Heavenly Vault Holy Land have teamed up to create this Secret Realm, which only Cultivators below the Foundation Establishment Stage can enter."

"They pretend to be Cultivators at the Qi Cultivation Stage and enter the Secret Realm as well, but once inside, they do not use their own power but rather rely on the Realm’s power to pass through."

"Then, what was the original name of this Secret Realm?" Lu Yang asked.

"Assignment One."

.....

"Just as I thought, I’ve been separated from them." Xin Ding appeared all alone in the dense forest of the Chi Xiao Ancient Realm, unable to even discern directions.

"According to Junior Brother Xin Chen’s words, the locations after entering the Chi Xiao Ancient Realm are random. If lucky, some acquaintances might be transported to the same place. It seems my luck wasn’t good."

"The Chi Xiao Ancient Realm is divided into an outer layer and an inner layer. The outer layer has some ordinary opportunities, while the inner layer has a higher chance of encountering special opportunities. However, the inner layer is also more dangerous."

"There are two methods of departure: automatic departure after three days, or choose to leave after obtaining an opportunity... Who’s there!"

Xin Ding, sensing something, turned and demanded loudly. After cultivating Lion Roar, his voice naturally carried a commanding presence.

"This little monk has quite the sensitive perception. Is it that you have specially cultivated your Spiritual Sense?"

Accompanied by the rustling of the bushes, a young girl in white appeared, like a Youlan orchid in a secluded valley, completely out of sync with the surrounding atmosphere.

Yu Youlan looked at Xin Ding with a smile that was not quite a smile, finding the coincidence quite amusing.

"So it’s the Benefactor Yu Youlan from the Heavenly Vault Holy Land," Xin Ding greeted with palms together, feeling relieved. He had heard that this Holy Daughter was whimsical and quirky but had no bad intentions; she was someone he could associate with.

Yu Youlan quietly observed Xin Ding’s reaction to her presence and discovered that the little monk’s gaze was clear, and when looking at her, there was no trace of impure thoughts—a rarity indeed.

"Alright, little monk, I have something to do, so take care of yourself."

Yu Youlan hadn’t forgotten her mission; she came to the Ancient Realm fully prepared, with a thirty percent chance of entering the inner layer to find the other half of the Holy Land’s heritage.

"Amitābha."

Yu Youlan smiled sweetly at Xin Ding and then went on her way.

As she weaved through the jungle, running for a while, she couldn’t shake the feeling that someone was following her. She stopped and hid in a treetop, on guard for the newcomer.

Before long, Xin Ding passed by.

"Little monk, are you following me?"

Yu Youlan was surprised, having thought this monk was virtuous; why would he resort to tailing her?

"Benefactor, you misunderstand. I’m merely heading toward the inner layer, and it only happens that our paths coincide," said Xin Ding.

"Coincide?" Yu Youlan eyed him skeptically, not believing his words. The shortest and safest route from the outer to the inner layer was a secret their Holy Land had only recently unraveled. How could Western Paradise Temple possibly have figured it out?

"You go ahead."

Without any hesitation, Xin Ding continued onward, and Yu Youlan followed him.

Could it really be the same route I intend to take?!

After following for a while, Yu Youlan began to believe what Xin Ding had said.

"Little monk, how did you know to take this route?"

"The Buddha said, ’Not to be spoken of,’" replied Xin Ding, who dared not reveal anything about Tofu Heavenly Venerable, as it involved matters of great causality and the potential destruction of Western Paradise Temple.

"Tell me," pressed Yu Youlan with a sweet, coaxing voice that could easily make any weak-willed cultivator bow to her demands.

"Not to be spoken of."

"An interesting little monk," Yu Youlan commented, her interest in Xin Ding increasing. She had been born with a silver spoon and chosen by the Patriarch to be the Holy Daughter, unmatched in talent and background. She had never failed to get what she wanted.

She was eager to see what other abilities this little monk had!

The two shared the same route, chatting and laughing along the way.

Mainly, it was Yu Youlan who talked and laughed on her own, while Xin Ding rarely responded.

The more Yu Youlan interacted with him, the more she felt that this little monk was quite extraordinary. No matter how she tried to probe and hint, he remained tight-lipped about the accurate insights he held about Lu Yang.

"Youlan, I didn’t expect you to be here as well," said the newcomer.

As the two were chatting, a group of people emerged from the forest. The leader was dignified and majestic-looking. The only imperfection was that the hem of his clothes was slightly torn as if he had been through a fierce battle recently.

The situation outside was not entirely peaceful. The presence of demon beasts and malevolent spirits would cause disturbances. The reason Xin Ding and his companion had not run into any so far was that they had been following the safest route.

Xin Ding recognized the leader; it was Gao Tianxiu, the Holy Son of Tuo Luo Holy Land. The others must be his followers.

"Greetings to Benefactor Gao."

Gao Tianxiu frowned slightly. He hadn’t expected Yu Youlan to be accompanied by someone. While Yu Youlan appeared affable and approachable to outsiders, upon interaction, one would discover that she kept everyone at a great distance, allowing no one to come close to her.

Why was this ordinary-looking, averagely-cultivated little monk so close to Yu Youlan?

"Who among you knows this little monk?" he asked one of his followers.

"Holy Son, this monk should be Xin Ding from the Western Paradise Temple. I heard he was originally a Servant Monk who fortuitously entered the Martial Monk Institute."

"So he is just a Servant Monk." Gao Tianxiu sneered, having thought the monk had some impressive background.

"Gao Tianxiu, I’ve told you many times before, we aren’t that close, and you shouldn’t address me by name!" Yu Youlan said, somewhat annoyed. Gao Tianxiu was like a clingy plaster that she couldn’t shake off.

"Youlan, I’m just worried about you. This Ancient Realm is fraught with dangers. We’ve already encountered more than one batch of demon beasts and malevolent spirits. It wouldn’t be good if someone alone like you got deceived or ran into some danger," he said.

As he spoke, Gao Tianxiu’s gaze drifted towards Xin Ding, his implication clear.

"The concern of Holy Son Gao is appreciated, but my affairs are really none of your business!"

"It seems Benefactor Gao has some misunderstandings about me."

"Little monk, beat it. You have no place here!"

"Benefactor Gao, I’m afraid that would be asking too much of me."

"It seems you prefer punishment over a toast!"

Gao Tianxiu let out a sinister chuckle as the full aura of his Consummation of Qi Refinement erupted. The group of followers behind him also released their auras, revealing themselves to be on the eighth or ninth levels of Qi Refinement. Any of them alone was more highly-cultivated than Xin Ding!

"What are you all trying to do!" Yu Youlan exclaimed angrily, stepping forward to protect Xin Ding. She was no match if all these people were to rush them at once.

"Youlan, this is a battle between men. You shouldn’t interfere!"

Gao Tianxiu, confident of victory, pressed forward towards Xin Ding. With more than a dozen people at the Qi Cultivation Stage, he believed it impossible for Xin Ding to escape!

"Benefactor Gao, neither toasts nor penalties are for me to consume."

"Brave kid, let’s all go at him together!"

As the crowd moved to surround him and there was no escape, Xin Ding clasped his hands together and sighed. Spiritual Energy surged, the space around twisted, and the trees seemed to come to life, roots drilling out and branches growing wildly, entangling Gao Tianxiu and his men like a demonic dance.

"What sorcery is this!" the crowd exclaimed in shock. How could the trees transform? Since the discovery of Chi Xiao Ancient Realm, they had never heard of such a thing!

To them, with their cultivation levels and their numbers, the suddenly animated ancient trees were meaningless.

Xin Ding placed one hand on his chest and gently pressed down with the other. Space obeyed his command as an invisible giant hand pressed down, pinning Gao Tianxiu and the others wrapped by the trees to the ground with a thud!

Xin Ding remained calm and unruffled, approaching Gao Tianxiu.

Gao Tianxiu struggled to lift his head, his eyes filled with immense fear. The light shining on Xin Ding’s head obscured his features, making him appear both divine and demonic.

"Benefactor Gao, I am a monk; I do not drink alcohol."

"Benefactor Yu, let us continue on our way."

"Ah, oh." Yu Youlan snapped out of her daze. She hadn’t expected such a turn of events either.

Xin Ding’s performance just now was akin to him being the master of the Ancient Realm!

Within the Green Blade Sword Small World, the Tofu Heavenly Venerable was half delighted and half vexed.

He was pleased with Xin Ding’s performance.

"The problem is, when I went to the Ancient Realm before, why didn’t I encounter such opportunities?"

Chapter 1037: 1036: Fast Pass

Yu Youlan closely followed Xin Ding, her interest in the little monk growing ever more intense.

"We’re here."

Standing in front of a stone gate tens of meters high, Xin Ding was surrounded by the incredibly precious Foundation Establishment Grass from ancient times. This was precisely Xin Ding’s secret for coming to the Ancient Realm. He bent down and picked ten plants, enough to refine ten Foundation Establishment Pills.

With them, even those with the poorest aptitude could reach the Foundation Establishment Stage.

Outside the dense, primordial forests of the Ancient Times, Chi Xiao Ancient Realm’s inner layer held a majestic hall carved from a single small planet—a feat only possible during the Ancient Times. The modern Cultivation World no longer had the means to create such wonders.

"So this is the Ancient Stone Hall inside," Yu Youlan exclaimed. It was her first visit to the Chi Xiao Ancient Realm, and she hadn’t expected the Ancient Stone Hall to be so grand. Even the palace of the elder patriarch at her Holy Land was not as imposing.

"However, such a vast Ancient Stone Hall is just for testing those at the Qi Cultivation Stage?" A question surfaced in Yu Youlan’s mind. Were cultivators at the Qi Cultivation Stage worthy of such a Secret Realm?

Xin Ding shared her doubts and decided to consult the Tofu Heavenly Venerable. He knew how to enter the inner circle and how to harness the hidden powers of the Ancient Realm, all thanks to the Tofu Heavenly Venerable’s guidance.

"Heavenly Venerable, do you know why this Ancient Realm was built in the first place?"

In the world, what secrets were unknown to the Tofu Heavenly Venerable? "Heh, the origin of this Secret Realm is hardly worth mentioning; it was simply constructed by a few juniors at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage in order to pass the tests set by Immortals."

Juniors at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, tests by Immortals!

Xin Ding was astonished; the Heavenly Venerable could always reveal his glorious past with just a few words.

Lu Yang wasn’t lying. As the Immortal Lineage Second Boss, his stature was immensely respected. By the hierarchy, the two at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage from the Heavenly Vault Holy Land were juniors in front of him.

The sealed stone gate opened, and the two entered the Ancient Stone Hall.

Each time the Ancient Realm was accessed, only ten percent of those at the Qi Cultivation Stage could enter the Ancient Stone Hall. On average, entry happened on the third day, so the progress of the two was already far beyond everyone else’s.

Yu Youlan blinked at Xin Ding, "Inside this Ancient Stone Hall, there are eight Sub-Halls, each with its own challenges. Only by passing all the challenges can one enter the Inner Hall and obtain the inheritance!"

"It’s difficult for you to overcome all the challenges alone. Why don’t we cooperate?" she suggested.

Xin Ding calmly shook his head, "Benefactor Yu is mistaken."

"Mistaken?" Yu Youlan found that hard to believe. This was the pattern that generations of Qi Cultivation Stage cultivators from her Holy Land had summarized through trials; how could it be wrong?

"The eight Sub-Halls of this Ancient Stone Hall are arranged according to the scene of the Innate Eight Diagrams. One only needs to pass through the Kan, Li, and Gen directional Sub-Halls in order to enter the Inner Hall."

"Is that really so?" Yu Youlan instinctively doubted this, but considering Xin Ding’s confident assertion together with his previous actions, she thought what he said must be true.

Xin Ding didn’t insist on Yu Youlan believing him; it was enough for him to believe in himself.

Without paying further attention to Yu Youlan, Xin Ding resolutely headed towards the Kanfen Hall.

"Hey, little monk, wait for me. Let’s go together," Yu Youlan decided to trust the little monk.

The two arrived at Kanfen Hall, where various magic artifacts were wrapped in bubbles, floating in the air. Each one was precious to those at the Golden Core Stage and extremely tempting for those at the Qi Cultivation Stage.
