"Netherworld Blood Sea!" Divine Monarch Sichen summoned a blood-red ocean, the stench of blood piercing the heavens. During the Great Qian Period, human life was cheap, and it was easy to cultivate the Netherworld Blood Sea.

Haoran Buddhism clashed with the blood-red ocean, and Divine Monarch Sichen could not withstand the two adversaries, quickly finding himself at a disadvantage!

"We’re under attack!"

The Western Paradise Temple descended into chaos, its monks unable to believe that someone would dare to attack their sanctuary.

"Great Brightness Spell!"

"Vajra Pestle!"

Ming Huai and Ming Tian saw their attacks taking effect and pressed their advantage, effortlessly unleashing various Demon Suppressing Magic Spells. However, as the battle progressed, Divine Monarch Sichen’s attacks began to gain the upper hand, and they soon appeared overwhelmed.

"What’s going on? Why does it feel like I can’t muster any strength?" Master Tomorrow spoke, his voice conveying a sense of exhaustion.

"You’ve finally noticed?" Divine Monarch Sichen laughed, his Time Dao Fruit Outline granting him the ability to accelerate the depletion of an individual’s physical strength. Even those at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage couldn’t withstand such exhaustion. Before his Time Dao Fruit Outline, they had no right to fight him!

Only a Semi-Immortal could match him.

Using the Dao Fruit Outline required minimal energy expenditure, almost unaffected by the draining of physical strength.

"The two of you fall back. I’ll fight him!" The Unmoving Ming King Dharma aspect flew out as Master Mingyu decisively made his move, brandishing the Wisdom Sword.

Masters Ming Huai and Ming Tian clenched their teeth, knowing that even if they stayed on the battlefield, they would only be a burden to their Senior Brother, and turned to fly downward.

"Hurry and leave the Western Paradise Temple!"

A battle between Semi-Immortals could obliterate everything; the vast Western Paradise Temple was likely beyond salvation.

"What leave the Western Paradise Temple? We need to hurry and get as far away from Western Heaven City as possible!" Master Ming Huai bellowed, knowing that in a battle of Semi-Immortals, the farther one fled, the better.

The Wisdom Sword swung down, aiming to split Divine Monarch Sichen in two!

Divine Monarch Sichen moved as though he had anticipated Master Mingyu’s action, stepping aside just in time to avoid the strike.

Another ability of the Time Dao Fruit Outline: foresight into the future.

The future is uncertain, and predictions further in the future are more likely to be inaccurate.

But conversely, predicting the near future, such as the very next moment, is much more accurate!

"Is this your Dao Fruit Outline?" After dodging the attack, Divine Monarch Sichen no longer underestimated Master Mingyu, as that previous sword strike clearly possessed power beyond what those in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage could wield.

"Fairy, what kind of Dao Fruit Outline does Master Mingyu have?"

Fairy Eternity furrowed her brows, tilted her head left and right in confusion, "It’s not one of the Dao Fruit Outlines that appeared in Ancient Times, I don’t recognize it."

No one would know what Master Mingyu’s Dao Fruit Outline was.

Because this was a Dao Fruit Outline that had never appeared before, Master Mingyu named it the Unmoving Ming King Dao Fruit Outline.

Should he overcome the Immortal Ascension Tribulation, upon becoming an immortal, Master Mingyu would deservedly be known as the Unmoving Ming King, capable of all the legendary powers associated with the Unmoving Ming King in Buddhist scriptures!

"Demon-Subduing Pestle!"

The Unmoving Ming King Dharma aspect wielded the Demon-Subduing Pestle; a strike could mean death or severe injury to anyone at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage. Divine Monarch Sichen too would be gravely wounded if hit.

The two Semi-Immortals fought amid the clouds, their forms flickering in and out of sight, throwing past and present into disarray as if ancient myths were coming to life once again!

"He seems to be nearing the Consummation of his Time Dao Fruit Outline," observed Fairy Eternity after watching for a while.

"Nearing Consummation?" Lu Yang was stunned — didn’t that mean Divine Monarch Sichen was qualified to become an immortal?

"However, the way he’s using it seems clumsy, not like something he has condensed on his own. Could it be that he forcibly fused it through incense offerings of faith?"

Chapter 1047: 1046: Borrowing the Incense of Buddha Country

The Incense Faith forcibly converged an initial form of the dao fruit. The Shameless Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit was formed this way.

"A nearly consummated initial form of the Time Dao Fruit? Doesn’t that mean he can sense the suppression of the Dao Fruit?"

Lu Yang remembered something and took out three Seeking Immortal Tokens he had seized from Great Qian Cultivators.

Originally, there were four, but since the Yingtian Immortal had already been found, there was no need to carry it.

The Seeking Immortal Tokens were quite strange. Even though the Yingtian Immortal hid in the Ancient Ring’s Small World, making Ashy Bean unable to detect him, the Ghost Monarch holding the Seeking Immortal Token could sense the location of the Yingtian Immortal.

"Could it be that the Seeking Immortal Tokens are made from nearly consummated initial forms of Dao Fruits?"

With the power of the Great Qian Dynasty, it is entirely capable of forcibly converging four Dao Fruit Outlines, leveraging the rule of ’Dao Fruit’s suppression over its initial form’ to track back the Four Ancient Immortals.

"If that’s the case, then the user of the Time Dao Fruit..." Figuring out the method of the Seeking Immortal Tokens’ creation, Lu Yang quickly thought of another issue.

"Heavenly Venerate, can you make a move?" Xin Ding’s shouting voice interrupted Lu Yang’s train of thoughts, the voice anxious, very concerned for Master Mingyu’s safety.

Xin Ding couldn’t intervene in the battle between demi-immortals; her only reliance was the mysterious and unpredictable Tofu Heavenly Venerate who had always been aiding her.

Although Tofu Heavenly Venerate had said that once he took action, the entire Western Paradise Temple would be reduced to ruins.

But now the problem is even if Tofu Heavenly Venerate doesn’t make a move, Western Paradise Temple is about to be reduced to ruins anyway!

...

In the eyes of Specter Monarch Sichen, the future was clear. Although Master Mingyu’s attacks were powerful, they were futile if they missed!

"And even if you could hit me, what about it?"

Specter Monarch Sichen voluntarily took a hit, protected in front by the Sky Opening Axe, and accurately blocked the Wisdom Sword of the Unmoving Ming King with the axe face, merely stepping back a few steps and feeling a numbness in his hand, without sustaining any actual damage.

The greatest weakness of Specter Monarch Sichen was his lack of effective attack and defense methods, a gap filled by the presence of the Sky Opening Axe; now he could truly claim his cultivation was complete, with no flaws to speak of.

Even if he couldn’t discern what Ming Yu’s Dao Fruit’s initial form was, his attack methods simply could not affect him!

"But it is indeed bothersome, always blocking my way." Master Mingyu was no easy opponent to deal with, and despite several attempts by Specter Monarch Sichen to leave the Western Paradise Temple with the Sky Opening Axe, he was consistently stopped by Master Mingyu.

Even though Specter Monarch Sichen could foresee the future, facing Master Mingyu’s net-like attacks, he struggled to escape.

"If you won’t let me escape, then don’t regret it!"

Specter Monarch Sichen changed his attack direction and headed straight for the monks and commoners of Western Heaven City who had not yet escaped, exhaling green poisonous gas.

"Five Poison Scripture!"

If inhaled, a Divine Transformation Stage Cultivator wouldn’t withstand a moment and would dissolve into pus, let alone cultivators below the Divine Transformation Stage.

Seeing this scene, Master Mingyu’s eyes widened with anger, and his speed increased a notch, directly rushing in front of Specter Monarch Sichen and spreading his Dharma Aspect to block the poison gas.

"How could he be this fast?!" Specter Monarch Sichen had not expected Master Mingyu to seemingly transform, his speed and strength greatly enhanced, and the Unmoving Ming King Dharma solidifying further.

In Buddhist legends, the Unmoving Ming King is a Dharma Protector God with the duty to protect believers. Reflected on the initial form of the Dao Fruit, it meant the more people he protected, the stronger his power became.

"Buddha Light Shines Everywhere!"

The Dharma Aspect released Buddha Light, like the fierce sun, and the poison gas hissed under the Buddha Light.

The Unmoving Ming King also has legends of curing diseases, with believers praying to the Unmoving Ming King for health.

"Not good!" Specter Monarch Sichen, caught off guard, was enveloped by the Buddha Light and unexpectedly immobilized on the spot, unable to move.

"Bodhi Freedom Heaven!"

The Dharma Aspect of the Unmoving Ming King, with eyes wide in anger, raised the Demon Suppressing Vajra Pestle high, surrounded by golden Buddha flames on the vajra pestle.

With this strike, Specter Monarch Sichen was doomed to die!

"The flow of time in the world slowed down!"

"The flow of my own time sped up!"

Divine Monarch Sichen continuously used two abilities of the Initial Form of Time Dao Fruit, making his speed reach an extreme level. To him, the world seemed as if it had stopped.

"Golden Cicada shedding its skin!"

He performed the escape technique, leaving behind a shell, while his true self took the opportunity to break free from the shackles of Buddha Light.

Boom—

The forceful strike of Master Mingyu hit nothing but air, only destroying a mere shell.

"You’re courting death, junior!" Divine Monarch Sichen no longer played along—if not for his quick reaction, he would have perished at the Western Paradise Temple.

Divine Monarch Sichen swung the Sky Opening Axe towards the Dharma Aspect, directly carving a huge hole in its chest!

And this was only a fraction of the power of the Sky Opening Axe!

The two clashed again, and despite Master Mingyu becoming stronger under the "Protection" blessing, he still failed to inflict any real damage on Divine Monarch Sichen.

"Senior Brother couldn’t be about to lose, could he?" Master Mingtian said anxiously, not expecting his extremely powerful Senior Brother Mingyu to be pressured so hard.

"That involves the Initial Form of Time Dao Fruit," sighed Master Minghuai with a heavy heart. Causality and time were the trickiest magic skills, and at the stage of Dao Fruit Outline, this was still the case, with nobody wanting to face a Semi-Immortal possessor of a Time-Type Dao Fruit Outline.

"Moreover, the biggest problem with Senior Brother’s Dao Fruit Outline is that he has no specialty. Being well-rounded means having weaknesses all around, making it difficult to gain the upper hand against Time-Type Dao Fruit Outlines."

Boom—

After hundreds of exchanges, the Immovable Ming King Dharma Form, like a battered kite, was struck by the Sky Opening Axe and sent flying backward, smashing through countless buildings of the Western Heaven Temple, all the way to the Chaos Forbidden Land.

After experiencing such intense combat, the chaotic fog of the Forbidden Land had almost dissipated. Master Mingyu clutched his chest; the Immovable Ming King was fabled to have healing powers, but that was no match for Divine Monarch Sichen’s relentless attacks.

Divine Monarch Sichen stood triumphantly, feeling like an omnipotent immortal at that moment, capable of dictating the fates of others. "It’s time to flatten this so-called Chaos Forbidden Land and Western Heaven Secret Realm!"

He noticed Xin Zhen and Xin Ding hiding in a pit, his lips curling into a cruel smile: "You’re included in this end."

Divine Monarch Sichen wound up the Sky Opening Axe, faintly reenacting the primordial scene of Sky Opening, making heaven and earth tremble!

"Sky Opening!"

"Senior Brother!" Master Minghuai and Master Mingtian screamed loudly, wishing they could instantly teleport to block the strike, but they were too far off and couldn’t make it in time.

Clang!

Against all expectations, something wrapped in rag flew out and collided with the Sky Opening Axe, astonishingly blocking the strike!

The rags turned to ashes upon impact, revealing an unparalleled Immortal Sword.

"Who goes there!"

The figure wearing a tofu mask flew out with the Green Peak Sword, smiling at Divine Monarch Sichen: "Hehe, junior, I have some connections with Yingtian Immortal. If you surrender now and return the Sky Opening Axe, I might spare your life."

"A mere Divine Transformation Stage Cultivator dares to speak so arrogantly, truly unaware of how to spell ’death’!" Divine Monarch Sichen froze for a moment, but as soon as he discerned Lu Yang’s cultivation level, he realized he had been bluffed.

"It seems you do not agree," the man in the tofu mask sighed, deeply disappointed by Divine Monarch Sichen’s response, "I am entangled in causality and prefer not to fight, why must you press me?"

The man looked up, his eyes calm under the mask, and slowly said:

"Very well, I shall borrow the incense of the Buddha Country."

"I vow here to resurrect the Worldly Venerable."

Chapter 1048: 1047: Ascending to Immortality in One Step

The entire Buddha Country was roused by a mysterious force, and began to tremble slightly, as if a great beast was struggling underground, with the shaking becoming more intense.

Sand flew about, and dust filled the air; from the abbots of the twelve top temples in the Buddha Country to the commoners at dawn, all were utterly unaware of what was happening.

A dazzling light surged up from beneath the sand dunes, reaching for the sky without harming a person or damaging anything, piercing through the scorching sunlight and aiming straight for the heavens.

If a great power were to fly into the cosmos and look down upon the Buddha Country, they would discover countless beams of light scattered throughout the land, extending infinitely, bending, and converging towards the center of the Buddha Country, the Western Paradise Temple.

This had never happened before in all of history!

"It’s a supreme vow, someone has fulfilled a supreme vow!" Finally, the oldest monk from the Transcendance Tribulation Stage in the Buddha Country recalled a familiar scene; this was the sign of fulfilling a supreme vow as recorded in the ancient scriptures.

A hundred thousand years ago, the Master of Righteous Virtue made the supreme vow to "spread Buddhist teachings throughout the Central Continent."

To fulfill the supreme vow, Master of Righteous Virtue established the Suspension Temple, which became the source of Great Xia’s Buddhist teachings. From that time, countless temples rose from the ground, appearing in Great Xia and forming a force alongside the sects.

When Master of Righteous Virtue fulfilled the supreme vow, the Buddha Country shook, and it was recorded in the annals of history.

However, today’s event was far grander than when Master of Righteous Virtue had fulfilled his supreme vow, to the extent that the ancient Transcendance Tribulation Stage monk did not associate it with this event at first.

"Who exactly made such a supreme vow, and how was it fulfilled?" The monk’s lips trembled, his face awash with shock, fully aware of the difficulty in fulfilling a supreme vow, an almost impossible feat. Yet the reality before his eyes was that indeed, someone had accomplished it.

Beyond the shock was more a feeling of fortune.

Fortunate to witness this scene in his lifetime.

An endless stream of incense and fire poured toward Lu Yang, and in that moment, Lu Yang’s soul and flesh rapidly ascended to an inconceivable realm!

An Immortal’s Body!

In a place no one could see, an initial form of the Immortal Dao Fruit merged into the immortal body.

It was the one Fairy Eternity had sent out during the New Year!

Lu Yang, feeling the body overflowing with boundless power, knew that this enhancement was only temporary. Once he left the Buddha Country, he would return to his original state.

But within the Buddha Country, supported by the power of incense and fire, he was an Immortal!

Just as he predicted, he was the most orthodox of Buddhist cultivators. For Buddhism, the existence of Fairy Eternity was crucial. Having revived Fairy Eternity, he naturally received endless incense and fire.

Lu Yang slowly opened his eyes, and a cold light flashed through his pupils, startling the Divine Monarch Sichen so much that he shivered uncontrollably.

"You brat from Great Qian, why must you force this venerable one to take action?"

The Divine Monarch Sichen trembled with fear, unable to believe that the person who had just been in the Divinity Transformation Stage instantaneously elevated his realm to an unbelievable height.

It was his own complacency during the heat of the battle that inflated his ego. He thought he was invincible and didn’t regard anyone else as a threat.

How could anyone wielding an Immortal Sword capable of blocking the Sky Opening Axe’s attacks be an ordinary individual?!

Who exactly was the opponent, and what did he mean by reviving the Worldly Venerable?

The Divine Monarch Sichen’s mind descended into chaos, losing the ability to think.

"Who... who exactly are you?"

"Buddha Land in Palm." Lu Yang spoke the four words coldly, and even these simple words exerted tremendous pressure.

With the Immortal Body, the Small Town in his palm finally transformed into the Buddha Land in Palm.

The Divine Monarch Sichen, able to foresee the future, saw a future filled with despair.

Lu Yang pointed to the sky, instantly pressing the Divine Monarch Sichen to the ground. Sichen let out a wretched scream as he was pressed into the earth, his limbs shattered, in an extremely gruesome state.

"I’ll go, I’ll go right now!" Divine Monarch Si Chen screamed in horror, knowing the future was useless, facing absolute suppression, he couldn’t escape at all!

Divine Monarch Si Chen got up from the ground, his limbs instantly recovering, trying to flee the place.

Now, to him, the Western Paradise Temple was hell, a zone of forbidden life, a place one must not set foot in. He wanted to flee as far as possible!

"World time, slow down!"

"Personal time, speed up!"

Divine Monarch Si Chen desperately used the Initial Form of the Time Dao Fruit. He only resented that it was formed with the Power of Incense and Fire, not something he had comprehended himself, unable to unleash its full potential.

Facing the slowed time, Lu Yang remained unmoved. Not a ripple stirred in his eyes, and he simply said in an unchanging voice from ancient times, "Did I say you could go?"

"One Sword Becomes Ten Thousand."

Lu Yang gently shook the Green Peak Sword, which transformed into countless Green Peak Swords. All the swords in Western Heaven City and the nearby major cities heeded the call, flying towards the Western Paradise Temple.

These originally ordinary swords, after being baptized by the Immortal Sword Energy, improved in quality by one or even several major grades!

Looking around, the entire Western Heaven City was covered with swords, on the ground, in the sky, densely packed.

This could no longer be described as a Swordsmanship Domain; it should be called a Swordsmanship World!

Trapped in a world of swords, where could Divine Monarch Si Chen possibly flee to?

Shadows of swords flew chaotically, completely unavoidable. He tried to use the Sky Opening Axe to defend, but if he could block one sword, how could he block a thousand, ten thousand?

With every sword that flew by, a piece of Divine Monarch Si Chen’s flesh was sliced off. As a transcendent in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, the missing flesh would instantly regrow.

But this ability instead plunged him into endless agony.

The world’s time slowed, and his perception of pain increased exponentially, his screams unceasing.

Divine Monarch Si Chen wanted to poison civilians to distract Master Ming Yu. For such a person, Lu Yang had no need to hold back.

Xin Ding looked up, dumbstruck by the spectacle. Was this the true power of Tofu Heavenly Venerable? So formidable, so terrifying.

Ye Zijin, who was mingling in the crowd, was so shocked that he couldn’t close his mouth.

How could he not recognize the Green Peak Sword when others did not?

"No wonder Eldest Senior Sister wasn’t worried about Junior Brother’s safety."

Fairy Eternity lived in the Spiritual Space, and with Lu Yang’s arrival in the outside world, only Qing Yan and two others remained in the Green Blade Sword Small World.

They trembled, their hearts pounding violently. It seemed they knew something tremendously serious. Could they be silenced?

"Kneel."

Though Lu Yang’s voice was not loud, it clearly spread throughout the entire Western Heaven City.

The command to kneel was like a Verbal Spell. Divine Monarch Si Chen felt an unnamed force pressing down on his head, and uncontrollably, his body fell forward, thumping onto his knees.

"Leave, leave me alive, please... otherwise my lord and The Controller will not let you go!" Divine Monarch Si Chen blurted out in fear, unsure if he was begging or threatening.

He had always had an easy life, designated by The Controller as the monarch guiding time. After gaining control over the Time Dao Fruit Outline, his rise was meteoric, demanding wind and getting wind, calling for rain and receiving it. In the Great Qian Dynasty, wherever he went, he was a revered figure, never having encountered such a situation before.

Divine Monarch Si Chen knelt on the ground, the Sky Opening Axe cast aside. Lu Yang walked over leisurely, radiating Dao Charm, as if heaven and earth vibrated with his steps.

Lu Yang picked up the Sky Opening Axe, his eyes filled with a chill that hadn’t thawed for ten thousand years.

"Considering I’ve taken action bearing tremendous causality this time, do you think I will let you live?"

Chapter 1049: 1048 Self-Detonation

The arena was deathly silent; no one had anticipated that Si Chen God Lord, who mastered the outline of the Time Dao Fruit, would fall to the Mysterious Immortal like leaves before the autumn wind.

The once unrivaled Si Chen God Lord didn’t even have the chance to retaliate, a stark contrast from his previous demeanor!

Lu Yang held the Green Peak Sword in his left hand and the Sky Opening Axe in his right, wielding two killing immortal weapons, his murderous intent soaring to the skies.

It was certain he would take Si Chen God Lord back, but it was more troublesome to bring him alive. Rather than that, it was better to just kill him, store his body in the Green Blade Sword Small World, and lock his soul in the Spiritual Space without missing anything.

Just as Lu Yang was about to make his move, a splendid divine talisman appeared on Si Chen God Lord’s forehead, evolving into an emperor who ruled over the world.

"Leave some leeway in what you do, why must you insist on pushing so hard?" he said.

"Why don’t you consider it for my sake and spare Si Chen’s life? I will apologize on his behalf for any wrongdoings he has committed."

"Mid-heaven Emperor Lord?" Lu Yang’s eyes narrowed slightly, it seemed that the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord had left a contingency within Si Chen God Lord, ready to act in times of danger.

"Is that the legendary Mid-heaven Emperor Lord?" The crowd outside Western Heaven City was stirred up, never expecting the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord himself to intervene.

"Just an avatar, and it also has the right to apologize for him?"

Seeing that Lu Yang was not giving him any face, the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord stopped pretending and took a harsher stance.

"Just now, your excellency mentioned taking action despite bearing immense causality, presumably your excellency is entangled with causality, and hasty actions will incur the punishment of causality. Under such circumstances, do you really think you can easily defeat my avatar?"

Lu Yang’s gaze turned icy, looking at the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord as if he were looking at a corpse, "This lord has commanded the winds and clouds for tens of thousands of years, and yet no one has dared to threaten me!"

"Then let’s fight!"

"Fight!"

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord’s avatar fused with Si Chen God Lord, swallowing the outline of the Time Dao Fruit.

The Emperor avatar leapt up, soaring into the sky.

"Swallow the sky and earth!"

Instantly, the color of the heavens and earth changed, the spiritual essence above Western Heaven City was completely devoured. Yet, this was just the beginning; this technique could devour everything, including Western Heaven City below.

"Let’s see how big your appetite is, Buddha Land in Palm!"

A pair of hands holding the sun, moon, and stars appeared, embodying the Way of space within the world, one hand in the heavens and one on the earth, trapping the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord’s avatar in between!

The hand was so large that it surpassed the limit of what the outline of the Devouring Dao Fruit could devour!

Boom!

The hands clapped together like the collision of myriad stars, creating shockwaves so powerful they blew away distant mountains!

In the realm of immortal cultivation, mountains were no different from pebbles!

Seeing the grave situation, Master Mingyu deployed the Immovable Ming King Dharma Aspect to protect everyone.

The more people he needed to protect and the stronger the enemy, the stronger his Immovable Ming King Dharma Aspect became. However, there were limits to its strength; his Dharma Aspect had not yet reached the level capable of stepping into the "Immortal" tier!

"Ten Thousand Swords Returning to Origin!"

"Emperor’s Fist!"

The Emperor avatar unleashed its uniquely supreme Emperor’s Fist, ferocious as thunder, swift as lightning, colliding with the Green Peak Sword. Golden runes filled the sky, and the Sword Qi was dazzling, blinding onlookers from discerning who had the upper hand.

"Devour, go!"

The Emperor avatar created over a dozen Devouring Black Holes, tearing space apart, twisting and warping eerily. A Transcendence Tribulation Cultivator encountering even one black hole would be devoured to the point of leaving nothing behind.

Even though Lu Yang had an Immortal Body, he dared not make rash contact!

"Let’s see how you handle this!"

However, contrary to the Emperor avatar’s expectations, Lu Yang’s aggressive and overwhelming aura did not lessen, showing no fear of the Devouring Black Holes.

The moment a Devouring Black Hole touched Lu Yang, it vanished, leaving him completely unharmed!

"How is this possible?"

Even an Immortal Body formed by incense should have a process where it is devoured by a black hole, and then the incense replenishes the devoured parts!

"But what of that, can you actually hit me?"

Now, with the outline of the Time Dao Fruit, the Emperor Avatar could predict the future, and although Lu Yang’s sword was both fast and ruthless, once the direction of the sword was predicted, it posed no threat!

This was precisely the most troublesome aspect of the time-related Dao Fruit; once the future was foreseen, many attacks became ineffective!

"Not good, it seems the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord has taken the upper hand!" Master Ming Huai shouted anxiously, noticing that several of Lu Yang’s tricky attacks had been dodged by the Emperor Avatar.

"Three heads and six arms!"

"Emperor’s Fist!"

"Primordial Qi!"

"Taiyin Divine Thunder!"

Taking advantage of the victory, the Emperor Avatar, using his three heads and six arms, simultaneously launched three different types of attacks, not believing that Lu Yang could come out unscathed!

Each of these three moves alone were enough to shatter the heavens and reshape the world, let alone when all three were used together— their combined power was unimaginable!

Even Master Ming Yu and others, who had never seen these techniques and were Great Cultivators themselves, felt the heart-palpitating force emitted by the Emperor Avatar and couldn’t help thinking that a great disaster was looming!

The Emperor Avatar laughed wildly, "I’d like to see what moves you have left..."

Before he could finish, he saw Lu Yang sheathe his sword and sigh.

"I really didn’t want to use this move."

The Emperor Avatar did not understand the implication of Lu Yang’s words, and suddenly there was a flash of light before his eyes.

Immortal Self-Detonation?!

The Emperor Avatar’s eyes nearly popped out.

Boom——

A terrifying explosion occurred above Western Paradise Temple, and in an instant, the temple was reduced to ruins.

The Emperor Avatar staggered out from the explosion, covered in wounds; this was the first time he encountered such a suicidal fighting method, but luckily, he was a notch above and survived the self-detonation.

"Ha ha, self-detonation or what, it is still my victory..."

Then he saw Lu Yang also fly out from the explosion, unscathed.

Lu Yang gave the Emperor Avatar a slight smile.

Boom boom boom——

Continuous Immortal Self-Detonation.

The Emperor Avatar did not understand what was happening and suffered continuous explosions, dying instantly.

He might have been lucky to survive one self-detonation, but he couldn’t survive them all.

"Tsk, his body was blown away, what a pity." Lu Yang examined the loot, finding that Si Chen God Lord’s physical body was completely obliterated, leaving only a soul.

"But there’s still a gain." Lu Yang contentedly stored Si Chen God Lord’s soul into his Spiritual Space.

"Speaking of which, self-detonation is quite useful." Lu Yang smacked his lips, the fancy attacks of the Emperor Avatar indeed paled in comparison to self-detonation.

"Did we win?" Master Ming Huai uncertainly said, having been terrified by the sudden and continuous self-detonations and could hardly believe the power at the center of the explosions.

"It seems we did win." Master Ming Yu was also unsure, this battle surpassed his comprehension.

Master Ming Yu flew into the sky bravely, clasped his hands together, and thanked this unknown immortal.

"Thank you, Venerable Immortal, for your assistance."

"I have some connections with your Buddhism, it was just a small effort."

Suddenly, Lu Yang felt a warmth in his chest, and he took out three Seeking Immortal Tokens, finding that the one inscribed with the word "Time" was heating up!

Chapter 1050: 1049 Time Immortal

When he saw the Time Seeking Immortal Order, Lu Yang paused for a moment.

What’s going on, is the Time Immortal nearby?

Lu Yang landed, and the Time Seeking Immortal Order grew hotter and hotter.

He looked around. After experiencing a series of self-detonations, the Western Paradise Temple had turned into ruins, only the Mahavira Hall, though swaying, had not collapsed.

Is there something strange about the Mahavira Hall?

Lu Yang walked straight toward the Mahavira Hall— the rising temperature of the Seeking Immortal Token confirmed his suspicion.

Master Mingyu muttered something bad under his breath and anxiously followed behind Lu Yang, "Venerable Immortal, Venerable Immortal, that place must not be approached."

Lu Yang turned a deaf ear, ignoring Master Mingyu’s persuasion, and walked to the entrance of the Mahavira Hall. A voice came from inside the hall.

"Mingyu, this man is an old friend of mine. I shall catch up with him; you go and take care of the aftermath."

"Yes." Hearing that voice, Mingyu’s attitude did a one-eighty: he no longer stopped Lu Yang and turned to leave.

Indeed, after the battle had just taken place, people were panicked—it really did require his presence to stabilize the people’s hearts.

Lu Yang’s heart beat sped up slightly as he heard the voice coming from inside the Mahavira Hall.

It was the voice of the Time Immortal; the Time Immortal was right here.

But if the Time Immortal was in the Mahavira Hall, why hadn’t Fairy Eternity discovered it before?

Lu Yang placed the Green Peak Sword into his Identity Jade and entered the Mahavira Hall.

Stepping inside, a fresh fragrance hit him—it was a blend of sandalwood and lotus scents. The space inside the hall was vast, with a huge Golden Body Buddha statue enshrined in the center. The statue was tens of meters tall, with a benevolent face and half-closed eyes, as if contemplating the truths of the world.

Around the Buddha statue were exquisite murals depicting the many stories of the Worldly Venerable. Each painting was lifelike and captivating.

The golden Buddha statue opened its eyes, stepped down from the lotus throne, and at the same time, its body shrank to the height of an ordinary person. Although its demeanor was still compassionate, the face had changed, looking to be only in the early twenties.

The golden Buddha statue looked Lu Yang over carefully, fixating on the wooden plaque on Lu Yang’s chest, "You have just used the Initial Form of Eternity Dao Fruit, where did you get it from?"

"Time, you’re still alive?!"

The voice of Immortal Fairy resonated from within Lu Yang’s body, and Immortal Fairy excitedly emerged.

The Time Immortal, seeing Immortal Fairy, was also extremely excited, "Immortal, you have really resurrected?"

"Yes, it was Little Yang... Lu Yang who resurrected me!"

Little Yang? The Time Immortal paused for a moment, what sort of strange appellation was this?

Then the Time Immortal looked solemnly at Lu Yang and expressed his heartfelt gratitude with two words, "Thank you."

"No, it’s nothing, just a coincidence."

Although every first meeting with Jiang Lianyi, Ao Ling, and Yingtian Immortal resulted in heavy thanks when they learned he had resurrected the Immortal Fairy, such treatment still made Lu Yang slightly uncomfortable.

"A mere coincidence is quite enough." They had strived for a hundred thousand years to achieve what Lu Yang had, and whether it was a fluke or not, it remained a tremendous favor.

Time Immortal glanced at the wooden plaque hanging from Lu Yang’s chest, and recalling the past, he sighed internally. Initially, through the time channel, he saw a part of himself on Lu Yang and speculated whether his future self would meet with disaster.

He was extremely cautious, yet his predictions still came to pass—he truly had met with disaster.

"By the way, Time, how are you still alive?"

The Time Immortal looked strangely at them. Was this a question one should ask upon first meeting?

However, considering Immortal Eternity’s personality, asking such a direct question did seem reasonable.

"How much do you know about the Fire Dynasty?"

"The Fire Dynasty? We’ve met Lianyi, Xiao Ling, and Ying Tian. We know quite a bit about the affairs of the Fire Dynasty now."

"You’ve met Ying Tian? Where is he now?"

"He lives inside an Ancient Ring Small World. The Ancient Ring is in the hands of a disciple from the Laurel Fairy Palace."

"I see. When I obtained the Sky Opening Axe, I thought something had happened to him. It’s good that he’s alive."

"Since you’ve met Ying Tian, then you probably know almost as much as I do about the affairs of the Fire Dynasty. He knows as much as I do, so there’s not much to talk about."

"I heard from Ying Tian that you were secretly attacked at the end of the Fire Dynasty era. Who did it?"

The Time Immortal shook his head lightly. "I don’t know. But it’s definitely one of the three of them. One among them was possessed by someone pulling the strings from behind the scenes!"

"You can use the Time Dao Fruit for time retrospection. How could someone secretly attack you?"

Like a scene from thirty thousand years ago, the Time Immortal slowly exhaled, his expression grave. "My Dao Fruit was tampered with, preventing me from performing time retrospection. I suspect that the hidden manipulator’s Dao Fruit is also related to time."

Immortal Fairy Eternity puzzled, "Also related to time? How is that possible? You possess a time-related Dao Fruit. How could they have one too?"

"That is unknown. It’s just a hunch. Perhaps other Dao Fruits could also have an effect on time retrospection."

"What happened afterward?"

"After the sneak attack, I was severely wounded. The Firmament Tree body was beyond recovery, so I had no choice but to abandon it. My soul performed astral projection, leaping thousands of years into the Great Qian Period. However, I didn’t stay in the Great Qian but chose to go to the Western regions instead."

The Nian Beast can leap through time, and naturally, the Time Immortal could as well.

"Without a physical body and only my soul remaining, if I encountered Silent Extermination Immortal or the hidden manipulator, I wouldn’t have the strength to fight back."

"To construct a true form, I established the Buddha Country in the Western regions and hid inside this Buddha statue in the Mahavira Hall of Western Paradise Temple. Only the abbots of successive generations knew of my existence."

"This Mahavira Hall was constructed using the Power of Incense and Fire, creating a Small World inside, capable of evading the perception of Silent Extermination Immortal."

The Mahavira Hall being brimming with incense is not unusual and wouldn’t raise suspicion.

"In the Buddha Country, I promoted that Buddhist cultivators should transform alms into building a Golden Body for the Buddha. Gradually, this became one of the methods for Buddhist cultivation."

"The act of Buddhist cultivators building a Golden Body for the Buddha would generate the Power of Incense and Fire. As the Buddha, I absorbed part of this incense power."

"At one point, I incidentally found the Sky Opening Axe belonging to Yingtian Immortal, which I used as the treasure of the Western Paradise Temple to conceal my existence."

"Whenever the Western Paradise Temple faced danger, I would predict the future and advise them to use the Sky Opening Axe at the most opportune time to overcome crises."

"I’ve observed that this Lu Yang’s Immortal’s Body is formed by the Power of Incense and Fire, but it cannot be maintained for long and cannot leave the Buddha Country."

"Such a body can’t withstand intense combat." Saying this, the Time Immortal paused for a moment, looking at Lu Yang with evident envy.

"Of course, that is assuming Lu Yang doesn’t possess the Initial Form of the Immortal Dao Fruit."

"I’ve been accumulating strength to shape a permanent Immortal Body."

"The accumulation of incense power is a lengthy process, and the waiting is rather dull. So, I randomly performed time leaps. It could be a few years, a dozen, or dozens of years. At each time node I leaped to, I had the abbot of Western Paradise Temple tell me about the events that had occurred over those years."

"Until just now, during a time leap, I sensed Master Ming Yu calling out to me, saying that the Western Paradise Temple was in grave danger. I also sensed fluctuations from the Time Dao Fruit Outline and the Initial Form of the Immortal Dao Fruit, which prompted me to jump to the current time node."
